Catalog number : M00279
Uniform title: dīkṣottara
Main title: dīkṣottara of the nisvāsakārika
Manuscript : IFP Transcript T0017B from page 795 to end
Description:
IFP Transcript T0017B from folio 795 to the end.

The Dīkṣottara of the Nisvāsakārika which is the fifth section of the Niśvāsatattva saṃjitā. An important early Agama of the śaivasiddhānta .
Notes:
Revision 0: December 16, 2013
Publication country : India
########################################################################### 



p. 795)

niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ tatvaṃ tṛṭigātraṃ prakīrtitam |
kathaṃ vijñāyate śaṃbho etadicchāmi veditum || 1 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ yattvayā paripṛcchritam |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi hitāya tava suvṛte || 1 ā ||

bhuvanādhvā kalādhvā ca tatvādhvā ca varānane |
varṇamantra padādhvā ca kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ || 2 ||

prāṇaike saṃsthitā devi veditavyaṃ hi deśikaiḥ |
bhuvanādhvā tu yatprokto mayā pūrvaṃ hi suvrate || 3 ||

ṣaḍviṃśattatvairākṣiptaṃ veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |

p. 796)

punaścāṣṭādaśaistatvaiḥ kalābhedaiḥ punasthitam || 4 ||

punaśca navabhistatvaiḥ kathitaṃ surasundari |
tatsarvañcāṣṭabhirbhedairākṣiptaṃ punareva tu || 5 ||

ekeṣu **? lkaṃ tatvaṃ tatvayuktaṃ samudvahet |
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulañcāraṃ yanmayā kathitaṃ tava || 6 ||

hṛtpadmādyavadhiṃ devi veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
āpādatalamadhvānamutsaheta varānane || 7 ||

bhuvanādhvā sa vijñeyo deśikena mahātmanā |
tattvādhvā ca varārohe teṣāñcordhvaṃ vyavasthitaḥ || 8 ||

kalikāliktamātmānamucchvasantaṃ bahisthitaḥ |

p. 797)

kalādhvā tu sa eveti kathitastatra pūrvaśaḥ || 9 ||

evamanyo'pi deveśi veditavyaḥ prayatnataḥ | 
ṣaṭtṛṃśāṅgulaṃ parityajya kramavacca sumadhyame || 10 ||

tṛṭimātraṃ sa vijñeyo dehamuktastu niṣkalaḥ |
svāmibhāvaṃ tu yattasmin dvādaśāntamavasthitaḥ || 11 ||

saṃyuktaśaktinā buddhaṃ tṛṭimātrastu niṣkalaḥ |
śaktyā dehasthito hyātmā niṣkalaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 12 ||

tatvastho vyāpayeddevi dehānmuktastu niṣkalaḥ |
susamiddhe yathā vahnau śikhālihati cāṃbare || 13 ||

dehasya tadvarārohe līlāyati tatpade |

p. 798)

dīkṣākāle'bhimānantu tadvat kuryāttu deśikaḥ || 14 ||

ahameva paraṃ tatvaṃ tatvalīnastathaiva ca |
śaktyāraṇibhūtāyā punastatra kṣipāmyaham || 15 ||

evaṃ yo vetti tatvena vahnivaddehamadhyataḥ |
atītaśśiva tadvahneḥ sa tu yojayate pare || 16 ||

śaktyā tāvatsa yujyeta yāvattatvanna vindati |
udite tu pare tatve tatrastho'pi na vāryate || 17 ||

bandhamuktaḥ pareśānte pare līyanti śāśvate |
līnassarvagato devi dharmādharma vivarjitaḥ || 18 ||

hṛtpadmādyāvattatpadmaṃ pṛthak saṃhāralakṣitam |

p. 799)

yāvadvaktramanuprāptaṃ dvibhāgaṃ tyajate tu saḥ || 19 ||

tṛtīyaṃ tyajate svalpo tryaṃśairmuktastu niṣkalaḥ |
atītaṃ dvādaśānte tu śaktipadmaṃ prakīrtitam || 20 ||

tatrasthaṃ tṛṭimātrantu kālaṃ tasya prakīrtitam |
niṣkalaśśaktipadme tu prabuddhastatra tiṣṭhati || 21 ||

atītassuprabuddhastu unmanatvaṃ yadā priye |
na kālaṃ na kalācāraṃ na tatvā na ca devatā || 22 ||

sunirvāṇaṃ paraṃ śuddhaṃ guruvaktre pratiṣṭhitam |
taṃ prāpya na nivartante bhayasaṃsārabandhane || 23 ||

na tasya cāmarākhyātaṃ niṣkalāvasthitasya tu |

p. 800)

etatte niṣkalaṃ tatvamuktameva samāsataḥ || 24 ||

caturvidhaṃ mayā devi kathitaṃ tatvamuttamam |
trividhaṃ tu parityajya śaktyantaṃ yastṛṭi smṛtaḥ || 25 ||

tṛṭau paṭau tu sthitvā vai ṣoḍaśāṃśe vyavasthitaḥ |
sa bhoktā sarvatatvānāṃ śaktyante yastṛṭismṛtaḥ || 26 ||

sa bhoktā sarvatatvānāṃ śaktinā sahasaṃyutaḥ |
śaktyatīto varārohe tatvena saha yujyate || 27 ||

catuḥ padmaṃ bhavetpadmaṃ vyāpiṇī karṇikāsthitā |
tatra śakticatuṣkantu sūkṣmākhyaṃ parikīrtitam || 28 ||

śrutvā budhvā vimucyante nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |

p. 801)

sarvatantreṣu sāmānyaṃ tatrasthaṃ viṣuvaṃ smṛtam || 29 ||

prāṇasya tallayaṃ devi tatrastho'pi nivartate |
sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva mayanañca tṛtīyakam || 30 ||

praśāntaṃ prāpya deveśi niṣkalaṃ viṣuvaṃ smṛtam |
tribhāgabhājite cāre niṣkalaḥ praṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ || 31 ||

praśāntastu smṛto binduḥ niṣkalaḥ praṇavasya tu |
sa cetasya smṛto jīvo yadā śaktyā tu yujyate || 32 ||

anyathā niṣkalohyātmā suṣuptāviva lakṣyate |
cinmātrantu smṛto hutvā cetā śaktiḥ prakīrtitā || 33 ||

śrutvā budhvā vimucyante nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |

p. 802)

sarvatantreṣu sāmānyaṃ tantrasthe viṣuvaṃ vadet || 34 ||

prāṇasya tallayaṃ devi tatrasthena nivartate |
tathā yuktamidaṃ sarvaṃ nānāceṣṭāni kārayet || 35 ||

tatsthāne līyate jñānī jñe * * *? paraṃ layam |
kalpanārahitaṃ jñāṃ sarvatatvavivarjitam || 36 ||

vyāpakaṃ sarvatatvānāṃ pāraṃparyeṇa saṃsthitam |
etatte niṣkalañcāraṃ kathitantu mayākhilam || 37 ||

na deyañca tvayā devi anyāyapathavartine |
kapaṭenopapannāya śravasyapi na dāpayet || 38 ||

vyāpinī vyomarūpā ca anantā nāyakā smṛtā |

p. 803)

mūrtidevasya vikhyātā śaktyā devabhuvasya tu || 39 ||

pañcavyomasmṛtāhyete śūnyāvasthā tu devatā |
brahmādīnāṃ varārohe śūnyadehaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ || 40 ||

etatte devatāyogaṃ prathamaṃ parikīrtitam |
śaktyavasthāntarī bhūtāssūkṣmādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 41 ||

tatsthacetātmakā devāśśabdarūpāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
tṛtīyā tu varārohe jñātavyā deśikena tu || 42 ||

dvividhasthāntare devi jyotīrūpaṃ vicintayet |
ta evamanurūpantu pañcamāṃ kathitaṃ tava || 43 ||

īśavaktre tathā ghore vāmassadyastathaiva ca |

p. 804)

trividhaṃ mantradehantu veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 44 ||

brahmāviṣṇustathā rudro īśvarastu sadāśivaḥ |
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca || 45 ||

śūnyasparśastathā śabdo jyotīrūpaṃ tathaiva ca |
mantrarūpaṃ tathaiveha kathitaṃ tatvato mayā || 46 ||

sthūlātsthūlatarairbhāvaiḥ sūkṣmātsūkṣmataraiḥ punaḥ |
pañcabrahmāṇi deveśi veditavyāni yatnataḥ || 47 ||

śūnyasparśastathā śabdo jyorūpaṃ tathaiva ca |
tatvarūpaṃ tathaiveha pañcabrahmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 48 ||

evaṃ jñātvā ca budhvā ca mucyate pāśabandhanāt |

p. 805)

vrajate śivasāyujyaṃ śivena sahamodate || 49 ||

ṣaṭpadārthamidaṃ devi mantrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ tava |
veditavyaṃ prayatnena siddhimokṣārthakāṅkṣibhiḥ || 50 ||

evaṃ sarveṣu mantreṣu kathitaṃ tava śobhane |

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
deva uvāca- 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ hitāvaham |
yena vijñātamātreṇa vidhimuktaṃ labheddhṛvam || 1 ||

ṣaḍvidhastu śivo jñeyo yoginā sumahātmanā |

p. 806)

śabdavyomatayā jyotiḥ vigraho bhuvanastathā || 2 ||

mantramūrtistu paramaḥ ṣaḍvidhastu śivo'vyayaḥ |
mantramūrtiṃ viditvā tu mantrasiddhimavāpnuyāt || 3 ||

bhuvaneśatvamāpnoti bhuvanāsaktamānasaḥ |
brahmādīnāṃ surendrāṇāṃ sādhane vigrahaṃ smaret || 4 ||

yogināmadhipatyarthaṃ jyotistho yogamabhyaset |
śabdatatve pare līnaḥ śabdāntargatamānasaḥ || 5 ||

paramemākṣamāpnoti śabdātsarvārthasiddhaye |
tasyātītaṃ paraṃ devi śaktivyomaprakīrtitam || 6 ||

tasya dhyānādbhavetsiddhiḥ muktiścaiva na saṃśayaḥ |

p. 807)

vigraho bhuvano jyotiḥ śabdacchāmantathaiva ca || 7 ||

ete paraprakāśantu śivadhyānaparaśśivaḥ |
śivamevaṃ vidhaṃ jñātvā sādhakastu samāhitaḥ || 8 ||

prāpnoti vividhāṃ siddhiṃ muktiñcaiva na saṃśayaḥ |
mantramūrtiparaṃ jñātvā siddhiṃ muktimāpnuyāt || 9 ||

jape homārcanādhyānāt siddhayaśca yathārthataḥ |
bhuvanantu bhuvanākāraṃ brahmāṇḍādi yathākramam || 10 ||

nānāratnamayaṃ divyamanaupamyaguṇānvitam |
indranīlamahānīlaiḥ rājataiśca vibhūṣitam || 11 ||

padmarāgamayastaṃbhaiḥ nānāratnopaśobhitam |

p. 808)

divyasṛggandhavastrāḍhyaṃ divyaiśvaryasamanvitam || 12 ||

bhuvanaṃ devadevasya jñātvā tallayamāpnuyāt |
vigrahaṃ vigrahākāraṃ śivavarṇādilakṣaṇam || 13 ||

śaśāṅkārdhadhanaṃ tryakṣaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
śūnyahastaṃ vṛṣārūḍhamumādehārdhasaṃyutam || 14 ||

śaṅkhakundendudhavalaṃ sahasrādityavarcasam |
vigrahaṃ devadevasya sarvakāmārthasādhanam || 15 ||

sādhanaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ bhūmyarthe ca smaretsadā |
jyotidhyānātsadāyogī yogasiddhimavāpnuyāt || 16 ||

aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ yogī krīḍādilakṣaṇam |

p.809)

vyālākāramanākāraṃ vyomavyāpī paraśśivaḥ || 17 ||

sarvavyāpinamīśānaṃ cintayā śivamāpnuyāt |
nirguṇaṃ sāñjanātītaṃ gṛhanakṣatravarjitam || 18 ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ paraṃ vyoma vicintayet |
vyomalīnamanāḥ samyak vyomastho vyomatanmayaḥ || 19 ||

paraṃ mokṣamavāpnoti yatra śabde layaṃ gataḥ |
śabdadhyānāt paromokṣaḥ siddhayaśca na saṃśayaḥ || 20 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena śabdadhyānaṃ samāśrayet |
vāmapārśve iḍā tasya ciñciṇīśabdamuttamam || 21 ||

prākṛtaṃ nāma caitaddhi sthūlaśca parikīrtitaḥ |

p. 810)

dakṣiṇena suṣumnā hi ciravyakrinī smṛtā || 22 ||

pauruṣannāma cetyukta sūkṣmasya parikīrtitam |
ghaṇṭānādavirāmāntāntyamadhyaparaṃ smṛtam || 23 ||

nirālaṃbo nirādhāraḥ tadeva paramaṃ padam |
ciñciṇīcāravakyante nirālaṃbo nirāśrayaḥ || 24 ||

ghaṇḍānādavirāmānte śivo'haṃ yā nirāśrayaḥ |
hṛdisthaṃ mantrasaṅghātaṃ kṛtvā mūrtinyasedbudhaḥ || 25 ||

brahmādibhiḥ kalaistatvaiḥ sarvasiddhikarasmṛtaḥ |
sa tu cintayate nityaṃ tatrasthaṃ bindumīśvaram || 26 ||

citrastha īśvaro nādaḥ sa tu sākṣācchivasmṛtaḥ |

p. 811)

haṃsākhyaścaiva binduśca nādo nādānta eva ca || 27 ||

evaṃ jñātvā bhavenmantrī śivaḥ sākṣādalepakaḥ |
etattatparamaṃ guhyametattatparamaṃ śivam || 28 ||

etattatparamaṃ guhyametattatparamaṃ padam |
etattatparamaṃ jñānametattatparamākṣaram || 29 ||

nātaḥ parataro dhyeyo nātaḥ parataraṃ śivam |
vidite saṃyagevaṃ hi mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 30 ||

sādhakasya vidhihyeṣā sarvakāmārthasiddhidā |
eteṣāṃ parato devaḥ śabdarūpavivarjitaḥ || 31 ||

vyāpakassarvatatvānāṃ mayā pūrvamudāhṛtam |

p. 812)

taṃ viditvā vimucyante sidhyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 32 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ |
devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi śūnyabhedamanuttamam |
kathaṃ vijñāyate śaṃbho ratiguhyataraṃ param || 1 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

viśūnyaṃ sarvatatveṣu gīyate surasundari |
tasyāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi lakṣaṇantu surārcite || 2 ||

vyāpakena samāyuktamayanaṃ tatprakīrtitam |

p. 813)

divārātryośca madhye tu veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 3 ||

na rātrirna divā caiva na nindukalakalā tathā |
pañcadhā tu parityajya praśāntaṃ śūnyamucyate || 4 ||

dehātītaṃ tṛtīyantu niṣkaleti vibhāvyate |
nāsāyāntu yadātītaṃ tacchūnyañca tṛtīyakam || 15 ||

ghaṭikāntu paricchedaṃ yāvattanmukhamaṇḍalam |
ayanaṃ śūnyamityuktaṃ kathitaṃ jñānamuttamam || 6 ||

rasānāṃ śūnyatasthāpya vijñānamupajāyate |
adhastāttu parityajya pañcadhā parameśvari || 7 ||

praśāntaṃ śūnyamityuktamūrdhvaṃ kiñcinna saṃspṛśet |

p. 814)

etatpraśāntaśūnyantu jñātvā mucyeta bandhanāt || 8 ||

gamāgamamayogena tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam |
viditaṃ tadvarārohe dehamuktamidaṃ smṛtam || 9 ||

tadevaṃ kathitaṃ devi śūnyamuktaṃ śivāgame |
ūrdhvaśūnyamadhaśśūnyaṃ madhyaśśūnyaṃ tṛtīyakam || 10 ||

nirāmayaṃ caturthantu kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ |
sā mayā kathitā hyete caturthantu nirāmayam || 11 ||

sā māyā api deveśi tatvāvasthena saṃsthitā |
tena doṣāya vartante śeṣaṃ mahānulakṣaṇam || 12 ||

caturthaṃ yanmahacchūnyaṃ vijñaptiḥ sā tu kīrtitā |

p. 815)

na tasya kalpanā devi pāraṃparyeṇa saṃsthitaḥ || 13 ||

taṃ jñātvā kathyate jñeyaṃ jñānī śeṣeṣu kathyate |
kriyātītantu tattatvaṃ gīyate sarvataśśubhe || 14 ||

tasyāgamī tu saṃprāptiḥ yasya tuṣṭā sa eva ca |
evaṃ triśūnyabhedantu kathitaṃ tava suvṛte || 15 ||

caturthaṃ yanmahacchūnyaṃ vijñaptiḥ sā tu kīrtitā |
na tasya kalpanā devi pāraṃparyeṇa saṃsthitaḥ || 16 ||

taṃ jñātvā kathyate jñeyaṃ jñānī śeṣeṣu kathyate |
kriyātītantu tattatvaṃ gīyate sarvataśśubhe || 17 ||

tasyāgamī tu saṃprāptiḥ yasya tuṣṭā sa eva ca |

p. 816)

evaṃ triśūnyabhedantu kathitaṃ tava suvrate || 18 ||

caturthaṃ yanmahacchūnyaṃ sarvaṃ tacca prabodhakam |
tena jñātena saṃjñeyī dīkṣānugrahakṛt bhavet || 19 ||

anyathā na gururjñeyo na dīkṣā na ca muktidā |
pañcavyomāḥ purā proktāḥ te tvayā viditāḥ śubhe || 20 ||

nā jñātena vimucyanti sve sve vividhagocare |
brahmāṇamāditaḥ kṛtvā yāvaddevaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 21 ||

pañcavyomāḥ samākhyātāḥ saṃhāreṇa varānane |
ekaikaṃ pañcadhā devi veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 22 ||

śūnyabhedaṃ paraṃ teṣāṃ uktāvasthā tu kīrtitā |

p. 817)

svabhāvavarṇato devi jñeyaṃ vijñāyate yathā || 23 ||

tathā te kathayiṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
aṇavastrayamākhyātaṃ hṛtkarṇaṃ tālumāśritam || 24 ||

nimnavattadvarārohe varṇo bhavati nityaśaḥ |
brahmāviṣṇastathā rudra abhāvaḥ sūtravatsmṛtāḥ || 25 ||

caturthastvīśvaraśśānto veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
tacchabdo nāda ityuktaḥ sa jīvaḥ kathitastava || 26 ||

sparśastvasmiṃstu deveśi sā śaktiḥ parikīrtitā |
tatkṣayena na kiñcit syāt guruvaktraṃ saducyate || 27 ||

saṃyogamevamevaṃ syāt vyākhyātaṃ tava śobhane |

p. 818)

mantrāstathaiva jñātavyā deśikena mahātmanā || 28 ||

nādaṃ binduṃ tathā devaṃ vāṅmayaṃ sa carācaram |
śrūyate ucyate caiva śivaśaktiviceṣṭitam || 29 ||

evaṃ jñātvā ca budhvā ca mantrāstasya vaśo bhavet |
sidhyate nātra sandehaḥ śivaśaktyupabṛṃhitaḥ || 30 ||

ajñātvā tu varārohe śivaśaktiviceṣṭitam |
mantrāstasya na sidhyanti na ca yāti paraṃ padam || 31 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ |
devyuvāca - 

p. 819)

sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedena deva deva tvayā purā |
kathitaṃ tatra sadbhāvaṃ tanmayā viditaṃ prabho || 1 ||

punaśca śrotumicchāmi bhagavan vaktumarhasi |
ke ca kasmin gṛhādeva dīkṣitā devadānavāḥ || 2 ||

ṛṣividyādharāssiddhā gandharvāpsarasaḥ prabho |
yakṣarākṣasabhūtāśca piśācoragakinnarā || 3 ||

guhyakāśca mahādeva krūrāścaiva vināyakāḥ |
svāntena dīkṣitādeva yasya siddhi dhruvaṃ bhavet || 4 ||

tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi bhagavan kathayasva me |
koṭibhedāḥ samākhyātāḥ śākhābhedāśca śaṅkara || 5 ||

p. 820)

śivabhedañca deveśa sarvametadbravīhi me |

īśvaraḥ - 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi tantrabhedamanuttamam || 6 ||

mantramaṇḍalasaṃkhyāñca śāstrāṇāñcaiva saṅgraham |
dīkṣitāni mayā yasmin na śaṅkāśca yathā purā || 7 ||

sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ śaivānāṃ mantramūrtinā |
prakāśitaṃ purā devi mamedaṃ parameṣṭhinā || 8 ||

sarvajagataśceha sṛṣṭā ca parameśvaraḥ |
saṃhartā ca na sandehaḥ mantramūrtiḥ paraśśivaḥ || 9 ||

avatāramahaṃ vakṣye nāmasaṅgrahanirṇayam |

p. 821)

dīkṣāvidhiniyogañca kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ yathāśrutam || 10 ||

mantrayogaprabhedañca pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ |
tasyādirbhagavān devaśśivo vai mantramūrtimān || 11 ||

mantratantrapraṇetā ca mantratantraprakāśakaḥ || 11 1/2 ||

tena prakāśitaṃ pūrvaṃ śivenānandamūrtimān || 12 ||

tadeva vacanaṃ jñānaṃ mama vaktre niveśitam |
mayāpi tatvadeveśi tvayā dattaṃ pitāmahe || 13 ||

brahmaṇāpi surādhyakṣe viṣṇave pratipāditam |
viṣṇunāpi hutāśāya tena dattantu vāyave || 14 ||

vāyunāpi purādattaṃ sūryāya ca na saṃśayaḥ |

p. 822)

sūryeṇa cāpi somāya kathitaṃ punareva tu || 15 ||

somenaiva tu deveśi dharmāya pratipāditam |
evaṃ taddaśadhā jñānaṃ śivoktaṃ tatra saṅgrahe || 16 ||

khyāpitaṃ tatvamīśena mantramūrtiniveśitam |
sa eva jagataścāsya mantramūrtirmaheśvaraḥ || 17 ||

tatprasādādidaṃ sarvaṃ jñānavijñānamuttamam |
prāpyate devadeveśi mokṣañca bhuvi durlabham || 18 ||

sārvakāmikamityevaṃ provāca bhagavāñcchivaḥ |
lokodbhaveti rudreṇa punareva prakīrtitam || 19 ||

devyāyaṇaṃ yadā jñānamajiteti prakīrtitam |

p. 823)

kāraṇaṃ bhagavān brahmā ajiteti janārdanaḥ || 20 ||

dīptābheti yathāgniśca vāyuḥ sūkṣmeti vai punaḥ |
sahasrāṃśeti dharmāya aṃśumāniti bhāskaraḥ || 21 ||

suvratāḥ candramāḥ prokto daśatantraprakāśakaḥ |
śivaśaktimadhiṣṭhāya vibhinnaṃ vai punasthitaḥ || 22 ||

daśadhā nāmabhedena lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
evameti surāssarve śivajñānasya pāragāḥ || 23 ||

avatāraṃ punaścaiva bahudhā parikīrtitam |
asyaiva jñānamukhyasya śivajñānasya sāṃpṛtam || 24 ||

bhedamaṣṭataraṃ proktaṃ  tathā'ṣṭādaśadhā punaḥ |

p. 824)

aṣṭādaśavidhairbhedaiḥ tatra bhedaiḥ punasthitaḥ || 25 ||

ājagāma suvarlokabhuvarlokakramāgataḥ |
tasya bhedā bhavantyete saṃjñā ca parikīrtyate || 26 ||

ṛṣibhistatra kalpajñaiḥ viditaṃ tatra muttamam |
vijayaṃ prathamaṃ teṣāṃ niśvāsaṃ tadanantaram || 27 ||

svāyaṃbhuvamataścaiva vātulaṃ tadanantaram |
vīrabhadramiti khyātaṃ rauravaṃ mākuṭaṃ tathā || 28 ||
ṁṁṁṁṁṁṁṁ
vimalaṃ candrabhāgañca jñānañca mukhavālakam |
prodgītaṃ lalitañcaiva siddhisantānameva ca || 29 ||

śarvodgītañca vijñeyaṃ kiraṇaṃ pārameśvaram |

p. 826)

īśvaraḥ prathamaḥ prokto devī ca tadanantaram || 30 ||

brahmā ca ṣaṇmukhaścaiva vīrabhadrastathaiva ca |
nandīśvaro mahākāla īśānaṃ candramāstathā || 31 ||

bṛhaspatiḥ samākhyāto devī vīkā ca vāsavaḥ |
lalitā caiva caṇḍaśca śāntapāṇī tathaiva ca || 32 ||

sanatkumāro viṣṇuśca pārameśvarameva ca || 32 1/2 ||

vijayamīśvarajñānaṃ devyā niśvāsasaṃhitām || 33 ||

brahmā svāyaṃbhuvaścaiva kumāro jñeyavātule |
vīreṇa vīrabhadrastu nandīrauravameva ca || 34 ||

makuṭeti mahākālaṃ visannaramuśanasā |

p. 827)

candramāścandrabhāsañca jñānañcāṅgirasaṃ tathā || 35 ||

dadhīcī mukhabhinnantu prodgītaṃ kavacameva tu |
lalitaṃ lalitā devi daṇḍahastaṃ tathaiva ca || 36 ||

santānaṃ nāśapāṇiḥ syāt śarvodgītañca viṣṇunā |
kiraṇaṃ brahmaṇā caiva parameśe pārameśvaram || 37 ||

ete tantrāḥ pūrvaṃ śivajñānasya pāragāḥ |
te cāpyante mahābhāgāḥ kramārādyāśśacīpatiḥ || 38 ||

mantrasiddhāstathā cānye ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ |
mantrabheda praṇetāro mantrakalpakarāstathā || 39 ||

ebhireva śivajñānaṃ sthāpitaṃ vasudhātale |

p. 828)

nāmabhedaprasaṃkhyātamārādhya parameśvaram || 40 ||

mantrakalpe tathā khyātaṃ bhūtakalpe tathaiva ca |
gaṇaguhyakabhedaiśca rudrabhedaiśca saṃjñitam || 41 ||

tasya bhedāḥ smṛtāḥ pūrvaṃ daśadhā parikīrtitāḥ |
punaścaiva samākhyātā naṣṭā daśavidhāstathā || 42 ||

vijaye bhagavān rudraḥ provāca brahmaṇe purā |
tasmin mantrāḥ samākhyātāḥ śrūyatāṃ varavarṇinī || 43 ||

aṣṭau mantrasahasrāṇi tathā pañcaśatāni ca |
ekapañcāśatañcaiva tathā vai saptasaptatiḥ || 44 ||

devairjñātamidaṃ devi kiñcittasmādihāgatam |

p. 829)

vijaye tu samākhyātaṃ tasmin brahmā tu dīkṣitaḥ || 45 ||

trīṇi mantrasahasrāṇi niyutaṃ trīṇi cāpare |
aṣṭau caiva tu lakṣāṇi daśabhirmaṇḍalaisthitam || 46 ||

niśvāse tu samākhyātaṃ nandīśo yatra dīkṣitaḥ |
śukraḥ kubero varuṇo yamaścaivāśvināvapi || 47 ||

gaṇāścaiva mahābhāgā rudrāścaiva tu dīkṣitāḥ |
niśvāse tu pare tantre kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 48 ||

sahasramekaṃ vijñeyaṃ niyatā ṣaṣṭisaṅgraham |
śatamekaṃ tathā cānyaṃ mantrāṇāṃ parisaṃkhyayā || 49 ||

ekādaśavidhairbhedaiḥ tathā saptavidhaiḥ punaḥ |

p. 830)

saptavidyādharaṃ tantraṃ tathā svāyaṃbhuveti ca || 50 ||

nāmadvayaṃ samākhyātaṃ dakṣādyā yatra dīkṣitāḥ |
prajānāṃ patayaścaiva tathā brahmasutāśca ye || 51 ||

dīkṣitāśca niyuktāśca asurāścaiva santatam |
yakṣarākṣasagandharvāḥ piśācoragakinnarāḥ || 52 ||

garuḍādyo dvijāścaiva ye ca lokeṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
sahasramekaṃ mantrāṇāṃ triṃśadbhirmaṇḍalaiḥ sthitam || 53 ||

pañcadhā bhedabhinnantu āgneyo yatra dīkṣitāḥ |
gātarodhe gṛhāścaiva ye ca vighnādayo gaṇāḥ || 54 ||

āgneyamiti tantre'smin tejassaṃbhavase bhavam |

p. 831)

ekādaśa sahasrāṇi koṭitrīṇi ca saṃgrahe || 55 ||

maṇḍalānāṃ śatañcaiva aṣṭadhā varaṇe sthitam |
aṣṭādaśa vidhairbhedaiḥ loke mantragaṇān bahūn || 56 ||

bahudhā parisaṃkhyātaṃ pañcagarbhapurassaram |
visarpitañca saṃbodhaiḥ kūṭamudbhūtakāraṇaiḥ || 57 ||

atiśayānvitaścaiva gohyaguhyakasaṃbhavaiḥ |
atra te ṛṣayaṃssiddhāḥ balakhilyāstapodhanāḥ || 58 ||

ayane dakṣiṇe devā ye ca vai uttare sthitāḥ |
anena caiva siddhānte devā vai mānavādayaḥ || 59 ||

tatsādhyastantrametattu vīrabhadre'trasaṃjñitam |

p. 832)

koṭirekaṃ sahasrañca mantramaṇḍalaviṃśakam || 60 ||

dvividhaṃ nāmasaṃjñābhiḥ rauraveti ca viśrutam |
pañcamantraśatā jñeyāḥ caturmūrtivyavasthitāḥ || 61 ||

makuṭeti samākhyāto nidhanakṣetravādibhiḥ |
nalāntarmātṛkalpaiśca ṛṣibhiḥ sevyate sadā || 62 ||

śatamekantu vijñeyaṃ bhinnaścāpi tathā vidhāḥ |
maṇḍalāṣṭakasaṃyuktaṃ visarannāmanāmataḥ || 63 ||

daśamantrasahasrāṇi daśabhirmaṇḍalairyutaḥ |
daśabhedāḥ samākhyātāḥ candrabhāsamiti smṛtam || 64 ||

śatārdhamekamantrāṇāṃ ekavīrapratiṣṭitam |

p. 833)

ekamaṇḍalagūḍhantu nārasihmeti viśrutam || 65 ||

yatrete dīkṣitā daityāḥ nalino devakaṇṭakāḥ |
mayādyā nalinaścaiva nalinādyāśca yatra vai || 66 ||

rasakṣetrakriyāpādā vijñeyā bahubhedataḥ |
mantrairdvādaśabhiryuktaṃ maṇḍaladvayasaṃyutam || 67 ||

dvidhāvasthena deveśi mukhabiṃbamiti smṛtam |
dvisahasre śate dve ca maṇḍāśca trisaptatiḥ || 68 ||

saptaśākhāprabhedaśca prodgītannāma nāmataḥ |
yogināṃ paramo yatra mantrabhedaḥ pravartate || 69 ||

ekavīradharairmantraiḥ japakarmādisiddhaye |

p. 834)

trayastriṃśatsamāyuktaṃ vidyāśaktiviceṣṭitam || 70 ||

mahāmahādipramukhaiḥ vidyābhiśca susaṃskṛtaiḥ |
mahāvratavidhānañca dīkṣāvidhisamudbhavaḥ || 71 ||

kapālottarakalpaiśca siddhikalpaiśca yojitaḥ |
nāḍīsañcārasaṃyuktaṃ lalitaṃ pañcamaṃ calam || 72 ||

ājñāsiddhistu vijñeyā mantrāṇāṃ śatayojitam |
daśadhā maṇḍalākhyātaṃ siddhamityeva saṃjñitam || 73 ||

trīṇi mantrasahasrāṇi viṃśatiścaiva maṇḍalāḥ |
koṭidvayantu mantrāṇāṃ śatānāṃ parikīrtitam || 74 ||

mantrapañcasamāyuktamekamaṇḍalapūjitam |

p. 835)

trimaṇḍalamathājñeyaṃ gūḍhadevaistu kīrtitam || 75 ||

mantrāṇāntu sahasre dve śatāḥ pañcaśatāni ca |
maṇḍalānāṃ śatañcaiva śarvodgītamiti smṛtam || 76 ||

śṛṇu mantrasahasrāṇi maṇḍalāścāpi viṃśatiḥ |
kiraṇe hi samākhyātamādityo yatra dīkṣitaḥ || 77 ||

koṭisaptasamākhyātamādityo yatra dīkṣitaḥ |
koṭisaptasamākhyātaṃ mantrāṇāṃ niyutaṃ tathā || 78 ||

maṇḍalānāṃ sahasrantu saṅgrahaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
saṃkhyātā mantrabhedāśca śākhābhedāśca tatvataḥ || 79 ||

maṇḍalāścāpi mantrāṇāṃ sarvatantrottarāṇi ca |

p. 836)

śivaproktā smṛtāṃhyete tantrakalpabahu smṛtāḥ || 80 ||

anityatā ca bhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭvā lokānimān purā |
*? tatvaṃ yāti saṃjñāśca vidyātatvaṃ śivātmakam || 81 ||

devāsuramanuṣyāśca mantrasiddhāśca ye smṛtāḥ |
gandharva yakṣarakṣāśca piśācā ṛṣayastathā || 82 ||

sarve te mantrasiddhāstu teṣāmāyustu mantrajāḥ |
mantrajāpena siddhāstu sarve te sādhakāḥ smṛtāḥ || 83 ||

tatvaṃ vijñāya muktistu satyametanmaheśvari |
idaṃ sāramidaṃ sāraṃ yassarvaṃ jñātumicchati || 84 ||

api varṣasahasrānte śāstrāntenādhigacchati |

p. 837)

vijñāyākṣaratanmātraṃ gurumārādhya yatnataḥ || 85 ||

prārthayeta mahādeva guruṃ tatvaviśāradam |
tatprasādādbhavenmuktiḥ saṃsārabhavabandhanāt || 86 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare tantrāvatāraḥ caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ |
devī - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi kiñcitkautūhalānvitā |
tatvamācakṣva deveśa yadi syādvarado mama || 1 ||

bindunādaṃ tathā śaktināḍītrayamidaṃ śubham |
yatra tatvañca deveśa utkrāntisiddhisādhanam || 2 ||

p. 838)

ekādaśavidhañcaiva navātme sarvamāśritam |
tena sarvamidaṃ kāryaṃ tena sarvamidaṃ tatam || 3 ||

dṛśyate ca kathaṃ binduḥ katirakṣarayojitaḥ |
kasmin kasmin pradeśe tu draṣṭavyaṃ bindumīśvaram || 4 ||

karaṇa eva kathaṃ tasya yena dṛśyate tatvataḥ |
nādañcaiva tathā śaktiḥ hṛllakṣasya ca darśanam || 5 ||

sarvametadyathā nyāyaṃ kathayasva prasādataḥ |

īśvaraḥ -

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ rahasyamidamuttamam || 6 ||

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ hitāvaham |

p. 839)

navātmakena sarveśi yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu || 7 ||

varṇāṣṭakaṃ dhṛvaṃ devi binduvyāpaka ucyate |
nādaṃ tasya śikhā proktā śaktistu paramaṃ sthitā || 8 ||

ekādaśa padārthantu kāraṇaṃ paramasti ca |
guruvakrācca labhyeta yathā tenaiva coditaḥ || 9 ||

akāra ukāra bhedena makāreṇa tathaiva ca |
yathā karmāṇi kurute kathayāmi tavākhilam || 10 ||

sarvajñānaparaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sarvajñānaṃ sudurlabham |
anādiparamaṃ jñānaṃ anādi paramavyayam || 11 ||

bindunādaṃ tathā śaktiṃ sūkṣmaṃ paramavyayam |

p. 840)

yathā prāpsyasi tannādaṃ binduśca bhuvi durlabham || 12 ||

na mayā kasyacitkhyātaṃ tatte vakṣyāmi suvrate |
bhruvormadhye samākhyātaṃ devadevaṃ maheśvara || 13 ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ sarvatatvaprakāśakam |
varjitaṃ darśatatvaiśca darśatatvaprakāśakam || 14 ||

dṛśyate ca tadā tejo jvalantaṃ svena tejasā |
dīpau tasya samākhyātau ubhau tau śaśi bhāskarau || 15 ||

avasthā sthitā cetākhyātayantau santataḥ |
iḍā dṛśyati vāmena suṣumnā dakṣiṇena tu || 16 ||

tābhyāṃ madhye paraṃ sthānaṃ yatra bindurvyavasthitaḥ |

p. 841)

ghaṭamadhye yathā dīpaḥ tadghaṭaṃ vai prakāśate || 17 ||

upadeśavihīnastu paraṃ tatvaṃ na dṛśyate |
yathā'darśo malairgrastaḥ pratibiṃbaṃ na dṛśyate || 18 ||

tadvadyogavihīnasya pratyakṣaṃ na ca dṛśyate |
ubhayorapi pāṇibhyāṃ dhārasaṃsthāpya buddhimān || 19 ||

upadeśasamāyogāt dṛśyate bindurīśvaraḥ |
nāsā śrotraṃ tathā cakṣuḥ ubhayorapicchādayet || 20 ||

ṣaḍ dvārāṇi ca saṃsthāpya paśyate bindumīśvaram |
chādanāt tyajyate binduḥ śuddhasphaṭikanirmalaḥ || 21 ||

vidhuṃ nirmalaṃ binduṃ sa binduṃ tejasānvitam |

p. 842)

śabdanādavinirmuktaṃ dhyātavyaṃ paramaṃ śivam || 22 ||

bindulīnamanaḥ kṛtvā dhyāyedbindusuyantritaḥ |
yathā sarvagato vāyuṃ pīṭhastho na ca dṛśyate || 23 ||

evaṃ sarvagato binduḥ yogahīno na dṛśyate |
araṇistho yathā vahniḥ vidyamāno na dṛśyate || 24 ||

yogahīnastathā binduḥ na tu dṛśyati sundari |
saṃyogena tathā binduḥ pratyakṣo dṛśyate tadā || 25 ||

sarpiḥ kṣīre yathā'vyaktaṃ vidyamānaṃ na dṛśyate |
tadvat sarvagato binduḥ yogahīno na dṛśyate || 26 ||

yathā sarpi ca agniśca prayogeṇa tu sundari |

p. 843)

prāpyate ca tathā binduḥ yogināñca mahātmanām || 27 ||

toyakānto maṇiryadvat prakṣipto'pi mahāhade |
prakāśayati tattoyaṃ ātmakīḍena tejasā || 28 ||

uddhṛtena maṇiryadvat jalamagnau prakāśake |
tadvadyogavihīnasya binduścaiva na dṛśyate || 29 ||

dhyānātsā jāyate binduḥ saṃyogāddṛśyate punaḥ |
dhyānaṃ yogaṃ samākhyāya paśyate bindumīśvaram || 30 ||

yogināṃ pratyayo binduḥ pratyayātprāpyate param |
paratvādaparaṃ nāsti nityayuktaṃ sadā bhavet || 31 ||

kāraṇena tu saṃprāptiḥ prayogāccaiva sundari |

p. 844)

kathitaṃ sa rahasyantu jñātavyaṃ yogibhissadā || 32 ||

dṛṣṭvā bindurjvalantaṃ tu netra madhye vyavasthitaḥ |
vijñeyā ca sadā devi śikhā tasyaiva madhyataḥ || 33 ||

dṛśyate tatparaṃ sūkṣmaṃ bindukoṭisamaprabham |
tallayassatataṃ yogī lakṣayetsusamāhitaḥ || 34 ||

śṛṇuṣvāvahitā devi nādañca bindudurlabham |
nava tatvāni deveśi yānyuktāni navātmake || 35 ||

teṣāṃ madhye yakārādyāḥ saptabījāḥ samāhitāḥ |
ādihuṅkārasaṃyuktaḥ saptaite bhuvidurlabhāḥ || 36 ||

phaṭkāreṇa samāyuktāḥ sadyodbhramaṇa viśrutāḥ |

p. 845)

sadyassaṅkrāmaṇantyete ekameva niyojitāḥ || 37 ||

ṣaṣṭhañca yatparaṃ bījaṃ vargānte ca vyavasthitaḥ |
hākārarūpasaṃyogāt cintyamānastu sotkramāt || 38 ||

evamanye samākhyātāḥ pañcabījaṃ samāsataḥ |
akārādimakārāntā makārokārayojitāḥ || 39 ||

sadyotkrāmakarāhyete kathitāstava śobhane |
etāssaptasamākhyātāḥ kṣurikāḥ prāṇahārikāḥ || 40 ||

lakṣañjapenna sandehaḥ satyaṃ devi vadāmyaham |
saptānāntu samākhyātamaṣṭa oṅkāra ucyate || 41 ||

huṃphaṭkārasamāyuktāḥ sadya utkrāmayetprabhuḥ |

p. 846)

oṅkāraṃ sarvadevatyaṃ sarvavyāpī paraśśivaḥ || 42 ||

tryakṣarañca tridevatyaṃ trimātrādyadhikaṃ tathā |
tatra cotpadyate śaṃbhuḥ śabdātītastu niṣkalaḥ || 43 ||

parassūkṣmataro devaḥ sarvapāśavimocakaḥ |
nānāmārgasthito vyāpī sarvalokasya durlabhaḥ || 44 ||

trīṇi sthānāni dehe'smin yatra nādo vyavasthitaḥ |
hṛdaye caiva karṇe ca bhruvormadhye tathaiva ca || 45 ||

yatra sūtrākṛtiḥ sūkṣmaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasaprabham |
turyadvāramiti proktaṃ devayānamiti smṛtam || 46 ||

mārgatrayamiti proktaṃ nāḍītrayamiti smṛtam |

p. 847)

devatānāṃ trayañcaiva śaktitrayamiti smṛtam || 47 ||

phalatrayamiti khyātaṃ dehe'smin parameśvari |
sarvagaḥ sarvato vyāpī sarvamāpūryatiṣṭhati || 48 ||

akāraṃ hṛdi madhye tu jāgartītyavasthitaḥ |
padmasya karṇikāmadhye brahmā tatra vyavasthitaḥ || 49 ||

ukāraṃ karṇamāśritya brahmasthānaṃ taducyate |
tālujihvamiti khyātaṃ dvitīyaṃ sthānamucyate || 50 ||

akārannetramādhye tu suṣuptapadamucyate |
lavaṃ kasya tu tatsthānaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 51 ||

ardhamātrapade tasya turyabrahmapade sthitaḥ |

p. 848)

mūrdhni sthānaṃ paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ mokṣamārgapradāyakam || 52 ||

turyātītaṃ paraṃ devi susūkṣmapadamavyayam |
taṃ jñātvā mucyate jantuḥ bhavetsaṃsārabandhanāt || 53 ||

turyātītamiti proktaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati |
evaṃ vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ vāṅmayaṃ sa carācaram || 54 ||

eteṣāṃ nādamadhye tu śivaṃ tatra vyavasthitaḥ |
hṛdayaṃ dehamadhye tu tatra padmaṃ vyavasthitam || 55 ||

karṇikāpadmamadhye tu akāraṃ tasya madhyataḥ |
tasya madhye viniṣkrāntaṃ nādaṃ paramadurlabham || 56 ||

ukārañca makārañca bhitvā nādo vinirgataḥ |

p. 849)

turyadvāraviniṣkrāntaṃ dvayantasya vicintayet || 57 ||

akāre tu samutthāya brahmadvārasamudgatam |
eṣa śabdassamākhyāto mokṣamārgapradāyakam || 58 ||

etaddhyānavaraṃ divyaṃ sevitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
dhyānena tu kimanyena tapasogreṇa vā punaḥ || 59 ||

kiṃ tīrthagamanairvāpi dhyānairvāpi supuṣkalaiḥ |
vidante nādasadbhāve bindudeve ca śobhane || 60 ||

yadyekaṃ viditaṃ nādaṃ sūkṣmamekaṃ parāparam |
athāpi dīpadehastu ākāśe tu yathā śivā || 61 ||

līnā sarvagatā tadvat na dṛśyati cāṃbaram |

p. 850)

evaṃ sūkṣmāti sūkṣmasya nādatatvasya nirṇayaḥ || 62 ||

eṣa nādaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ sarvatantreṣu cottamaḥ |
sūryaraśmisahasrābhā madhye nāḍī prakīrtitā || 63 ||

sahitā sarvatatvaiśca śaktinā saha saṃyutā |
sā nāḍī sarvaśabdānāṃ tatvānāñcaiva śobhane || 64 ||

saṅgṛhya samudāyena śivatatve tu līyate |
sūkṣmā ca sarvagā caiva jñātvā jñānī vimucyate || 65 ||

hitvā sūryañca somañca sarvagaḥ sa śivo bhavet |
* * * * * * * * * * * * ##?

* * * * * * ? sā tu nādaṃ tasyāṃ tu līyate |

p. 851)

sūryamaṇḍalamadhye tu akārastu vyavasthitaḥ || 67 ||

tatra cotpadyate śabdo viśramecchaśimaṇḍale |
svayaṃ viṣṇussamut * * * * * * * * * 
* ? || 68 ||

* * * * ? paraṃ sthānaṃ vahnimaṇḍalamuttamam |
makāreti ca vikhyāto bhitvā nādo layaṃ sthitaḥ || 69 ||

piṅgalāmārgamāpannā turyadvāreti viśrutā |
ardhamātrā tu * * * * * * * * * * 
* ? || 70 ||

dhyāyettu paramaṃ nādaṃ turyadvāreṇa yogavit |
layaṃ tasya tu deveśe yatrāsau paramaśśivaḥ || 71 ||

tadeva niṣkalaṃ tatvaṃ cintyate mokṣavādibhiḥ |

p. 852)

* * * * * * * ? nte śivatatvavido janāḥ || 72 
||

devyuvāca - 

maṇḍalānāñca devānāṃ tatvānāñca maheśvara |
hṛllakṣaḥ paramo jñeyo yogināñca tathaiva hi || 73 ||

tasmin pravartate siddhiḥ yogañca bhu * * * * ? |
* * * * * * * * * * * * 
* ? marhasi || 74 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

sabījanirbījavibhāgena navamātmā parikīrtitaḥ |
hrāsahrāsavibhāgena nānāsiddhiphalapradā || 75 ||

ṣaḍakṣaraparañcaiva pañcākṣaraparaṃ śubham |

p. 853)

caturakṣaraṃ vijānīyāt * * * * * * * ? || 76 ||

* * * * * ? deveśa aṅgāni kathitāni tu |
saṃpūrṇaḥ śiva ityuktaḥ sa eva parameśvari || 77 ||

ekaikahrasitā ye tu bindurukārayojitā |
hṛdayādi samuddiṣṭā jñātavyāstu varānane || 78 ||

* * * * ? ete sarve udya utkramaṇā smṛtāḥ |
hṛllakṣadarśanaṃ devi acirādeva jāyate || 79 ||

idamanyatparaṃ devi kathitāmyanu * * * ? |
* * * * * * * * * * * ? 
śca tathaiva ca || 80 ||

yakāra ukārasaṃyuktaṃ varṇatrayavivarjitam |

p. 854)

dhyāyamāno varārohe bījadīpena bodhitaḥ || 81 ||

saptarātreṇa paśyeta hṛllakṣaṃ parameśvari |
viṣṇuliṅgāni saṅkā * * * * * * * * * 
? || 82 ||

* * * * * ? te yogī pītanīlānyanekaśaḥ |
kṛṣṇaśuklāṃstathā cānyān paśyate raktavarṇakān || 83 ||

nijairdīpitahṛllakṣaṃ saṃharetsaptame'hani | 
jvalate hṛdi padme tu nijadīpasubo * * ? || 84 ||

* * * * * * * * * * * * ##?
jvalate dīptavaddevi hṛdayaṃ nijadīpitam || 85 ||

hṛllakṣamevaṃ vikhyātaṃ jñātavyaṃ yogibhissadā |

p. 855)

nābherūrdhvaṃ vitantukam * * * * * * * * 
? || 86 ||

* * * * * * * * * * * * 
* * * * ? |

karṇikāpadmamadhye tu bindustasyaiva madhyataḥ |

hṛdayaṃ ṣaḍakṣaraṃ prāptaṃ bījaṃ paramadurlabham |
* * * * * * * * * * * ? tto

evamanye'pi ye proktā veditavyā hitaiṣibhiḥ |

idaṃ tatparamaṃ * * * * * * * * * 
* ? |
suvarṇena sahaikatvaṃ gacchate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 90 ||

p. 856)

jñānasiddhistathā devi paśuryāti paraṃ padam |
śivena ca saṃhaikatvaṃ gacchate * * * * * ? || 91 ||

* * * * ? daśāhādvā paśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
daśāhānnaparaṃ laṅghet paśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 92 ||

etallakṣaparaṃ divyaṃ sevitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
hitāya sa * * * * * * * ? tava śobhane || 93 ||

navaśaktisamāyuktaṃ dharmajñānasamanvitam |
āsanaṃ kalpya deveśi tatra nyāsantu kārayet || 94 ||

pūjayetsakalaṃ devi pañcabrahma samanvitam |
ekanāvaraṇenaiva pūjanīyo maheśvaraḥ || 95 ||

p. 857)

sarvasiddhikaro devaḥ pūjyamāno dine dine |
evaṃ pūjya yathā nyāyaṃ japedhyāyetprayogavit || 96 ||

daśalakṣeṇa deveśi manasā yadyadīpsitam |
tattatprāpnotyayatnena sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ || 97 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare bindunādayogaprakaraṇe pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ 
- 
devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi yajanañcānupūrvaśaḥ |
yena vijñātamātreṇa sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

p. 858)

īśvaraḥ - 

prakṛtiñcāditaḥ kṛtvā śivatatvamapaścimam |
ādyamadhye tu ye tatvāḥ kathayāmi yathākramam || 2 ||

ādivaktraṃ likhetpadmaṃ kesarālaṃ sa karṇikam |
tasmin sarvamaśeṣantu vidhiṃ kuryādvarānane || 3 ||

devatamaṇḍalāṃścaiva tritatvena samanvitaṃ |
prakṛtirvinyasenmadhye mūrtinā sahasaṃyutam || 4 ||

sakalīkṛtatatvaistu śivasyāvāhanaṃ kuru |
anantaṃ hṛdayaṃ tasya vyomarūpaṃ śirasmṛtam || 5 ||

vyāpinī tu śikhā proktā anāyakaṃ kavacaṃ smṛtam |

p. 859)

anāsitābhavedastaṃ śivāṅgāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 6 ||

makāraṃ hṛdayaṃ proktamukārantu śirasmṛtam |
īkārantu śivā devi ākāraṃ kavacaṃ viduḥ || 7 ||

aṃkāramastramityuktaṃ kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmāṇamastradaivatyaṃ kavacaṃ viṣṇudaivatam || 8 ||

* * * ? hṛdayaṃ rudraṃ śiraṃ īśānadaivatam |
śivā tu devadeveśi tvameva parikīrtitā || 9 ||

navātmānaṃ śivaṃ vidyāt sarvataḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
puruṣaṃ pūrvatonyasya āgneyyāṃ niyatiṃ nyaset || 10 ||

kālaṃ dakṣiṇapatre tu māyāṃ vai nairṛte diśi |

p. 860)

vidyā paścimataḥ proktā īśvaraṃ vāyugocare || 11 ||

sadāśivaṃ paraṃ tatvaṃ uttareṇa tu vinyaset |
śivatatvaṃ paraṃ śuddhamīśānyaṃ parikalpayet || 12 ||

eṣa te yajanaṃ devi prakṛteḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
svatatvaṃ madhyataḥ kṛtvā yaṣṭavyāni dine dine || 13 ||

evaṃ tatvānyajeddevi prakṛtyādiśivātmikām |
śivāṅgā ye janāḥ proktāḥ karṇikāyāṃ vyavasthitāḥ || 14 ||

keyūrāḥ puṣkalāntaistu kāryā nityaṃ sadā budhaiḥ |
eṣa eva vidhirdeviḥ prakṛtyādi śivāntikāḥ || 15 ||

vidhimevaṃ sakṛt jñātvā na bhūyo janmabhāk bhavet |

p. 861)

uttiṣṭhantardhāne ca pādacāre tathaiva ca || 16 ||

khaḍge ca pāduke caiva añjane recane tathā |
guḍikākāśagamane dantakāṣṭhe pavitrake || 17 ||

vidveṣākarṣaṇecaiva śāntike pauṣṭike tathā |
pūrvasūtreṇa tu ye proktāḥ tatsarvaṃ labhate naraḥ || 18 ||

ekaikaṃ japato mantrī japennānena mantrataḥ |
punaśca śṛṇu deveśi yajanaṃ kathayāmi te || 19 ||

cirāvaraṇasaṃyukta * * * * * * * * ? | 
sakalīkṛtya deveśi niṣkalena śivaṃ smaret || 20 ||

coditāḥ pūrvasūtre tu śivāṅgāḥ kathayāmi te |

p. 862)

sakalaṃ hṛdayaṃ devi sakalāḍhyaṃ śirasmṛtam || 21 ||

khamalantu śikhā proktā kṣapaṇaṃ kavacaṃ tathā |
kṣayāntamastramityuktaṃ śivāṅgāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 22 ||

śūnyenaiva tu deveśi tena tatvaṃ vicintayet |
vidyāṅgāste tu vijñeyāḥ pūrvasūtre tu ye smṛtāḥ || 23 ||

hṛdayaṃ madhyato nyasya śiraḥ pūrvadale nyaset |
dakṣiṇena śikhāṃ dadyāt kavacaṃ paścimena tu || 24 ||

uttareṇāstramityuktaṃ vinyasettu yathākramam |
vidyāṅgā hṛdayaṃ devi āgneyyāṃ parikalpayet || 25 ||

īśānyāntu śiro dadyāt evamādikrameṇa tu |

p. 863)

dvitīyāvaraṇe mantratatvāni parikalpayet || 26 ||

pūrvamādikrameṇaiva vinyasettu yathākramam |
eṣate kathyate devi tatvānāṃ yajanaṃ prati || 27 ||

prakṛtyādi vibhāgena yajettamanupūrvaśaḥ |
evaṃ yaṣṭvā tu tatvāni japeccaiva navātmakam || 28 ||

lakṣaikaikaṃ japenmantraṃ sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt |
manasā cintitaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpādayati mantriṇaḥ || 29 ||

sūtreṇaiva tu yatproktaṃ tatsiddhiphalamāpnuyāt |
dvirāvaraṇametaddhi yajanaṃ samudāhṛtam || 30 ||

punaśca yajanaṃ vakṣye tatvānāṃ varavarṇinī |

p. 864)

tribhirāvaraṇaistatvānyaṣṭvā siddhimavāpnuyāt || 31 ||

pañcabrahmasamāyuktaṃ śivāṅgaiśca samanvitam |
garbhāvaraṇametaddhi kartavyaṃ surasundari || 32 ||

dvitīyaṃ tatvabhiḥ kāryaṃ vidyāṅgaiśca tṛtīyakam |
evaṃ tatvagaṇān devi yaṣṭvā siddhimavāpnuyāt || 33 ||

caturāvaraṇenaiva yaṣṭavyā tu tavadhyati |
yaṃ yaṣṭvā sidhyate mantraṃ mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 34 ||

tatvāni siddhitānyeva karmaṇā manasā girā |
nānena sadṛśaṃ yāgaṃ triṣu lokeṣu vidyate || 35 ||

sādhakānāṃ hitārthantu kathitantu tavākhilam |

p. 865)

pañcabrahma śivāṅgaiśca garbhametattu kalpayet || 36 ||

sakalādi śivāṅgā ye dvitīyāvaraṇe nyaset |
pūrvasūtre tu yatproktā aṅgāścaiva navātmake || 37 ||

tṛtīyāvaraṇe te tu yaṣṭavyā susamāhitaiḥ |
navātmapañcabhirbhedaiḥ yanmayā parikīrtitam || 38 ||

caturāvaraṇe te tu yaṣṭavyāḥ sādhakottamaiḥ |
evaṃ yaṣṭvā tato mantrī japayogaṃ samārabhet || 39 ||

eṣa te kathito devi caturāvaraṇena tu |
budhvā smṛtvā ca mucyante sādhakājjanmabandhanāt || 40 ||

punareva pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā |

p. 866)

yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilam || 41 ||

āvāharodhasānnidhyavisarjanavisarjitam |
nyāsamātreṇa mucyante sādhakājjanmabandhanāt || 42 ||

pūrvoktantu vidhiṃ kṛtvā sādhakaḥ parameśvari |
paramākṣaraṃ nyasettatra karṇikāyāntu mantriṇā || 43 ||

sakalādi krameṇaiva pūrvādārabhya vinyaset |
paramākṣarasya yogoyamevaṃ te samudāhṛtam || 44 ||

sakalaṃ pūrvadigbhāge karṇikāyāntu vinyaset |
pūrvapatre punardevi vinyasetparamākṣaram || 45 ||

āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ tatra śūnyatatvantu dakṣiṇe |

p. 867)

kalāḍhyaṃ naiṛte caiva khamalaṃ paścime tathā || 46 ||

kṣapaṇaṃ vāyudeśe tu kṣayāntañcottare tathā |
īśānyāṃ vinyasenmantrī kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ parameśvari || 47 ||

sakalaṃ padmapīṭhantu kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ |
āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ tatra karṇikāyāntu vinyaset || 48 ||

pūrvapatre tu sakalamāgneyyāṃ paramākṣaram |
dakṣiṇe śūnyatatvantu kalāḍhyaṃ nair-ṛtena tu || 49 ||

paścime khamalaṃ proktaṃ kṣapaṇaṃ vāyugocare |
kṣayāntamuttareṇaiva kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ naiśagocare || 50 ||

niṣkalaṃ padmapīṭhantu āgneyyāṃ parikalpayet |

p. 868)

dakṣiṇe karṇikāyāntu śūnyatatvantu vinyaset || 51 ||

samalaṃ pūrvapatre tu āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ nyaset |
paramākṣaraṃ nyaseddevi dakṣiṇe samudāhṛtam || 52 ||

kaṇṭhoṣṭhyaṃ nair-ṛte caiva khamalaṃ paścime nyaset |
kṣapaṇaṃ vāyudeśe tu vinyasetsamudāhṛtam || 53 ||

śūnyasya padmapīṭhantu kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ |
nair-ṛte karṇikāyāntu kalāḍhyaṃ vinyasecchubhe || 54 ||

pūrvapatre tu sakalamāgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ nyaset |
śūnyaṃ dakṣiṇato nyasya nair-ṛtyāṃ paramākṣaram || 55 ||

paścime khamalaṃ proktaṃ āgneyyāṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam |

p. 869)

kṣayāntamuttareṇaiva kaṇṭhoṣṭhamīśagocare || 56 ||

caturthante samākhyātaṃ pañcamaṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam |
paścime karṇikāyāntu khamalaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 57 ||

pūrvapatre tu sakalamāgneyyāṃ niṣkalasmṛtaḥ |
dakṣiṇe śūnyatatvantu kalāḍhyaṃ nair-ṛtena tu || 58 ||

paramākṣarantu deveśi paścimeva tu vinyaset |
vāyavyāṃ kṣapaṇaṃ caiva kṣayāntañcottareṇa tu || 59 ||

kaṇṭhoṣṭhamīśadeśe tu pañcamaṃ kathitaṃ tava |
vāyavyāṃ karṇikāyāntu kṣapaṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 60 ||

sakalaṃ pūrvapatre tu āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ nyaset |

p. 870)

śūnyañca dakṣiṇe caiva kalāḍhyaṃ naiṛtena tu || 61 ||

khamalaṃ paścimenaiva vāyavyāṃ paramākṣaram |
uttare tu kṣayāntañca kaṇṭhoṣṭhyamīśagocare || 62 ||

ṣaṣṭha eṣa samākhyātaḥ pīṭhaste parikīrtitaḥ |
uttare karṇikāpīṭhe kṣayāntaṃ tatvavinyaset || 63 ||

pūrvataḥ sakalaṃ devi āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ param |
dakṣiṇe śūnyamityuktaṃ kalāḍhyaṃ naiṛtena tu || 64 ||

paścime khamalañcaiva vāyavyāṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam |
paramākṣarantu deveśi uttareṇa tu vinyaset || 65 ||

kaṇṭhoṣṭhyaṃ paramaṃ tatvamīśānyāṃ parikalpayet |

p. 871)

saptamaṃ te samākhyātaṃ tatvaṃ sukharārcite || 66 ||

īśānyāṃ karṇikāpīṭhe kaṇṭhoṣṭhyaṃ parikalpayet |
pūrvapatre tu sakalamāgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ param || 67 ||

śūnyaṃ tu dakṣiṇe patre kalāḍhyaṃ naiṛtena tu |
khamalaṃ paścime caiva kṣapaṇaṃ vāyugocare || 68 ||

kṣayāntamuttare caiva īśānyāṃ paramākṣaram |
aṣṭamaṃ te samākhyātaṃ pīṭhaṃ paramadurlabham || 69 ||

navabhāgaṃ smṛto yāgaḥ sarvabhāgeṣu cottamaḥ |
na mayā kasyacitkhyāta yāga eṣa sudurlabhaḥ || 70 ||

yaṣṭenānena yāgena kṛtārthacchinnasaṃśayaḥ |

p. 872)

vrajate śivasāyujyaṃ nirmalo vigatajvaraḥ || 71 ||

śodhyaṃ daśadaśāhutyā viṃśapañcādhikena tu |
pañcāṃśena śatenaiva dīkṣāṃ kurvīta deśikaḥ || 72 ||

triśaktidīpanaṃ kṛtvā śāntike pauṣṭitarpaṇe |
kartavyaṃ deśikendreṇa homakarma yathākramam || 73 ||

vistare vistaraṃ kuryādvittaśāṭhyanna kārayet |
svacittasyānusāreṇa yajanantu samārabhet || 74 ||

sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ sarvatantreṣu cottamam |
ekāśīti vibhāgantu kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 75 ||

karaṇaissaha saṃyuktā pṛthivyādiranukramāt |

p. 873)

ekenāvaraṇenaiva yajennityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 76 ||

kālayāsassa vijñeyaḥ saha tatvena suvrate |
evaṃ * * * * ? yāgāḥ kathitā hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 77 ||

evaṃ yojayate devi sādhakastu samāhitaḥ |
pṛthivī tena sakalā sarāṣṭrā nāpasaṃyutā || 78 ||

yajitaṃ tena suśroṇi bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
sa jñānī bhūtale nityaṃ sādhakaḥ sa śivaṃ nayet || 79 ||

yena dīkṣitamātrāstu paśavaḥ śivasaṃmukhāḥ |
prayānti nātra sandeho jñānavicchinnabandhanāt || 80 ||

sadāśivaśivaścaiva nādaśaktistathaiva ca |

p. 874)

catvāro hi mahāyāgāḥ akathyeyā varānane || 81 ||

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tadekāgramanā śṛṇu |
sthaṇḍile maṇḍale vā'pi vāyurākāśameva ca || 82 ||

agnirapsu ca deveśi yāgahomaṃ samārabhet |
manasā cakṣuṣā caiva vacasā ca na saṃśayaḥ || 83 ||

dhāraṇā karmayogena mocayennātra saṃśayaḥ |
ebhyaḥ pravartate sarvaṃ asmin sarvamidaṃ smṛtam || 84 ||

ebhyaḥ siddhiśca muktiśca bhikṣitā jāyate śubhe |
pūrvoktantu vidhiṃ kuryāt karṇikāyāṃ maheśvari || 85 ||

mūrtiṃ vinyasya tatraiva sakalīkaraṇaṃ tataḥ |

p. 875)

pañcabrahmakalaiścaiva śivāṅgaiśca samanvitaḥ || 86 ||

āvāharodhasaṃyuktaṃ dhyānayogasamanvitam |
pañcabhiḥ brahmabhiścaiva vidhiṃ kuryādaśeṣataḥ || 87 ||

caturāvaraṇairyuktaṃ yajeta susamāhitaḥ |
īśānaṃ madhyato dadyāt pūrvaṃ tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset || 88 ||

aghoraṃ dakṣiṇecaiva vāmadevaṃ tathottare |
sadyojātantu deveśi paścimena tu vinyaset || 89 ||

āgneyyāṃ hṛdayaṃ nyasya śira īśānagocare |
naiṛtyāntu śikhāṃ devi vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ nyaset || 90 ||

garbhāvaraṇametaddhi kathitaṃ tava sundari |

p. 876)

dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ devi kathyamānaṃ mayā śṛṇu || 91 ||

ghoṣaṃ pūrveṇa vinyasya rāvamagniṃ diśi nyaset |
svanaṃ dakṣiṇato nyasya śabdaṃ naiṛtake nyaset || 92 ||

sphoṭaṃ paścimato dadyāt dhvaniśabdantu vāyave |
uttareṇaiva jhaṅkāraṃ dhvaṅkṛtaṃtvīśagocare || 93 ||

navamaṃ yanmahacchabdaṃ madhye karṇikayā nyaset |
ukārādi smṛtā ye tu ghoṣādīnāñca vācakāḥ || 94 ||

tṛtīyāvaraṇe devi kathyamānaṃ mayā śṛṇu |
sakalaṃ pūrvavaktre tu āgneyyāṃ niṣkalaṃ nyaset || 95 ||

śūnyaṃ dakṣiṇato dadyāt kalāḍhyaṃ naiṛte nyaset |

p. 877)

khamalaṃ paścimenaiva kṣapaṇaṃ vāyugocare || 96 ||

kṣayāntamuttarecaiva kaṇṭhoṣṭhantu īśagocare |
paramākṣarantu deveśi madhyadeśe tu vinyaset || 97 ||

tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ devi kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
caturāvaraṇaṃ devi prakṛtyantaṃ tu vinyaset || 98 ||

pūrvamādikrameṇaiva vinyaset yathākramam |
eṣa yāgaparaṃ devi kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 99 ||

sarvasvaṃ gopitaṃ hyetat sādhakānāṃ hitāvaham |
siddhidaṃ muktidañcaiva nānena sadṛśaṃ bhavet || 100 ||

nindāścatuṣkalaścaiva śṛṇu devi vadāmyaham |

p. 878)

śāntyatītantu hṛdayaṃ āgneyyāṃ parikalpayet || 101 ||

sakalādyāḥ punaḥpaścānnavatatvā yathākramam |
eṣa sādāśivo yogaḥ kathitastava śobhane || 102 ||

nādasyaiva pravakṣyāmi kathyamānaṃ nibodha me |
iḍikā dīpikā caiva recikā mocikā tathā || 103 ||

ūrdhvagāmini saṃyuktaṃ garbhamāvaraṇaṃ sthitam |
ūrdhvagāmini deveśi nyasedāgneyagocare || 104 ||

mocikeśānadigbhāge recikā naiṛte dale |
dīpikāntu mahābhāge vāyavyāṃ parikalpayet || 105 ||

hauṅkāraṃ hṛdayaṃ tasya huṅkārantu śirasmṛtam |

P. 879)

huṅkārantu śikhā proktā haiṃkāraṃ kavacaṃ bhavet || 106 ||

haḥkāramāstramityuktaṃ caturdikṣu ca vinyaset |
garbhāvaraṇametaddhi kathitaṃ surasundari || 107 ||

śeṣaṃ pūrvavaddeveśi ācarecca yathākramam |
asya guhyataraṃ vakṣye śaktiyāgamanuttamam || 108 ||

asmātsamāpyate yogamasmātsarvaṃ pravartate |
nānena kathitā martyā dīkṣānugrahakārakāḥ || 109 ||

na śaktirahitāsiddhi na ca yāti paraṃ padam |
tasmācchreṣṭhopariṣṭhaśca nākhyena paridīkṣaṇe || 110 ||

na rajomaṇḍalaścaiva na lupte tu parigraham |

p. 880)

adhivāsanañcaivatri sarvaśeṣavivarjitam || 111 ||

sānumetatsamākhyātaṃ sādhakānāṃ hitāvaham |
sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ yanmahatsarvatomukham || 112 ||

āvāharodhasānnidhya visarjanavivarjitam |
nyāsamātreṇa mucyante sādhakājanmabandhanāt || 113 ||

catuṣvaktraṃ likhetpadmaṃ kesarojvalasakarṇikam |
āsanaṃ kalpayenmantrī tatra nyāsantu kārayet || 114 ||

pañcabrahmaśivāṅgaiśca śivena sahasaṃyutam |
pūjayitvā vidhānena devadevaṃ maheśvaram || 115 ||

īśānaṃ madhyato dadyāt pūrve tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset |

p. 881)

aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe caiva vāmadevaṃ tathottare || 116 ||

sadyojātantu deveśi paścimena tu vinyaset |
āgneyyāṃ hṛdayañcaiva īśānyāntu śiro nyaset || 117 ||

naiṛtyāṃ tu śikhāṃ devi vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ nyaset |
astraṃ diśāsu vinyasya makārādyāstu ye smṛtāḥ || 118 ||

paramākṣaraṃ nyasenmadhye karṇikāyāṃ maheśvari |
dakṣiṇe karṇikāpīṭhe vinyasecca navātmakaṃ || 119 ||

vidyāṅgā hṛdayaṃ tasya vinyasetsurasundari |
śiraḥ śikhā ca kavacamastrañcaiva yathākramam || 120 ||

prakṛtiḥ dakṣiṇe patre puruṣaṃ naiṛtena tu |

p. 882)

paścime niyatiṃ dadyāt vāyavyāṃ kalavinyaset || 121 ||

māyātatvottareṇaiva īśānyāṃ vidya eva tu |
īśvaraṃ pūrvatatvaṃ āgneyyāṃ tu sadāśivam || 122 ||

paścime karṇikāmadhye śāntyatītantu vinyaset |
āgneyyāṃ hṛdayañcaiva īśānyāntu śironyaset || 123 ||

naiṛtyāntu śikhāṃ devi kavacaṃ vāyugocare |
uttare karṇikāyāntu praṇavaḥ parikalpayet || 124 ||

śāntyatītantu hṛdayaṃ śāntiśca śira ucyate |
vidyāśikhā samākhyātā pratiṣṭhāṃ kavacaṃ viduḥ || 125 ||

nivṛttirasramityuktaṃ śikhāṅgāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |

p. 883)

ghoṣantu pūrvadigbhāge āgneyyāṃ ravameva ca || 126 ||

dakṣiṇe tu svanaḥ proktaḥ śabdaṃ naiṛtagocare |
paścimena smṛtaḥ sphoṭo dhvaniḥ vāyavyato nyaset || 127 ||

jhaṅkāramuttareṇaiva dhvaṃkṛtaṃ śaivagocare |
pūrvataḥ karṇikāmadhye rudroṅkārantu vinyaset || 128 ||

āgneyāṃ hṛdayañcaiva īśānyāntu śiro nyaset |
naiṛtyāntu śikhā proktā vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ tathā || 129 ||

astre hastapradeśe tu hrasvaṃ vai saṃprayodayet |
śāntiṃ pūrvadiśābhāge vidyāṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset || 130 ||

pratiṣṭhāmuttareṇaiva nivṛttiḥ paścimena tu |

p. 884)

śāntyatītaṃ paraṃ tatvamastraṃ devasya kīrtitam || 131 ||

sūkṣmavāyudiśābhāge susūkṣmā naiṛtena tu |
amṛtācāgnidigbhāge kathitā tatvaśobhane || 132 ||

amṛtā mṛtā tu īśānyāṃ kathitā tu varānane |
eṣa yāgavaro devi nāmṛtaḥ kasyacinmayā || 133 ||

mama priyeti bhakteti tena te kathito mayā |
evaṃ yaṣṭvā tu vidhinā dīkṣāṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 134 ||

śatena pañcaviṃśena pañcāśena daśena tu |
pañcasvaśikhāṃ hutvā dīkṣānirvāṇagāminī || 135 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare yāgādhikāraḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ -
p. 885)

devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi dīkṣāvidhimanuttamam |
prakriyāśūnyamantrāṇāṃ śāsanaṃ grahaṇaṃ tathā || 1 ||

tadvarañca mahādeva kasmin kāryaṃ maheśvara |
chedanaṃ kutra vā teṣāṃ śuddhatatvasya śaṅkara || 2 ||

sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva tathā sakalaniṣkalam |
tritatvaṃ paratatvañca tadeva kathayasva me || 3 ||

mantrādhyayanakarmāṇi amṛtīkaraṇaṃ tathā |

p. 886)

yāni śreyastamānisyuḥ kathayasva prasādataḥ || 4 ||

anyat guhyatimandeva yannoktaṃ na pracoditam |
tadasmin sarvamasmākaṃ kathayasva prasādataḥ || 5 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilam |
guhyāt guhyataraṃ devi nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā || 6 ||

prakriyā sūtramantrāṇāṃ yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā śivatatvamapaścimam || 7 ||

kathayāmi samāsena saṃkhyādhvānasya yāvatī |
adhastātparameśāno hyanantaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 8 ||

p. 887)

taṃ hutvā devadeveśi kālāgnaṃ samupakramet |
yāmye śrotā tava proktā tasya te bhuvanā smṛtāḥ || 9 ||

anukrameṇa tān sarvān sahasaivādhikāribhiḥ |
tasyopari samākhyātā narakā ghorarūpiṇaḥ || 10 ||

śatamekantu deveśi catvāriṃśattathaiva ca |
anukrameṇa tān sarvān śodhayet parameśvari || 11 ||

upariṣṭāttu deveśi pātālāssapta eva tu |
śodhanīyāstu deveśi krameṇa varavarṇinī || 12 ||

bhuvanā bhuvanādhīnā bhuvanāntarnivāsinaḥ |
kathitāstu samāsena ekaviṃśadanukramāt || 13 ||

p. 888)

upariṣṭāttu bhūrlokaṃ āditaḥ kathayāmyaham |
triguṇena sthitā devi pātālena samāsataḥ || 14 ||

aṣṭadhā devayonyastu pañcadhā tiryagādayaḥ |
ekadhā caiva mānuṣyā jñeyaṃ saṃsāramaṇḍalam || 15 ||

brahmādimuddhareddevi yāvadbrahmādimāgatam |
evaṃ saṃsāracakrantu kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 16 ||

garbhādhānādimārabhya tathā devavratāni tu |
haviryajñāḥ pākayajñāḥ somasaṃkhyāstathaiva tu || 17 ||

saṃskārāśca samākhyātāścatvāriṃśatkrameṇa tu |
aṣṭādhikāśca jñātavyaṃ deśikena tu || 18 ||

p. 889)

etān hutvā tato mantrī sahasrakratavo nyaset |
padaṃ prāpya parityajya antyeṣṭastadanantaram || 19 ||

antyeṣṭiñca tato hutvā ūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā niyojayet |
bhogasthāneṣu saṃyojya tasmādbhogādviyojayet || 20 ||

viyojayitvā tadbhogān puryaṣṭakasamanvitān |
bhūrloke tu tato yojya bhuvarlokaṃ tato nayet || 21 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva brahmaṇi svāsane tathā |
rudrāsane tato yojya saddhi daṇḍakrameṇa tu || 22 ||

dhyātvā pāśupataṃ devi astraṃ paramadurjayam |
tena cāhutayassapta dadyācchudhyarthakāraṇāt || 23 ||

p. 890)

śatarudre tu saṃyojya śatasthāne yathākramam |
evantu pṛthivī śuddhā śatarudrastu yojitā || 24 ||

anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā pātālaṃ hemasaṃjñitam |
śatamekonaṣaṣṭiñca kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 25 ||

bhuvanān bhuvanādhīśān bhuvanāntarnivāsinaḥ |
prīṇayitvā kṛtān prājñaḥ tatve tatve yathākramam || 26 ||

oṃ bhuvanāya namaḥ | oṃ bhuvanādhipataye namaḥ | oṃ 
bhuvanāntarnivāsibhyaḥ | 

bhuvavyūhahomantu kartavyaṃ deśikena tu |
bhuvanāntarnivāsīnāṃ śikhenaivāhutiṃ hunet || 27 ||

p. 891)

evaṃ sa śodhanārthastu deśikena mahātmanā |
pārthivāvaraṇaṃ devi anantādiranukramāt || 28 ||

kathayāmi samāsena bhuvanānāṃ varānane |
śatadvayaṃ samākhyātaṃ tathā caikona saptatiḥ || 29 ||

kūśmāṇḍena sahaikatvaṃ kathitantu samāsataḥ |
pañcāṣṭakaṃ punaśśodhyaṃ pañcadhāvaraṇaiḥ kramāt || 30 ||

yena yenāṣṭakañcaiva yogāṣṭakamataḥ param |
krodhāṣṭakantu jñātavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 31 ||

tāneva śodhayeddevi tejoṣṭakamataḥ param |
tejoṣṭake tu saṃśuddhe śivadvādaśakaṃ punaḥ || 31 ||

p. 892)

mahādevāṣṭakañcaiva vīrabhadraṃ tathaiva ca |
maṇḍalādhipatiñcaiva kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 32 ||

etān śodhyavidhānena pradhānamupari hunet |
guṇatrayaṃ buddhitatvaṃ śodhanīyaṃ yathākramam || 33 ||

budhyā ca tatvaparyantaṃ pradhānasya tadantare |
krodhatejoṣṭakaṃ śodhyaṃ yogibhiḥ sa śivaissaha || 34 ||

vīrabhadreṇa saṃyuktaṃ maṇḍalādhipatena tu |
gurupaṅktitrayaṃ devi gurorantaritāstathā || 35 ||

sāmyāvasthā guṇānāntu tatpradhānaṃ pravakṣyate |
etatpradhānamityuktaṃ bahuvistarataḥ sthitam || 36 ||

p. 893)

kathitantu samāsena veditavyaṃ varānane |
tayo rūpāḥ samākhyātāḥ tatvānāntu purā mayā || 37 ||

tena rūpeṇa tān rudrān jñātvā śodhyena suvṛte |
anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā pradhānāntastathāvadhi || 38 ||

śatāni caturaḥ proktāḥ catuścatvāriṃśatpunaḥ |
ataḥ paratarañcaiva puruṣaṃ kathayāmi te || 39 ||

yena vijñātamātreṇa mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ |
aṃbhādyāḥ śodhane tatra pauruṣe tu yathākramam || 40 ||

maṇimātaramaṃbhānāṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
dharmo daśavidhaḥ proktaḥ tava devi na saṃśayaḥ || 41 ||

p. 894)

vikāraṣoḍaśaḥ proktāḥ buddhiraṣṭavidhā smṛtāḥ |
ahaṃkārastu trividho guṇatrayamudāhṛtam || 42 ||

prāṇaḥ pañcavidhaścaiva viddhi vidyāṣṭakaṃ tathā |
vigrahāṣṭakasaṃyuktā śodhanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 43 ||

kāmakrodhaśca lobhaśca janmamṛtyujarāstathā |
vyādhiśca śāśvato loke kṣuttṛṣṇāśokameva ca || 44 ||

vivādaśca bhayaścaiva madamohassudāruṇaḥ |
rāṇaśśokaśca mohaśca madamātsaryameva ca || 45 ||

dharmādharmaśca tṛṣṇā ca asvatantrā sadā tanuḥ |
ete bandhātmakā pāśāḥ gaṇapāśīmato'nyathā || 46 ||

p. 895)

tatvapāśāśca vividhā tatve tatve vyavasthitāḥ |
deśapāśāstu ye tasya śivena parikīrtitāḥ || 47 ||

vauṣaṭkārantu yuktena śodhanīyāstu deśikaiḥ |
śatapañcāśatenaiva pañcaviṃśena vā punaḥ || 48 ||

daśapañca daśenāpi trayaḥ pañcadvayena vā |
ekāhutyā tu deveśi śodhanīyāḥ śivādhvare || 49 ||

eṣa te homabhedastu kathito varavarṇinī |
dhyātvā bhaktimayaṃ śiṣyaṃ dīkṣāṃ kuryāttu deśikaḥ || 50 ||

bhuvanānāntu yā saṃkhyā śivena kathitā purā |
śatamekantu vikhyātā niyatyāṃ bhuvanādhipāḥ || 51 ||

p. 896)

sarve padmāsanāsīnāḥ vidyāvidyeśvarairvratāḥ |
śuklakṛṣṇavibhāgasthāṃ dhyātvā śodhyeta mokṣakaḥ || 52 ||

śuddhabuddhaprabuddhaśca daśaite bhuvanādhipāḥ |
kālatatve tu vikhyātā koṭyanekaparigrahāḥ || 53 ||

rāgatatve daśaproktāḥ bhuvaneśā mahābalāḥ |
suhṛṣṭaḥ suprahṛṣṭaśca surūpo rūpavardhaniḥ || 54 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva vedanīyo mumukṣubhiḥ |
ataḥ paratareṇaiva vidyeśānāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 55 ||

kalyāṇaḥ piṭhalo haṃsāḥ evamādi prakīrtitāḥ |
vidyātatve tu jñātavyāḥ deśikena mahātmanā || 56 ||

p. 897)

kālatatve tu vijñeyā ye rudrā bhuvanādhipāḥ |
vidhinā śodhanīyāstu deśikena mahātmanā || 57 ||

vāmajyeṣṭhaḥ tathā bhīmaḥ evamādi prakīrtitāḥ |
gopateśca tadordhvantu grathidvāre vyavasthitāḥ || 58 ||

asaṃkhyā rudrakoṭībhiḥ gopatiḥ parivāritaḥ |
ataḥ paratareṇaiva trikālaḥ kṣemīśa eva ca || 59 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva pañcarudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
yogakṣemapradātārā dīkṣādhvānaviśodhitāḥ || 60 ||

ata ūrdhvamananteśa pāśāścaiva tu saṃsthitāḥ |
madamohasamāyuktaṃ evamādimahāśaye || 61 ||

p. 898)

punā ṛṣikule proktaṃ tato vāgīśvarī punaḥ |
chaṅkārasādhyamoṅkāra dātāradavaneśikam || 62 ||

dhyānāhārañca bhasmeśaṃ parivārya vicakṣaṇaḥ |
pramāṇāṣṭakamevāpi māyāvaraṇasaṃsthitaḥ || 63 ||

rudrāṅkuśantu prathamaṃ vyūhamantre pratiṣṭhitam |
evamādīni hutvā tu munetmanāṃ vidhānavit || 64 ||

niyamāditaḥ kṛtvā māyātatvamapaścimam |
ebhyo madhye tu ye rudrāḥ kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ || 65 ||

niyatyāṃ daśa rudrāstu kālatatve daśa sthitāḥ |
rage'pi ca daśa proktāḥ vidyāyāṃ na punardaśa || 66 ||

p. 899)

kālatatve daśa proktāḥ grantherekaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
upariṣṭātpunaḥ pañcamāyātatvā tu saṃsthitāḥ || 67 ||

ata ūrdhvamananteśapāśāścaiva tu saṃsthitāḥ |
madamohādi saṃyuktāḥ pāśahastā mahābalāḥ || 68 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva yāvanmāyānta saṃsthitāḥ |
saṃkhyā vai bhuvanānāntu saptatiḥ saptacādhikāḥ || 69 ||

ātmatatvamidaṃ proktamaśuddhaṃ parikīrtitam |
anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā māyātatvamapaścimam || 70 ||

yebhyo madhye tu ye rudrāstān pravakṣyāmi saṃkhyayā |
ekasaṃśīkṛtāssarve kathayāmi tavākhilam || 71 ||

p. 900)

śatāstu ṣaṭ samuddiṣṭā catvāriṃśattathaiva ca |
bhuvanā saptacādhikyaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 72 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā vidhānantu śodhayedaviśaṅkitaḥ |
ātmatatvamidaṃ khyātaṃ māyāntaṃ surasundari || 73 ||

veditavyaṃ prayatnena vidyāyā śṛṇu sāṃpratam |
ananteśāditaḥ kṛtvā hotavyamanupūrvaśaḥ || 74 ||

bhuvane saṃsthitāssarve bodhanīyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
īśvare śodhanīyāstu māyāñjanavivarjitāḥ || 75 ||

ato rūpacatuṣṭayaṃ hotavyamanupūrvaśaḥ |
vāmājjyeṣṭhādayastasmāt śaktayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 76 ||

p. 901)

ato jñānakriyāśaktiḥ jñātavyāḥ punareva hi |
tāṃ viditvā mahādevi deśikaḥ pāśahā bhavet || 77 ||

tejīśaśca bravīśaśca pramāṇe paramaṃ padam |
dvau rudrau tu samākhyātau yogamokṣaphalapradau || 78 ||

tataḥ prāṇaḥ smṛto devi brahmadattaṃ tataḥ punaḥ |
catvārastu smṛtā rudrāḥ yogamokṣa phalapradāḥ || 79 ||

ekāhaḥ piṅgalo haṃso rudratrayamudāhṛtam |
atopari dhruvaṃ devi rudraikādaśakaṃ punaḥ || 80 ||

brahmadattādi saṃyuktā jaṃbhikāntāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu parato devi dhruvaścaiva tṛtīyakaḥ || 81 ||

p. 902)

saptakoṭiparīvārā sthitā bhuvananāyakāḥ |
ato nirañjanaṃ proktaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate || 82 ||

nirañjanamatikramya icchāśaktirvyavasthitā |
ataścaivopariṣṭāttu aṣṭau te bhuvaneśvarāḥ || 83 ||

pratirādyā smṛtā devi pradyatāntāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
śodhanīyā varārohe yogakṣemapradāyakāḥ || 84 ||

saṃsthitā sūkṣmarūpeṇa praticchāyā vidhāyinī |
ataḥ paraṃ nirālaṃbaṃ sarvadhyāna vivarjitam || 85 ||

svaśaktikiraṇāyānaṃ sarvaṃ vyāpya vyavasthitam |
nāparaṃ na parañcaiva parāparavivarjitam || 86 ||

p. 903)

sarvajñānaparātītaṃ nissaṃdigdhamavasthitam |
pāraṃparyeṇa saṃsthañca nirmalaṃ paramaṃ padam || 87 ||

na tasya śakyate vaktuṃ vijñeyo niṣkalaśśivaḥ |
tena sarvamidaṃ vyāptaṃ kalpanātītalakṣaṇam || 88 ||

na ca vastvantaraṃ kiñcit nyastatvāddhvanirucyate |
otaprotamanādyantaṃ sarvatatvāvabhāsakam || 89 ||

etat jñānaṃ parātītaṃ jñānasadbhāvamiṣyate |
etatte kathitaṃ devi bhuvanānāṃ samāsataḥ || 90 ||

saṃkṣepeṇa mayā proktaṃ bhuvanādhvā tu śobhane |
vidyātatve tu deveśi bhuvaneśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 91 ||

p. 904)

īśvare'pi tathaivāha tathā caiva sadāśive |
tān pravakṣyāhaṃ devi guṇatatvena suvrate || 92 ||

atītabhuvanāḥ proktāḥ vidyātatve samāsataḥ |
viditvā śodhane devi deśikaḥ tatvapāragaḥ || 93 ||

śivatatve tu ye proktāḥ śivena paramātmanā |
ekasaṃkhyaikataḥ kṛtvā pravakṣyāmi varānane || 94 ||

śataṃ vai saptadeveśi bhuvanānāṃ prakīrtitāḥ |
viṃśadvayañca jñātavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 95 ||

amanaskā iti sahasreṇa bhuvanādhvāni śuddhyati |
pūrṇāhutyā śatenāpi viśuddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 96 ||

p. 905)

niṣkalena daśenaiva śūnye pañcāhutistathā |
praśāntena trayaḥ proktāḥ sarvādhvānamaśeṣataḥ || 97 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśattatvairadhvānaṃ sakalena tu homayet |
praṇavena samākhyātaṃ śāntyatītena vā punaḥ || 98 ||

paramākṣareṇa hotavyaṃ parātmano maheśvari |
kālādhvanaṃ varārohe bhuvanādhvena homayet || 99 ||

tathā dhvaneṣu tajjetā homayecca śataṃ śatam |
kalādhvāne tathaiveha kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 100 ||

evaṃ saṃkṣepataḥ proktaṃ tatvādhvānaṃ viśeṣataḥ |
veditavyaṃ prayatnena deśikena mahātmanā || 101 ||

p. 906)

samayī putrako vā'tha sādhakācārya eva ca |
upapannāya dāntāya gurudevaratāya ca || 102 ||

teṣāmanugrahaṃ kāryamācāryeṇa mahātmanā |
pūjayitvā vidhānena devadevaṃ maheśvaram || 103 ||

agnau hutvā yathā nyāyaṃ haste yāgaṃ yajedbudhaḥ |
savyahaste tu yaṣṭavyaṃ vāmahastena saṃvrate || 104 ||

pūjayitvā vidhānena adhvādhaḥ kramaśo nyaset |
gandhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ yathā nyāyaṃ pūjayitvā vidhānavit || 105 ||

dhyātvā devaṃ maheśānaṃ śiṣyamāhūya mantravit |
ātmanyevaṃ vaśīkṛtya śiṣyasya parameśvari || 106 ||

p. 907)

ālabhya śivahastena tasya dehaṃ samantataḥ |
tatvāni dehato'nyasya sthānantu śirasi nyaset || 107 ||

jvālāmālākalākāraṃ dhyātvā devaṃ maheśvari |
dhyātvā devaṃ maheśānaṃ bhūmimadhye tu tatvavit || 108 ||

puṇyapāpaṃ dahettasya dhyātvā dehaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ kṛte tato devi śivahasteti sa smṛtaḥ || 109 ||

śivahastapradānena samayī sa vidhīyate |
anena vidhinā devi kuryāttasyāpyanugraham || 110 ||

sāyujyamīśvare tatve bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
bhūtapretapiśācebhyo rākṣasebhyassamādhipe || 111 ||

p. 908)

pannakebhyastu suśroṇi avadhyastridaśairapi |
adhvānaṃ kurute sohi na tu na vyāpayetparam || 112 ||

samayī sarvatantreṣu bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
putrako yena deveśi kathyate śivayogibhiḥ || 113 ||

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi kathyamānaṃ nibodha me |
gaṇayāgāditaḥ kṛtvā dṛṣṭvā khermaṇḍalāni tu || 114 ||

hutvā tu vidhivaddevi tatvapāśairviyojitaḥ |
yojitastu pare tatve putrakassa vidhīyate || 115 ||

sa eva mantrakalpajño mantratantra vidhānavit |
mantrakalpasuvettā ca deśakālavidhānavit || 116 ||

p. 909)

japayogavidhānajñaḥ tatvajñaśca vidhānavit |
tāpane dīpane caiva chedane ca tathaiva ca || 117 ||

mohanastaṃbhane caiva saṃpuṭajñastathaiva ca |
paramantravibhedajño mantrāpyāyanatatparaḥ || 118 ||

deśajātivibhedajñaḥ yojanāya ca tatparaḥ |
pātane daityabhūtebhyaḥ siṃhavidyādhareṣvapi || 119 ||

sarvajñaśca kṛtajñaśca śaucācāra samanvitaḥ |
samarthaśca parityāgī japāsaktassadā mataḥ || 120 ||

catuṣpāt saṃjñitābhijñaḥ kalābhijñastathaiva ca |
utsāhavān sadā dakṣaḥ sādhakassa vidhīyate || 121 ||

p. 910)

kalaśāṣṭadaśenaiva navapañcadaśena vā |
abhiṣekapradātavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 122 ||

saṃpuṭīkṛtya vidyākhyaṃ kalaśaṃ surasundarī |
abhiṣiñcettataḥ śiṣyaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 123 ||

pṛthivyādi samārabhya māyātatvamapaścimam |
saṃpuṭīkṛtya paścāttu vidyākhyenābhiṣecayet || 124 ||

evañcaivābhiṣiktastu sādhakassa vidhīyate |
pṛthivīmāditaḥ kṛtvā nirvāṇāntena śobhane || 125 ||

abhiṣiktastu deveśe ācāryassa vidhīyate |
maṇḍalasyeśabhāge tu kuryādvai caturaṅgakam || 126 ||

p. 911)

caturhastasuvistāraṃ hastamātrasamucchritam |
toraṇaissapatākaiśca diśāsu vidiśāsu ca || 127 ||

pīṭhamevaṃ vidhaṃ kṛtvā ācāryassusamāhitaḥ |
akālamūlakalaśaiścandanenopalepitaiḥ || 128 ||

cūtapallavasaṃyuktaiḥ dūrvāṅkurasamanvitaiḥ |
sarvoṣadhi samāyuktaiḥ ekaikaṃ kalaśaṃ kuru || 129 ||

pañcabrahma śivāṅgaiśca śivena sahasaṃyutam |
ekaikakalaśe devi vinyaseta yathākramam || 130 ||

samudrāssapta saptaiva ekaikakalaśe nyaset |
pṛthivyādīni kalaśe vinyaseta yathākramam || 131 ||

p. 912)

kautukairmaṅgalaiścaiva strīsaṅgītakasaṃyutaiḥ |
vedaśabdajayākāraiḥ pūrvādārabhya śobhane || 132 ||

abhiṣiñcecca matimān pṛthivyādi samāyutam |
nirvāṇakalaśaṃ yāvat eṣa eva vidhismṛtaḥ || 133 ||

abhiṣiktaṃ tadā śiṣyaṃ ānayecchivasannidhau |
arghyādibhissamabhyarcya adhikāraṃ samarpayet || 134 ||

saṃhitāṃ suk sruvañcaiva karaṇīṃ kartarīṃ tathā |
kartavyo'nugrahaṃ nityaṃ caturāśramasaṃyutam || 135 ||

vyākhyācānugrahaścaiva guruśiṣyaprabodhanam |
svalpamahni japaṃ kāryaṃ svalpamanhau tu homayet || 136 ||

p. 913)

svalpaṃ vai dhyānamuttiṣṭhet satrametatsadā gurau |
ete'dhikārā ye proktāḥ śivajñāne vikalpitāḥ || 137 ||

evantu śrāvayeddevi ācāryastatvapāragāḥ |
evaṃ kurvāmahe tāta yattvayā kathitaṃ mama || 138 ||

suvikalpena me kāryā śivājñā yattvayoditā |
evaṃ tvaṃ pūjayitvā tu ācāryaḥ sumahāmatiḥ || 139 ||

praṇipatya yathā nyāyaṃ tato devaṃ visarjayet |
visarjite tato devi gurupūjāmupakramet || 140 ||

maṇḍalaṃ hastamātrantu likhitvā tu samāhitaḥ |
tatrasthaṃ sthāpya pūjyeta yathāvidhi purassaram || 141 ||

p. 914)

mantraikādaśakenaiva pūjayeta yathākramam |
yathā bhagavataḥ pūjāṃ kuryādācāryakasya tu || 142 ||

vittaśāṭhyanna kurvīta ācāryaṃ pratisādhakaḥ |
śivavatpūjayeddevi ācāryaṃ tantrapāragam || 143 ||

tena pūjitamātreṇa śivo bhavati deśikaḥ |
tatprasādādbhavetsiddhiḥ muktiścaiva śivoditā || 144 ||

tasmātpūjyeta deveśi ācāryaṃ tatra pāragam |
karṇikākaṭakairvastraiḥ mekhalairmakuṭaistathā || 145 ||

grāmadeśapradānaiśca viṣayākheṭakaistathā |
hastyaśvarathayānaiśca rukmabhedaiśca saṃyutam || 146 ||

p. 915)

bastrakhaṇḍa samāyuktāṃ dhenuṃ dadyāt suśobhanām |
yena yena sutuṣṭena na tattat dadyāttu putrakaḥ || 147 ||

pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ātmānantu nivedayet |
nivedite tato devi anṛṇo jāyate guroḥ || 148 ||

saṃpūjya vidhinā devi guruñca susamāhitaḥ |
bhojayedgurubhaktāṃśca dīnānāthāṃśca tarpayet || 149 ||

śuklā tu dakṣiṇā dadyāt bhojayitvā vidhānataḥ |
vaktavyantu tato devi prīyatāṃ bhagavāñcchivaḥ || 150 ||

evaṃ yaḥ kurute devi sa siddhiphalabhāk bhavet |
anyathā kurute yastu ācāryaṃ pratisādhakaḥ || 151 ||

p. 917)

dīkṣājñānaṃ gurutvañca hitvā narakagāmikaḥ |
ācāryaṃ śivavanmantā vidyāccaiśvarasādhakam || 152 ||

putrakaṃ bhrātṛvaccaiva samaṃ caiva tathaiva ca |
mānyāḥ pūjyāśca deveśi anyonyaprītitāṃ vrajet || 153||

evaṃ vidividhānajño labhate śāśvataṃ padam |
tatvenāpi vidhijñena dīkṣitaḥ śivatāṃ vrajet || 154 ||

svacchandaḥ sarvago bhūtvā vicarettu yathā sukham |
na tasya hanyate siddhiḥ yāvaddevaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 155 ||

sādhakasya vidhiḥ khyātaḥ sarvakāmārthasādhakaḥ |
ācāryasya tu deveśe samayī putrakasya ca || 156 ||

p. 918)

sarveṣāntu samākhyātaṃ yathāvidhipurassaram |
sarvathā sarvamāno'pi tatvavinmocayetpaśum || 157 ||

tatvavit sa śivaḥ sākṣāt tasmāttaṃ pratipūjayet |
mānuṣīṃ tanumāsādya śive jñātaṃ prayacchati || 158 ||

tasmāttatvavidācāryaḥ śivavat pūjayet sadā |
māyā tasya na kartavyā kṛtvā māyāṃ vrajatyayam || 159 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ |
īśvaraḥ - 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ parameśvari |

p. 919)

pratyekaṃ saṃsmṛtā mantrāḥ mayā proktāstu suvrate || 1 ||

sādhāraṇaviśeṣāṇi śivena kathitāni vai |
pañcabrahmaśivāṅgaiśca vidyāṅgāni tathaiva ca || 2 ||

gāyatrī caiva sāvitrī tathaiva paramākṣaram |
śāntyatītañca praṇavaṃ tathā caiva navātmakam || 3 ||

vidyeśā lokapālāśca jñeyāssādhāraṇāni vai |
yatra pratyekahomasyāt tatra sādhāraṇairyajet || 4 ||

pratyekaśastu deveśi tāstu tatraiva pūjayet |
praṇave kathitā ye tu te tu pratyekaśasmṛtāḥ || 5 ||

nivṛtyādyāḥ kalāyāstu tāsu pratyekaśasmṛtāḥ |

p. 920)

padabhedavibhāgena aṣṭatriṃśatkalaistathā || 6 ||

mātṛkāyāṃ tathā devi pratyekaṃ samudāhṛtam |
śodhyaśodhakabhāvena tathā cānye prakīrtitāḥ || 7 ||

evaṃ jñātvā varārohe homakarma samārabhet |
daśayā dīpitairmantraiḥ karmakuryādyathepsitam || 8 ||

oṅkāradīpitā mantrāḥ namaskārāntayojitāḥ |
huṅkāramādiyogena svāhāntena tu mantriṇam || 9 ||

ākarṣayanti bhūtāni divyamānuṣapannagāḥ |
oṅkāramāditaḥ kṛtvā vauṣaḍantena mantriṇaḥ || 10 ||

śāntikaṃ phaladaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |

p. 921)

oṅkāradīpitā mantrā namaskārāntayojitāḥ || 11 ||

arcane tu prayoktavyā agnau svāhāntayojitāḥ |
suvauṣaṭkārasaṃyuktaṃ puṣyarthe viniyojayet || 12 ||

vaṣaṭkāreṇa saṃyuktamamṛtīkaraṇaṃ hitam |
manonmaneti mantrāṇāṃ indyanārthaṃ prayojayet || 13 ||

utsādane prayoktavyaṃ phaṭkāreṇa samanvitam |
māraṇe phaṭ phaḍityeva mantraṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 14 ||

ādihuṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ madhye mantrasamanvitam |
phaṭkāramantrayogena pāśacchedakarasmṛtim || 15 ||

eṣa mantraprayogastu kathitastava śobhane |

p. 922)

aviditvā na sidhyeta na ca yāti paraṃ padam || 16 ||

tasmānmantrapadasyaiva bhedanantu prayojayet |
sidhyante sādhakendrāstu prayogairmantrayojitaiḥ || 17 ||

yā yogena sidhyante na ca yānti paraṃ padam |
dīkṣā homaṃ sadā kuryāt svāhākārāntikaṃ padam || 18 ||

huṃphaṭkārakṛtenaiva pāśacchedantu kārayet |
vauṣaḍantena deveśi pūrṇāhutyāntu dāpayet || 19 ||

ekākṣarasmṛto mantro dvyakṣaratryakṣaro paraḥ |
pañcaṣaṭ saptacāṣṭau tu bahvakṣaramayaḥ paraḥ || 20 ||

ekākṣarasya mantrasya plutenoccāramiṣyate |

p. 923)

āpādatalamūrdhāntaṃ vyāpayitvā vyavasthitaḥ || 21 ||

hṛdi pādakalaṃ yāvat brahmā tatra vyavasthitaḥ |
caturviṃśatibhistatvaiḥ prakṛtibhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 22 ||

etadbrahmādhidaivatyaṃ vyākhyātaṃ tava śobhane |
bhuvanārthaṃ caturviṃśat vyākhyātāni śivena tu || 23 ||

puruṣañcāditaḥ kṛtvā māyātatvañca paścimam |
kathitaṃ viṣṇudaivatyaṃ hṛdayāt karṇikāvadhi || 24 ||

asmin madhye tu ye rudrā kīrtitā bhuvanaissaha |
viṣṇunā saha modante viṣṇutulyaparākramāḥ || 25 ||

tālumadhye gatoccāro rudrastatra prakīrtitaḥ |

p. 924)

vidyāvidyeśvaraiścaiva tatra te bhuvanaissaha || 26 ||

taṃ viditvā mahādevi rudratulyaparākramaḥ |
krīḍate rudraloke tu rudratulyabalo bhuvi || 27 ||

mūrdhni sthānagatoccāramaiśvaraṃ taṃ vinirdiśet |
sa tatra īśvaro devaḥ ādhipatyena saṃsthitaḥ || 28 ||

tacchabdantu yathā bhinnaṃ vidyātatvaṃ tatopari |
tatra devaḥ sadākhyastu ādhipatyena saṃsthitaḥ || 29 ||

śaktitrayaṃ kṛtādhāro dvidhāvasthena saṃsthitaḥ |
sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgena viditvā mokṣamāpnuyāt || 30 ||

caturthāvasthitaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sthūlamekavidhaṃ smṛtam |

p. 925)

iḍikādīpikā caiva rocikā mocikā tathā || 31 ||

sthūlametatsmṛto nādaḥ viditvā mokṣamāpnuyāt |
kalāhyetāstu catvāraḥ ekatve tu yadā sthitāḥ || 32 ||

yathā sthūlaṃ smṛtannādaṃ mayā te kathitaṃ śubhe |
sūkṣmamekavidhañcaiva ūrdhvagāmīti viśrutam || 33 ||

tatrasthā devatāḥ sarvāssamudāyena saṃsthitāḥ |
vyāptiyogena jñātavyā devatāhyaghanāśanāḥ || 34 ||

tasmātparatarā śaktiḥ pañcadhā saṃvyavasthitāḥ |
sūkṣmā caiva susūkṣmā ca amṛtā ca mṛtāmṛtā || 35 ||

sūkṣmabhāvā smṛtā hyetāśśaktestu parameśvari |

p. 926)

tasyāpi parataḥ sūkṣmā vyāpinītyabhidhīyate || 36 ||

tasyāḥ parataro devi gurupāraṃparāgatam |
taṃ viditvā vimucyante narāssaṃsārasāgarāt || 37 ||

mayā te kathitā devi śaktiḥ paramakāraṇā |
tasmin śūnyalayaṃ devi vighnantīṃ vinirdiśet || 38 ||

uccāreṇa vinā devi varṇā nijalayaṃ gataḥ |
kaṇṭhastho viramecchabdaḥ ardhacandra iti smṛtaḥ || 39 ||

tasya śabdasya yacchabda ṛṣimantaṃ vibhāvya ye |
nirodhī sā vinirdiṣṭā lalāṭe tu layaṃ viduḥ || 40 ||

bhinnametadyadā devi tadā nādaṃ vinirdiśet |

p. 927)

abhinno ghāṭamityuktaṃ lalāṭe tu layaṃ viduḥ || 41 ||

bhinnaṃ tadvadyadā devi tadā nādaṃ vinirdiśet |
abhinno ghāṭamityuktaṃ lalāṭastho nivartate || 42 ||

bindoḥ paratareṇaiva nādatatvaṃ layaṃ vrajet |
nādatatvalaye līnā sa paratvena gacchati || 43 ||

paratve saṃsthitā śaktiḥ taccheṣa vyāpinī tathā |
tasmādgurutaro devi gurupāraṃparāgataḥ || 44 ||

kāladeśavinirmuktamuccārālaṃbanā ṛtam |
japayogavinirmuktaṃ karaṇādhāravarjitam || 45 ||

bindumātravinirmuktaṃ hetuvādavivarjitam |

p. 928)

kliśyante devatāssarve ṛṣayaḥ sāṃśatavratāḥ || 46 ||

taṃ prāpya na nivartante ye cānye bhuvi mānavāḥ |
etatsarvaṃ paratvena kathitaṃ tatvamuttamam || 47 ||

mantrasya viṣuvaṃ devi śṛṇuṣva kathayāmi te |
mantramuccārato devi yāvannānyamano bhavet || 48 ||

tāvanmantrayatīrātmā dehamadhye vyavasthitaḥ |
namaskāraṃ tataḥ proktaṃ svāhākārāntayojitam || 49 ||

bindudvayaṃ yadātītaṃ tanmantraṃ viṣuvaṃ smṛtam |
prāṇe tu taccaturthī tu vibhedavaravarṇinī || 50 ||

praśāntaṃ prāpya deveśi niṣkalaṃ viṣuvaṃ smṛtam |

p. 929)

uttarāyaṇapūrvāhṇe dvitīye dakṣiṇāyanam || 51 ||

ubhayormadhyadeśe tu kālena viṣuvaṃ smṛtam |
catvāro viṣuvā devi jānīte deśikottamaḥ || 52 ||

sa vimocayate bandhaṃ kṣipraṃ saṃsārabandhanāt |
etatsarvaṃ samāsena mayā tu kathitaṃ tava || 53 ||

mantre samaraso bhūtvā tiṣṭhate parameśvari |
mantraṃ samaraso devi śivena parikīrtitaḥ || 54 ||

nāḍimadhyagatohyātmā śivabhaktibhirīśvara |
nāḍīsamaraso devi kathitastava śobhane || 55 ||

bhūteṣu vyāpya deveśi tiṣṭhate parameśvaraḥ |

p. 930)

sa bhūtasamaraso devi mayā te parikīrtitaḥ || 56 ||

sarveṣāṃ samarasānāntu śreṣṭhaṃ sarvaguṇodgatam |
tatvaṃ samāsato devi śivena kathitaṃ mama || 57 ||

tatvaṃ samāsayettattu durlabhaṃ tridaśairapi |
na mayā kasyacitkhyātaṃ tatvaṃ samarasaṃ śubham || 58 ||

catvāraḥ samarasā devi kathitāstu tato mayā |
tatvasandhānadeveśi tatvataḥ kathayāmi te || 59 ||

mantrāṇāṃ sandhānañcaiva adhvare tu yathā śṛṇu |
śivādi tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā anupūrveṇa mantravit || 60 ||

prāyaścittaṃ tato hutvā ātmanaśca tu deśikaḥ |

p. 931)

nāticitramapāyasya sūtracchedaṃ tathaiva ca || 61 ||

kṛśe sthūle raje caiva prāyaścittantu homayet |
etān hutvā tato mantrī mantrāṇāṃ dīpanaṃ hunet || 62 ||

paśohyālaṃbhanaṃ kṛtvā prāyaścittantu homayet |
huṅkāramādimadhyeṣu ante caiva prayojayet || 63 ||

phaṭkāraṃ tatparatvena dāpanantu prakīrtitam |
evaṃ sandīpanaṃ kṛtvā paśorathe varānane || 64 ||

tatastu sandhayīnmantrī tritatvañca yathākramam |
śivatatve tu vidyākhyāmātmatatvañca vidyayā || 65 ||

vidyātatvātmatatvena ekīkṛtya tu buddhimān |

p. 932)

tatastu śivatatvena sandhayīta vicakṣaṇaḥ || 66 ||

tatastritatvasandhānaṃ mantrāṇāṃ sandhanaṃ śṛṇu |
vidyeśāṃ śivatatvena lokeśān vidyasaṃjñite || 67 ||

astrāṇi cātmatatve tu sandhayet parameśvari |
evantu sandhānaṃ kṛtvā pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet || 68 ||

pātyamāne vikārāṇi agnimadhye sa paśyati |
sa dhūmaściṭaciṭāśabdo viṣṇuliṅgairvirājitaḥ || 69 ||

evantu lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā brahmaghnaṃ taṃ vinirdiśet |
nīlamamranibhākāraṃ sa dhūmaṃ śālikā mataḥ || 70 ||

śrūyamāṇe yadā vahnau surāpantu vinirdiśet |

p. 933)

asmin dheyane havye caiva pariplute * * *? || 71 ||

kṣaṇājjvalananirvāti steyakāraṃ vinirdiśet |
vistaraṃ ghorarūpañca sa dhūmañca likhenmahīm || 72 ||

evaṃ lakṣaṇavijñāne kṛtagurvaṅganāgamaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ rūpamādhatte kṛṣyamāno'pi pāvakaḥ || 73 ||

sa tu saṃyogiko devi pātakaṃ tasya nirdiśet |
upapātakināścānye tānyevamupalakṣayet || 74 ||

dhyākṣasvarastathā rūkṣakākolūkaśvaraśvaraḥ |
uṣṭragṛdhrasvarāghorāḥ śivāvānararāvakāḥ || 75 ||

sa dhūmandhūmalannīcayo bhūmyāñca vilikhetsakṛt |

p. 934)

jalate kradhukā śabdaḥ phaṭkāraśca sakṛt sakṛt || 76 ||

evaṃ vai lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā upapātakino narāḥ |
dṛṣṭvaitānyapacihnāni dīkṣākarma na kārayet || 77 ||

koṇadeśe ca taṃ sthāpya tamuddiśya ca deśikaḥ |
homayeddīpanañcaiva navatatvālayaṃ prabhuḥ || 78 ||

khaṭ khaṭeti ca catvāraḥ phaṭ phaṭeti punaḥ punaḥ |
paścāttatvakṛtaṃ tatvaṃ punarhomakṛtena tu || 79 ||

śudhyate tu kimāścaryaṃ tato homena pātayaḥ |
yathā viṣaṃ mahāghoraṃ saptadhātugataṃ haret || 80 ||

evaṃ harati tatpāpaṃ tatvavitpāpakarmaṇām |

p. 935)

brahmahatyādipāpāni upapātakināṃ tathā || 81 ||

śatamaṣṭaśatenāpi śodhayennātra saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ saṃśodhya śiṣyantu dīkṣākarma samārabhet || 82 ||

na tu saṅkīrtayetpāpamanyeṣāṃ deśikottamaḥ |
kathayā doṣamāyāti tasmātkruddhaṃ vivarjayet || 83 ||

kathitaṃ mantrasadbhāvaṃ sarveṣāmuttamottamam |

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ |
īśvaraḥ - 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāṃ surasundari |

p. 936)

prakriyāsūtrayogañca pāśānāṃ grahaṇaṃ tathā || 1 ||

yadyavijñāya kartavyaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilam |
kanyā kartṛtvasūtreṇa triguṇītriguṇī kṛte || 2 ||

pañcagavyena cābhyukṣya gandhatoyena vā punaḥ |
arcyekādaśakenaiva tatsūtrantu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 3 ||

arcayitvā tu tatsūtramācāryassusamāhitaḥ |
pādāṅguṣṭhapradeśe tu sūtraṃ kṛtvā pradhārayet || 4 ||

prasārayettataḥ sūtraṃ śikhāgrādyāvasthitam |
sakalīkṛtya tatsūtraṃ sarvamantrairvicakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||

yaśśivaḥ pūjito devi deśikena mahātmanā |

p. 937)

tena pāśāni saṃgṛhya sūtragranthyā tu kārayet || 6 ||

saṃpuṭīkṛtya taṃ pāśa gṛhetsaṃhāramudrayā |
tatvena bandhayedbhūyaḥ tān pāśāṃstu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 7 ||

krameṇa sarvapāśāni bandhayeddeśikottamaḥ |
badhvā pāśaṃ yathānyāyaṃ vāsayeddeva saṃyutam || 8 ||

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya devadevena saṃmataḥ |
dīpayeta tataḥ pāśāṃ huṃphaḍantaḥ krameṇa tu || 9 ||

ekaikamāhutiṃ datvā pāśe pāśe na saṃśayaḥ |
prabhāte vimale mantrī pradīptehani suvṛte || 10 ||

saṃhārakramayogena hunettatvānyathākramam |
homānte tu tato mantrī pāśacchedantu kārayet || 11 ||

huṃphaḍantaśivenaiva chitvā tenaiva homayet |

p. 938)

hutvā pāśāṃ tato mantrī pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet || 12 ||

pūrṇāhuti krameṇaiva sarvatatvān samuddharet |
tatsthānāttataḥ pāśān tatvaṃ tatvena sandhayet || 13 ||

śuddhatatvaṃ bhaveddhrasvamaśuddhadīrghasaṃyutam |
vilīnamānaṃ saṃcintya sandhītakaraṇānvitam || 14 ||

evaṃ krameṇa tān sarvān hunetsandhena suvrate |
pṛthivīmāditaḥ kṛtvā śivatatvamapaścimam || 15 ||

eṣa eva vidhiḥ proktaḥ pāśānāṃ chedanaṃ prati |
tatvānāṃ sandhanañcaiva eṣa eva vidhismṛtaḥ || 16 ||

bhuvanādhvakalādhve ca tatvādhve ca varānane |

p. 939)

varṇādhve ca padādhve ca mantrādhve ca tathaiva hi || 17 ||

yaśśivaḥ kalpitastatra tena pāśāni chedayet |
bhuvanādhve tataḥ kuryāt kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 18 ||

tatvaṃ saṃsthāpayetpūrvaṃ tato bhuvanajalpanam |
gandhairdhūpaiḥ samabhyarcya tato yonivikalpanam || 19 ||

gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya tāḍanañca paśosmṛtaḥ |
śikhayā tāḍanaṃ kṛtvā grāhyasaṃhāramudrayā || 20 ||

śaktyā saṃtāḍite puṃsi tata ūrdhvaṃ prayātyasau |
dvādaśāntamanuprāptaṃ tato grāhyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 21 ||

vāmayā yojayenmantrī tena dadyāttu āhutī |

p. 940)

adhikāre daśā dadyāt bhoge dadyāddaśāhutiḥ || 22 ||

laye dadyāddaśa prājñaḥ tatastatvādviyojayet |
viyojya mantravit prājñaḥ pāśacchedantu kārayet || 23 ||

viyojanantu jyeṣṭhyāyāṃ śivaḥ pāśannikṛntati |
pūrṇāhutiṃ tatonte tu raudraṃ tasmāttu uddharet || 24 ||

eṣa eva vidhiḥ proktaḥ sarvādhvānamaśeṣataḥ |
vidhimevaṃ sakṛt jñātvā mocayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 25 ||

tatvādhvāne tu mantrajñaḥ kathayāmi tavākhilam |
tatvamāvāhayeddevi tato homaṃ prakalpayet || 26 ||

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya tāḍanantu tataḥ punaḥ |

p. 941)

śivena yojayettatra hunettenaiva suvṛte || 27 ||

* * *? śataṃ śatañca pūrṇāhutyāṃ prati prati |
tritatvādhvaraśuddhe ca śikhācchedantu kārayet || 28 ||

tatvātmasthaṃ paśuṃ kṛtvā yojayet parame pade |
yojite tu pare tatve staumyamāhutayo hunet || 29 ||

oṃ sarvajñatāyai ca paripūrṇeti ca param |
paritṛpto bhavasveti nirvāṇaṃ bhavasveti ca || 30 ||

yathā sarvaṃgatohyevamāhutiḥ pratipādayet |
sarvavyāpīti hotavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 31 ||

bhavetsarvajñatā devi sa paśuḥ kāraṇājñayā |

p. 942)

yaduktaṃ devadevena tadbhavennātra saṃśayaḥ || 32 ||

kalāstathaiva jñātavyāḥ padenaiva tathaiva ca |
varṇādhvāne tathā proktaṃ kartavyaṃ deśikena tu || 33 ||

varṇā eva smṛtāstatvā varṇayonistathaiva ca |
praṇave yojanañcaiva tāḍane praṇavasmṛtaḥ || 34 ||

grahaṇaṃ praṇavenaiva utkṣepaḥ praṇavena tu |
varṇādhvāne yathā proktaṃ padādhvāne tathaiva ca || 35 ||

kalādhvāne tathaiveha ācāryeṇa mahātmanā |
mantrādhvānaṃ pravakṣyāmi saṃkṣepānna tu vistarāt || 36 ||

gāyatrī tu bhavedyoniḥ tayā mantrānviśodhayet |

p. 943)

sāvitryā tāḍanaṃ kuryāt grāhyaṃ tenaiva mantrataḥ || 37 ||

yojanaṃ tena kartavyaṃ tathā caiva viyojanam |
utkarṣaṇantu tenaiva sāvitryā tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 38 ||

pāśacchedaṃ śivenaiva kartavyaṃ deśikena tu |
pūrṇāhutiḥ pradātavyā yojanāya pare śive || 39 ||

vasordhārāprayogena pātayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ |
eṣa eva vidhirdevi kathito hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 40 ||

jñātavyaṃ deśikenaiva yojanāya pare śive |
sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 41 ||

dīkṣā pañcavidhā devi kartavyā deśikena tu |

p. 944)

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ hitāvaham || 42 ||

āsanaṃ praṇavenaiva kartavyaṃ deśikena tu |
anantadharmajñānañca vairāgyaiśvaryameva ca || 43 ||

navadhā śaktidevena praṇavaṃ vinyasedbudhaḥ |
padmañca candanaṃ tena karṇikāntena kārayet || 44 ||

puṣkarāntena kartavyā maṇḍalāntena kārayet |
devatāntena kartavyā mūrtyantenaiva kārayet || 45 ||

pañcabrahmā tu tenaiva aṣṭatriṃśatkalairyutaḥ |
āvāhanantu tenaiva kartavyaṃ surasundari || 46 ||

brahmaṇastu na sandeho deśikena mahātmanā |

p. 945)

nivṛttistu śirastasya iḍikāhṛdayaṃ viduḥ || 48 ||

sūkṣmāṃ śikhāntu vijñeyā sadyastu kavacaṃ bhavet |
pṛthivī tu bhavedastraṃ brahmaṇāṅgāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 49 ||

pūrveṇa praṇavaṃ dadyāt dakṣiṇena tathaiva ca |
paścimenottareṇaiva madhye praṇavasaṃyutam || 50 ||

āgneyyāṃ hṛdayañcaiva īśānyāntu śirasmṛtam |
śikhā naiṛtadigbhāge kavacaṃ vāyugocare || 51 ||

rudroṅkāreṇa kartavyaṃ rudratatvasya śobhane |
ikārastu śikhā devi akārastu śirasmṛtam || 52 ||

gakāro hṛdayañcaiva sadyastu kavacaṃ viduḥ |

p. 946)

lakāramastramityuktaṃ grāhakāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 53 ||

hantākhya bhedayettaistu grāhakaissurasundari |
bhūtatvaṃ brahmatatvasya praṇavena tu homayet || 54 ||

evaṃ hutvā vidhānena pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet |
yojane sādhakendrāya prajanatvamapāśrite || 55 ||

tasmin yuktaṃ tato mantrī brahmasāyujyatāṃ vrajet |
brahmaṇe tu yathā proktaṃ viṣṇave'pi tathā smṛtam || 56 ||

pratiṣṭhā tu śivasyaiva ākāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
rukāro hṛdayaṃ proktamīkārastu śikhā smṛtā || 57 ||

vāmantu kavacaṃ proktaṃ vakārostraṃ prakīrtitam |

p. 947)

pūrvoktena vidhānena kuryādāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ || 58 ||

sāyujyaṃ tādṛśaṃ proktaṃ śivena paramātmanā |
anādye tu pade devi viṣṇusāyujyatāṃ vrajet || 59 ||

tasminyuktamayā devi viṣṇutulyaparākramaḥ |
vidyāśirassamākhyātamaṅgāraṃ nāma nāmataḥ || 60 ||

ṛkāraṃ hṛdayaṃ tasya kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
aghoraṃ kavacaṃ devi rudratatvasya śobhane || 61 ||

brahmaṇe viṣṇave yadvat tadvatkuryāttu deśikaḥ |
āsane'yaṃ vidhirdevi mayā te parikīrtitāḥ || 62 ||

amṛtā tu śikhā tasya ikāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |

p. 948)

vidyāśirassamākhyātaṃ makāraṃ nāma nāmataḥ || 63 ||

ākāraṃ hṛdayaṃ tasya puruṣaṃ kavacaṃ viduḥ |
yakāramastramityuktamaṅgānyetāni ceśvari || 64 ||

yo vidhī rudratatvasya īśvare'pi tathā smṛtāḥ |
anantā rudratatvasya nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ padam || 65 ||

īśvare vyomarūpā tu kathitā tava śobhane |
nidhanena tu kartavyaṃ tatvena tu sadāśive || 66 ||

yathā pūrvaṃ mayākhyātamatrāpi vidhireva saḥ |
paramākṣaraṃ śikhā tasya śāntyatītaṃ śirasmṛtam || 67 ||

bindudevantu hṛdayaṃ īśānaṃ kavacaṃ viduḥ |

p. 949)

śūnyatatvaṃ hakārastu astraṃ tatparikīrtitam || 68 ||

sāyujyaṃ pūrvamevoktaṃ yathā kuryāttu nānyathā |
ete pañcamahāyāgā nākhyātā kasyacinmayā || 69 ||

naiṣṭhikā brahmacaryeṣu sthitā bhuvi ca mānavāḥ |
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya mayā tu kathitaṃ tava || 70 ||

liṅgoddhāravidhihyeṣa kathitastava suvrate |
naiṣṭhikānāntu kartavyaṃ liṅgoddhāraṃ samīritam || 71 ||

svajātīyapade sthāpya tato dīkṣāntu kārayet |
athavā brahmavidye tu abhiyuktastu sādhakaḥ || 72 ||

tatra dīkṣā tu kartavyā abhiṣekantu śobhane |

p. 950)

vaiṣṇave'pi tathaiveha kathitaṃ parameṣṭhinā || 73 ||

rudrarudragaṇānāntu sādhanaṃ parikīrtitam |
gaṇaguhyakamantreṣu sādhanenaiva sādhakaḥ || 74 ||

vināyakānāṃ sarveṣāṃ tatra dīkṣāntu kārayet |
ḍākinyo mātaraścaiva yoginyo'pi tathā parāḥ || 75 ||

asmiṃstu dīkṣitenaiva sādhakassa vidhīyate |
īśvare pāśupatyānāṃ uddhāre tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 76 ||

liṅgoddhāravidhānantu kartavyaṃ deśikena tu |
śuddhāyuktastu etasmin dīkṣitena muktimicchatā || 77 ||

sarvatatvaphalopetaṃ sarvatatvaphalodayam |

p. 951)

yajeddevādideveśaṃ sarveṣāṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 78 ||

eṣa sarvodayo nāma sarvamuktiphalapradaḥ |
eṣa te yoga ākhyātaḥ pañcavyomātmakaśśubhaḥ || 79 ||

śivayāgaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvayāgamanaupamam |
caturāvaraṇairyāgaṃ kartavyaṃ deśikena tu || 81 ||

pañcabrahma śivāṅgācca garbhametadudāhṛtam |
dvitīyāvaraṇe devi kalā śaktyātmakāni tu || 82 ||

diśāsu vinyaseddevi śaktestu vidiśāḥ kalāḥ |
nādasyekakalā yāstu diśāsu vidiśāsu ca || 83 ||

ya * * *? da kalā devi nindoścau tathaiva ca |
eṣa te yāga ākhyāto durlabhastridaśairapi || 84 ||

sarvasvaṃ gopitaṃ hyetat vyākhyātaṃ tava śobhane |

p. 952)

eṣa sarvādayo yāgāḥ kathitāstava śobhane || 85 ||

jñātvā budhvā ca yaṣṭvā ca mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
praṇavaṃ hyāsanaṃ kṛtvā mātṛkāṃ tu yajedbudhaḥ || 86 ||

muktintu praṇavaṃ kṛtvā yojayet susamāhitaḥ |
dakṣiṇe yojayedbījaṃ vāme yonintu vinyaset || 87 ||

sakalīkaraṇaṃ hyetat kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
avargaṃ pūrvapatre tu kavargañcāgnigocare || 88 ||

cavargaṃ dakṣiṇe caiva ṭavargaṃ naiṛtena tu |
tavargaṃ paścime caiva pavargaṃ vāyugocare || 89 ||

yavargamuttareṇaiva śavargantvīśagocare |

p. 953)

eṣa vai mātṛkāyāgaḥ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 90 ||

avargaṃ prathamaṃ tatvaṃ kavargaṃ puruṣaṃ smṛtam |
evaṃ vargakrameṇaiva śivatatvantu mātṛkān || 91 ||

oṅkāramātmatatvantu prathamaṃ parikīrtitam |
rudroṅkāreṇa deveśi vidyātatvantu kārayet || 92 ||

nidhanena tu hotavyaṃ śivatatvaṃ samāhitaiḥ |
evante mātṛkāyāgo durlabhastridaśairapi || 93 ||

kathitaṃ surahasyantu na mayā gopitaṃ tava |
iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare navamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
p. 954)

devyuvāca - 

dīkṣākāle samutpanne ācāryaiśśāstra tatparaiḥ |
kathaṃ tasmin pradeśe ca śiṣyāssthāpyā maheśvara || 1 ||

kasmiṃstu yatayasthāpyā brāhmaṇāśca maheśvara |
