kṣatriyāṇāntu yatsthānaṃ vaiśyānāṃ vada śaṅkara || 2 ||

śūdrāṇāṃ sthānamākhyāhi strīṇāñcaiva tathaiva hi |
grahamedhi yathā kāryo dīkṣākāle maheśvara || 3 ||

etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ brūhi me bhaktavatsala |
īśvaraḥ -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi niyogavidhimuttamam || 4 ||

yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvadoṣairna lipyate |

p. 955)

īśānyāṃ yatayasthāpyā viprāścottarataḥ sthitāḥ || 5 ||

śivāddakṣiṇataḥ sthāpyā kṣatravarṇasamudbhavāḥ |
vaiśyāḥ paścimadigbhāge śūdrā vai naiṛtena tu || 6 ||

śivā vāyavyadigbhāge saṃskāre yojayetstriyam |
evaṃ saṃsthāpya vidhivat kuryādālabhanakriyām || 7 ||

svadārāpatyabhṛtyātmā gṛhamedhī yadā bhavet |
tasya saṃskārayogo'yamatiruddhā samācaret || 8 ||

praṇavaṃ viniyuñjīyāt gurave gṛhamedhine |
gṛhasthaṃ śivavadbhāvaṃ tadbhāryāmagnikātanau || 9 ||

kumāraṃ tadapatyānāṃ suhṛdā mantranāyake |

p. 956)

dikpālaiḥ kulajān gṛhyāt śeṣānastragaṇānyaset || 10 ||

ekādhvarikasaṃskāramīhatāṃ gṛhamedhinām |
sadārāpatyapatyānāṃ niyogaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 11 ||

gṛhamedhīmaye yāge puṣpapāntaṃ visarjayet |
atraiva viniyuktasya striyāyānantu kārayet || 12 ||

svāmine patitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ striyasvapataye yadā |
mahādoṣamavāpnoti caryāyuḥ paripālyate || 13 ||

caryādoṣasamutpanne jāyate gurutalpagaḥ |
gurutalpasamaṃ pāpaṃ hṛṣṭasaṃskārapāpakṛt || 14 ||

prāpnoti narakaṃ śiṣyaḥ paryāyā duhitā kriyā |

p. 957)

puṣpalātacitānasya kriyayoḥ paripūraṇam || 15 ||

sarpiṣā homayedagnau śivena tu śatāhutim |
anena vidhinā devi doṣahīno bhavedguruḥ || 16 ||

yāga saṃpūrṇatāmeti hutaṃ mokṣaphalāvaham |
grahiṇāntu vidhāno'yaṃ kathitastava śobhane || 17 ||

yatīnāmāditaḥ kṛtvā mānyā śivasaṃhitā |
homaḥ kāryastu deveśi ācāryeṇa mahātmanā || 18 ||

mantrādhyayanayogyastu śivahastena jāyate |
śivamantrasya samayī mantratantrottaro bhavet || 19 ||

dīkṣāṃ prāpya mamāsidhyo mahāsiddhāruho bhavet |

p. 958)

tritatvaviniviṣṭastu mokṣayogyaḥ prajāyate || 20 ||

labdhānujño'bhiṣiktastu bindustho yogasiddhibhāk |
pratyayāni yathā samyak aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam || 21 ||

nādasthaḥ samavāpnoti tato bhayavivarjitaḥ |
viśokaḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā mṛtī bhavet || 22 ||

ya evaṃ vetti tatvena gurukarma viniścayam |
sa dīkṣāṃ kartumarheta anyathā pāpakṛt bhavet || 23 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā tu vinyāsaṃ śiṣyāṇāntu maheśvari |
agnisthānamupakramya karma nirvartayedguruḥ || 24 ||

agnikarmavidhānajño jihvālakṣaṇa tatvavit |

p. 959)

siddhāsiddhavibhāgajño varṇarūpahutāśane || 25 ||

agnistu devatāssarvā agnirūpo maheśvaraḥ |
tasya mantrāḥ parā mūrtiḥ ādyaṃ vai somasaṃbhavam || 26 ||

lakṣayetsaptajihvāstu hutāśanamukhaistathā |
saptajihvāvidhānajño homakarma samārabhet || 27 ||

jihvābhedanatatvajño juhuyādyo hutāśane |
yathā dāharate karma tatsarvaṃ saphalaṃ bhavet || 28 ||

hutvāsyāgniṃ vidhānena pūjyāhutya pratarpitam |
tuṣyate yajamānasya santuṣṭho vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ || 29 ||

aṅguṣṭhaparvavistīrṇā bahuhavyendhanocitā |

p. 960)

ekībhūtāstu tiṣṭhanti svalahavyendhanena tu || 30 ||

tatkuṇḍānapramāṇena mukhametaddhutāśane |
jvālāmālāsahasraistu pātālaṃ vadanaṃ smṛtam || 31 ||

agneḥ prabodhanaṃ kṛtvā jihvānāṃ lakṣayettadā |
devatārūpakañcaiva phalaṃ tasyaiva yādṛśam || 32 ||

pūrvapaścimato jihvā grahāstatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
homakāle prayatnena dve jihve parivarjayet || 33 ||

prathamā dharmajihvā tu nīlavarṇena saṃsmṛtā |
dvitīyā yakṣadaivatyā tāmrākārasamaprabhā || 34 ||

tṛtīyā somadevatyā śuklā sā cāmṛtodbhavā |

p. 961)

pañcamī viṣṇudevatyā atasīpuṣpasannibhā || 35 ||

ṣaṣṭhī ca sūryadevatyā śuklā raktā ca sā smṛtā |
saptamī bahudevatyā sarvarūpaistu dīpyate || 36 ||

prathamā niṣphalā jihvā dvitīyārthapradāyikā |
tṛtīyā praṇayetsarvāṃ jananaṃ sthāpya saṅgamān || 37 ||

yamajihvā jayaṃ gotraṃ pañcamī kulavardhinī |
ṣaṣṭhī tu sūryadevatyā ārogyaṃ sā prayacchati || 38 ||

saptamī dharmakāmārthān dadāti vidhipūjitāḥ |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena madhye homaṃ praśasyate || 39 ||

gṛhajihvā yamajihvā pūrvapaścimatasthitā |

962)

tasmātsarvaprayatnena dve jihve parivarjayet || 40 ||

agnivarṇā daśaproktāḥ karmasiddhimayāvahā |
ādityodayavarṇābhā snigdhavaiḍūryasannibhā || 41 ||

ghṛtavarṇanibhāścaiva lohavarṇāstathaiva ca |
taptāyasasavarṇābhā kusaṃbharasasannibhāḥ || 42 ||

haritālanibhāścaiva dhūmravarṇāstathaiva ca |
indrāyudhasavarṇābhā indugopakasannibhāḥ || 43 ||

bahuhavyendhanaiścaiva sa samiddhe hutāśane |
vidhūme lelihāne ca hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye || 44 ||

arciṣmān piṇḍitaśikhaḥ taptakāñcanasannibhaḥ |

p. 963)

snigdhaḥ pradakṣiṇāvartassarvakarmaphalapradaḥ || 45 ||

evaṃ vijñāya vidhivat guruṇā sādhakasya tu |
saṃskārayogaḥ kartavyo yathāvattannibodhaye || 46 ||

kṛtvā sūtramayān pāśān navātmenābhimantritān |
uccārya navatatvāni akāreṇa tu bheditān || 47 ||

mūrdhni kaṇṭhe ca hṛdaye nābhirūruḥ kaṭistathā |
jaṅghe gulphe tathā pāde sarvatra viniyojayet || 48 ||

bāhubhyāṃ hastayoścaiva sarvadehantu veṣṭayet |
navātmanāhutiśataṃ svāhākāreṇa pātayet || 49 ||

vidyate na tu deveśi paścācchūnyena homayet |

p. 964)

sānnidhyabandhanārthantu evaṃ bhavati śobhane || 50 ||

badhvā pāśaṃ yathā nyāyaṃ chedanañca tathocyate |
yojitavyaṃ śive paścāt putrako deśikena tu || 51 ||

śodhayitvā mahātmā tu pūrvoktavidhinā guruḥ |
garbhādhānāditaḥ kṛtvā catvāriṃśāṣṭakaṃ kramāt || 52 ||

saṃskāraṃ vidhivatkṛtvā mantraiḥ brahmodbhavaiḥ kramāt |
nikṣepoddhārayogādīn saṃskārāṃśca yathākramam || 53 ||

tataścchedantu pāśānāṃ anena vidhinā bhavet |
nakārasaṃpuṭaṃ devi pakārokārayojitaḥ || 54 ||

mastakaṃ bindunākrāntaṃ śiṣyadehe niyojayet |

p. 965)

śivena saha deveśi nātrakāryā vicāraṇā || 55 ||

atiguhyamidaṃ devi nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā |
eṣa sarvavidhānantu śreṣṭhasarvaguṇottamam || 56 ||

sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ kathitantu tavākhilam |
iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ |
devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi mātṛkādhvānamuttamam |
nissaṃśayakarañcaiva nirvāṇaphalasādhanam || 1 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

p. 966)

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilam |
tena vijñātamātreṇa prāpnoti paramaṃ padam || 2 ||

mātṛkāyāntu hotavyamadhvānantu yathākramam |
ṣakāraṃ śatarudre tu daśadhā parikalpayet || 3 ||

pañcāṣṭake śakārantu vakāro yoniraṣṭake |
yogāṣṭake yakārastu suśiveraṃ prabhusmṛtaḥ || 4 ||

paṅktitraye yakārastu makāraṃ pañcaviṃśake |
bhakārassaṃhitassādhye bakāraśśivasaṅkare || 5 ||

asādhye tu phakāro vai vakāro harirudrake |
daśaghāte nakārastu pañcaśiṣye ca ya smṛtaḥ || 6 ||

p. 967)

mahādeve dakārastu thakāro gopati smṛtaḥ |
śivabrahmapade tantu ṇakāro nandahemake || 7 ||

ḍhakārasyāddṛṣikule mahāmāyā dṛḍhaprabhuḥ |
vāgeśānte ṭhakārastu ṭakāraḥ pratipādhipaḥ || 8 ||

sādhusthāne tṛkārastu jhakāro vimale sthitaḥ |
dhyānāhāre jhakāro vai vijñeyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ || 9 ||

damane phastu vijñeyaṃ cakārañcaiva rudrake |
dhātāro vai dakāraśca ghakāraṃ bhasmamīśvare || 10 ||

praṇavāṣṭagakārantu mahavidyāṣṭake ca kham |
kakāro mūrtiraṣṭāsu oṣṭhānto rūpakaṃ viduḥ || 11 ||

p. 968)

śaktitraye binduyutaṃ dve vidye au tu kīrtitam |
oṅkāropyastramityuktamaiṅkāraṃ kavacaṃ viduḥ || 12 ||

e śikhā tu samākhyātā śiraṃ rūkāra ucyate |
hṛdayantu ḷ vijñeyaṃ ṛ sāvitrī nigadyate || 13 ||

gāyatrī tu ṛkārantu dharmādi ū prakīrtitam |
āsane u iti jñeyaṃ vijñeyaṃ ī ti karṇikā || 14 ||

ikāraḥ śaktisaṃsaktaḥ ākāraṃ pañcamīśvare |
sadāśivaṃ vijānīyādakāraḥ paramaśśivaḥ || 15 ||

pañcāśadakṣaraihyetaiḥ adhvānaṃ yastu vindati |
sa jñānāt sa śivassākṣāt sa yogī sa śivaṃ viduḥ || 16 ||

p. 969)

pañcapañcabhirāhutyairdaśabhirdaśabhistathā |
viṃśaviṃśatibhiścaiva pañcaviṃśatibhistathā || 17 ||

pañcāśacca śatañcaiva jñātvā śaktibalādbalam |
dīkṣākāryā tu mantrajñaḥ paśūnāñca yathecchayā || 18 ||

niṣkale caturāhutyā śūnye caiva trayaṃ punaḥ |
praśāntena dvayaṃ dadyāt tattu ekāhutiṃ hunet || 19 ||

sarvādhvānamaśeṣasya eṣa eva vidhismṛtaḥ |
etadrahasyaṃ paramaṃ gopitaṃ na prakāśitam || 20 ||

dīkṣitāya praśāntāya abhiṣiktāya dhīmate |
gurupriyāya dātavyaṃ śivabhaktiratāya ca || 21 ||

p. 970)

anyathā naiva dātavyaṃ śivabhaktiratāya ca |
anyathā naiva dātavyaṃ dīkṣājñānamidaṃ śubham || 22 ||

sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ sarvatantreṣu cottamam |
viditvā jñānavijñānaṃ mocayenmucyateti ca || 23 ||

vistaraṃ kathitaṃ yattu sakalena tu homayet |
varṇamantrapadenaiva kalayā ca caturvidham || 24 ||

bhuvanādhvā kalādhvā ca tatvādhvā ca varānane |
jñānādhvā ca padādhvā ca varṇādhvā ca yathā punaḥ || 25 ||

ṣaṭ prakāramidaṃ devi adhvānaṃ parikīrtitam |
eṣāmekadaśaṃ śodhyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 26 ||

p. 971)

pūrṇāhutistu dātavyā yojanāya pare śive |
vasordhāraprayogena dātavyaṃ deśikena tu || 27 ||

mantradravyaprayogena yujyate paśu tatpade |
tasmānmantrañca tatvañca mahimānañca śobhane || 28 ||

devatānāṃ parityāgaṃ svaśarīre yathākramam |
bindunādalayañcaiva layātītaṃ paraṃ padam || 29 ||

evaṃ krameṇa deveśi yojayetparamaṃ padam |
ataḥ saṃkṣepato devi kathayāmi tavākhilam || 30 ||

na rajo nādhivāsañca na bhūkṣetraparigrahaḥ |
yatra yatra pradeśe tu pūjayitvā maheśvaram || 31 ||

p. 972)

adhvānaṃ manasā dhyātvā ācāryastantrapāragaḥ |
bhuvanādhvā tu yaḥ prokto mayā tu tava suvṛte || 32 ||

taṃ dhyātvāhyātmadehe tu paśudeśe tathaiva ca |
homayettantravitprājñaḥ tatvamārgavicakṣaṇaḥ || 33 ||

āhutīnāṃ sahasrantu amanaskena deśikaḥ |
sahasreṇa tu yudhyeta amanaskena śobhane || 34 ||

bhuvanādhvaiṣa vikhyātaḥ śivena paramātmanā |
sruvānupūraṇaṃ yāvattāvatkālaṃ samādiśet || 35 ||

amanaskena saṃyukto deśikaḥ parameśvari |
sarvamantramayaḥ proktaḥ sarvatatvamayastathā || 36 ||

p. 973)

sarvadevamayaścaiva sarvabhūtamayassadā |
etaṃ hutvā tu pātavyaṃ pūrṇāhutyā tu śobhane || 37 ||

ante tu yojanaṃ kṛtvā śivasāyujyamicchatā |
evaṃ hutvā mahādevi labhate śāśvataṃ śivam || 38 ||

sarvadīkṣāmayaṃ sāraṃ śobhanaṃ gopitaṃ mayā |
anyāyā kathitā dīkṣā ekāhutyā tu śudhyati || 39 ||

ekāhutyāpi deveśi niyujyetparame pade |
kiṃ punarbahudhā hutyā śudhyateti kimadbhutam || 40 ||

āhutīnāṃ sahasreṇa śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ata evaṃ punarvakṣye saṃkṣepaṃ śṛṇu suvrate || 41 ||

p. 974)

paramākṣareṇa hotavyaṃ sarvādhvānamaśeṣataḥ |
pūrṇāhutiśatenaiva śudhyate ca śivādhvare || 42 ||

evaṃ vidhena deveśi dīkṣā nirvāṇagāminī |
niṣkalena mayā khyātaṃ śūnyenaiva tathā punaḥ || 43 ||

praśāntena samākhyātaṃ tatvenaiva tathaiva ca |
akathyeyamidaṃ śuddhaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 44 ||

evaṃ vai yo na jānāti na hi mocayate paśum |
na cāsau deśikaḥ prokto na ca yojyaḥ śivādhvare || 45 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare śivayāgapaṭalaḥ ekādaśaḥ |
p. 975)

īśvaraḥ - 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sakalaṃ parameśvari |
yasmin pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sa carācaram || 1 ||

siddhayo vividhāścaiva yasmin devi pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
sa jñeyassakalo devo varṇarūpadharaḥ prabhuḥ || 2 ||

sitaraktañca kṛṣṇañca kapilaṃ nālameva ca |
pītasvacchakapotañca śabalaṃ karburaṃ tathā || 3 ||

mūrdhā catuḥ pañcavaktraṃ daśabāhuṃ trilocanam |
sarvāyudhadharaṃ śaṃbhuṃ jaṭābhāravibhūṣitam || 4 ||

padmāsanasthaṃ deveśaṃ dhyāyetsakalavigraham |
sarvasiddhikaraṃ devaṃ sādhakānāṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 5 ||

p. 976)

uttiṣṭhe cātarvāme ca pādalepāñjane tathā |
gulikākāśagamane dantakāṣṭhaparitrake || 6 ||

sarvasiddhikaraṃ devaṃ manasā yadyadīpsitam |
sarvaṃ saṃpadyate samyak japataḥ sakalaṃ priye || 7 ||

dhyāyataḥ sakalaṃ devaṃ tasya siddhirna dūrataḥ |
pūjayetsakalaṃ devaṃ japetsakalaniṣkalam || 8 ||

dhyāyejjyotirmayaṃ sūkṣmaṃ kalābhinnantu tarpayet |
pūjayecchakalaṃ devaṃ śiraḥ pāṇyādibhiryutam || 9 ||

pañcamūrdhni tu saṃyuktaṃ pūjākāle sadā smaret |
japakāle sadā devaṃ kuryācchakalaniṣkalam || 10 ||

p. 977)

sakalaṃ mantrarūpantu kalārūpantu niṣkalam |
dhyāyejjyotirmayaṃ sūkṣmaṃ raktaṃ pītaṃ sitāsitam || 11 ||

evaṃ dhyānaparo bhūtvā yogasiddhimavāpnuyāt |
tarpaṇe sarvamantrāṇāṃ svāhākārasamanvitam || 12 ||

vauṣaṭkāravaṣaṭkāra suvauṣaṭkārameva ca |
huṃkāraḥ phaṭkṛtaścaiva dvirabhyastaṃ bhavediva || 13 ||

suvaṣaṭsvāhā namaścaiva jñātvā karmaṇi yojayet |
svāhākāre yadābhinno pratiṣṭhā nāma sā kalā || 14 ||

hutvā mātranivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhādi sabheditā |
vidyābindusamāyuktā śāntistu sa visargataḥ || 15 ||

p. 978)

tarpaṇaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya śivena kathitaṃ purā || 16 ||

viparītā kalā yā tu maraṇoccāṭane smṛtā |
uccāṭane ca vidveṣastaṃbhane ca prayojayet || 17 ||

sakalaṃ devadeveśi kathitantu samāsataḥ |
pañcatatvamayaṃ devaṃ sarvatatvamayaṃ tathā || 18 ||

sarvabhūtamayañcaiva sarvataḥ paryavasthitam |
yasmin yasmiṃśca deveśi jñāyate su parisphuṭam || 19 ||

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
saptatatvaparaṃ devi tatvamekādaśa prabhum || 20 ||

ṣoḍaśāvayavamātmānaṃ ekaviṃśātmakaṃ prabhum |

p. 979)

ṣaṭtriṃśātmā tu vijñeyaṃ kalātatvaṃ sa eva tu || 21 ||

mātṛkātmā tu vijñeyamekāśīti padātmakam |
mantrātmā tu sa vijñeyo bhuvanādhvā sa eva tu || 22 ||

pañcaviṃśaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sāṃkhyānāṃ parameśvara |
ṣaṭtriṃśakassavijñeyaḥ īśvaraḥ parameśvaraḥ || 23 ||

vedāntaviduṣaiścaiva gīyate parameśvaraḥ |
pañcarātraṃ tu tatvajñaiḥ sa eva parikīrtitaḥ || 24 ||

vaimalākārakaścaiva tathā pāśupatāśca ye |
bauddhaścārhatāścaiva nyāyavaiśeṣikāstathā || 25 ||

itihāsapurāṇajñā dharmaśāstraparāyaṇāḥ |

p. 980)

sarve mokṣavido devi svasaṃjñāpratipāditāḥ || 26 |

sevante devadeveśaṃ vedavedāṅgapāragāḥ |
sa eva kathito haṃsaḥ sakalaḥ parameśvari || 27 ||

tiṣṭhate devadeveśi sakalaḥ kalasaṃyutaḥ |
śaktinādārdhacandraśca bindudevastathaiva ca || 28 ||

akāraśca ukāraśca varṇassakalasmṛtaḥ *? |
eṣa dehe samākhyātaḥ sakalasya vinirṇayaḥ || 29 ||

athavācānyabhedena sakalaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
kathayeddevadevasya tannibodha samāsataḥ || 30 ||

vikāraḥ prathamaḥ prokta akārastadvitīyakaḥ |

p. 981)

ukārastu tṛtīyastu makārastu caturthakam || 31 ||

pañcamo bindunādastu īkāraḥ ṣaṣṭha ucyate |
ebhirvaṇairyadā hīnaṃ niṣkalaṃ parameśvaram || 32 ||

akāreṇa bhakāreṇa ikāreṇa tu śobhane |
ta ete saṃsthitaikastho hukāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 33 ||

tuṣṭā yā tu yadā yukto makāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
yadā bindugato śaṃbhuḥ nāsikāyāṃ pravartate || 34 ||

kakārāntaṃ vijānīyāt tirodhayati yoginām |
nābhivarṇairyadā hīnaṃ tadā śūnyaṃ vinirdiśet || 35 ||

ākāraśca hakāraśca īkārassaha saṃyutaḥ |

p. 982)

akāraḥ kathito devi bindunādapare sthitaḥ || 36 ||

tasyāpi paratareṇaiva śaktistu parikīrtitā |
śaktyātītaṃ yadā devi kiñciccheṣamavasthitam || 37 ||

vyāpinī sā vinirdiṣṭā sārṇo yadvatpipīlikā |
sakalānugraheṇaiva kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 38 ||

sakalāllabhate sarvaṃ yatkiñcidbhuvi durlabham |
eṣa devi samākhyāto dehe yasmātpratiṣṭhitā || 39 ||

sa yogena samāyuktaḥ uccāraḥ sa tu kīrtitaḥ |
dehayuktastu deveśe sakalaḥ paripaṭhyate || 40 ||

brahmāviṣṇustathārudra īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |

p. 983)

īkārasahasaṃyuktaṃ dehaṃ tasya varānane || 41 ||

etattyakto yadātmā tu niṣkalassa tu paṭhyate |
kathitaṃ pūrvasūtre tu tattvayā viditaṃ purā || 42 ||

svayaṃ niṣkramate devo dehaṃ tyaktvā sa mārutaḥ |
niṣkalassa samākhyāto veditavyaḥ suśobhane || 43 ||

pañcadeva vinirmukta ātmānaṃ yattu vindati |
ātmajñānena saṃyukto gacchate sa sadāśive || 44 ||

ūrdhvagāmī kalāhyekā kalāśakti catuṣṭayam |
vyāpinyā tu yadāyuktaṃ ṣaḍvarṇarahitaśśivaḥ || 45 ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ vidyutkoṭisamaprabham |

p. 984)

asaṃkhyānañca sūryāṇāṃ uditoditasuprabham || 46 ||

ūrdhvagāmī tadetaddhi kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
tatsthāne tu yadāhyātmā tadrūpaḥ pratibhāti saḥ || 47 ||

sūkṣmā nāmakalā yā tu śuddhasphaṭikanirmalā |
tasyāṃ madhye tadāhyātmā tadrūpaḥ pratibhāti saḥ || 48 ||

amṛtā nāma kalā yā tu anekakiraṇaprabhā |
tasmiṃsthāne tadāhyātmā tadrūpapratimo bhavet || 49 ||

amṛtāmṛteti yā proktā tasyā rūpaṃ yathā śṛṇu |
candrātapapratīkāśā amṛtaṃ sravate sadā || 50 ||

amṛtāmṛteti sā proktā kalāśakticaturthikā |

p. 985)

tatsthāne tu tadāhyātmā tadrūpaḥ pratibhāti saḥ || 51 ||

etat ṣaḍvarṇamuddiṣṭaṃ mayā te varavarṇinī |
ebhirvarṇairyadāhīnaṃ ṣaḍvarṇarahitaśśivaḥ || 52 ||

evaṃ jñātvā varārohe deśikaḥ pāśahā bhavet |
ṣaṭpadārthamidaṃ jñānaṃ kathitaṃ parameṣṭhinā || 53 ||

etatsthānavaraṃ divyaṃ jñātvāhyātmā prakīrtitaḥ |
yena yena ca te tattat bhāvābhāvamavāpnuyāt || 54 ||

sphaṭikopalavaddevi ātmā tu parikīrtitaḥ |
yadyadrūpeṇa saṃyujyet tadrūpaṃ pratibhāti ca || 55 ||

rūpavarṇavinirmuktaḥ karaṇendriyavarjitaḥ |

p. 986)

tasmātkaraṇasaṃyukto badhyate mucyateti ca || 56 ||

budhyamāno paro bhūtvā jñāna yajñaṃ sa vindati |
jñānena tallayaṃ jñeyaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvagato bhavet || 57 ||

jñānajñeyaḥ sadāhyātmā jñātvā mucyeta mocayet |
jñātvā hyātmā vinirdiṣṭo jñānajñeyaṃ sa jānate || 58 ||

viditvā mucyate jantuḥ saṃsārabhavabandhanāt |
evante jñānamākhyātaṃ ṣaḍvarṇaṃ parameśvari || 59 ||

eteṣāṃ parato devi arūpākhyo nirāmayaḥ |
na tasya śakyate vaktuṃ pāraṃparyeṇa saṃsthitam || 60 ||

taṃ viditvā mahādevi dīkṣakassa gururbhavet |

p. 987)

jñānavijñānavān so hi mocakassa gurussadā || 61 ||

sa cānugrahakartā ca pāśahā deśikottamaḥ |
brahmādayasmṛtāste tu ye rūpāḥ kathitā mayā || 62 ||

vyāpinī tu parāśaktiḥ sarvatatvaprabodhikā |
etaddevi mayā khyātaṃ tatvaṃ paramadurlabham || 63 ||

tvayā guhyataraṃ kāryaṃ na dadyādyasya kasyacit |
sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva tathā sakalaniṣkalam || 64 ||

brahmāviṣṇustathā rudrastribhistu sakalasmṛtaḥ |
bindudevaṃ paraṃ tatvaṃ jñeyaṃ sakalaniṣkalam || 65 ||

sadāśivaṃ mahātmānaṃ vijñeyaṃ niṣkalaṃ śivam |

p. 988)

ātmatatve trayo devā vidyātatve tathaiva ca || 66 ||

śivatatve'thavā devi vijñeyastu sadāśivaḥ |
śaktistu paramā devi tritatvaparamā sthitā || 67 ||

sarvatatvapadātītaḥ tatvānāṃ yo'vyayaḥ paraḥ |
tasmāt sarvamidaṃ devi tato sarvamidaṃ tatam || 68 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare sakalavicāro nāma dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ |
devyuvāca- 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi sakalasya vinirṇayam |
vyāptirbahuvidhā proktā samayā nāvadhāritā || 1 ||

p. 989)

pañcadevamayaṃ devaṃ kathaṃ nu sakalaḥ prabhuḥ |
sarvatatvamayañcaiva sarvabhūtamayaṃ katham || 2 ||

saptatatvadharaṃ devaṃ tatvamekādaśātmakam |
ṣoḍaśāvayavātmānaṃ ekaviṃśātmakaṃ katham || 3 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśātmakamevoktaṃ kalātatvassa eva tu |
mātṛkātmeti yatproktaṃ ekāśīti padātmakam || 4 ||

mantrātmā sarvamantrāṇāṃ bhuvanādhvā sa eva tu |
pañcaviṃśasavijñeyaḥ ṣaḍviṃśakastathaiva ca || 5 ||

vedāntavādino devastvameva parameśvaram |
pāñcarātrikatatvajñaḥ sa eva paramaṃ padam || 6 ||

p. 990)

vaimalākārakāścaiva tathā pāśupatāśca ye |
sarveṣu saṃsthitaṃ devaṃ sakalaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 7 ||

bauddhāścaivārhatāścaiva nyāyavaiśeṣikāstathā |
itihāsapurāṇajñā dharmaśāstraratāśca ye || 8 ||

sarvamokṣavido ye tu vedavedāṅgapāragāḥ |
sevante devadeveśaṃ sakalaṃ parameśvaram || 9 ||

etadeva tvayā proktaṃ na mayā viditaḥ prabho |
sarvametadaśeṣantu vistareṇa bravīhi me || 10 ||

svakīyāntatra yuktyā tu brūhi deva savistaram |

īśvaraḥ - 

p. 991)

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sakalasya vinirṇayam || 11 ||

anukrameṇa vakṣyāmi coditastu yathā tvayā |
pañcadevamayaṃ devi brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 12 ||

īśvarañca tathā proktaṃ tathā devaṃ sadāśivam |
pañcadevā smṛtā hyete sarvataḥ paryavasthitāḥ || 13 ||

akāraṃ brahmadevatyaṃ makāraṃ rudradevatam |
ūkāraṃ viṣṇudevatyaṃ bindudevaṃ tatheśvaram || 14 ||

nādarūpī tu yattasya vijñeyantu sadāśivam |
sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva kalāḍhyaṃ śūnyameva ca || 15 ||

khamalaṃ kṣapaṇañcaiva kṣayaṃ kaṇṭhoṣṭhyameva ca |

p. 992)

ete bhūtāssamākhyātāḥ karaṇaiḥ sahasaṃyutāḥ || 16 ||

vikārāṣṣoḍaśa proktāḥ saptaprakṛti sūkṣmagāḥ |
guṇatrayaṃ tathā devi bhūtātmakamidaṃ smṛtam || 17 ||

bhūtāḥ prakāśamāyānti tena bhūtātmakā smṛtāḥ |
bhūtaiḥ parivṛtā yasmin karaṇāśeṣaśobhane || 18 ||

etadbhūtātmakaṃ sarvaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
vidyāceśvaramāyā ca kālo niyatireva ca || 19 ||

puruṣaḥ prakṛtiścaiva saptaite kathitāni tu |
īśvarastu śiraścaiva vidyādakṣiṇarekhiṇī || 20 ||

māyāvāmena vijñeyā trilokā ca saṃsthitāḥ |

p. 993)

niyatiḥ kālo'dhastāttu boddhavyamanupūrvaśaḥ || 21 ||

puruṣaṃ vaktradeśe tu tasyādhaḥ prakṛtisthitā |
saptatatvadharaṃ devameṣa te samudāhṛtam || 22 ||

tatvamekādaśātmeti yaduktaṃ hi mayā purā |
śṛṇuṣvāvahitā devi kathayāmi tavākhilam || 23 ||

saptatatvopariṣṭhāttu jñeyaṃ tatvaṃ sadāśivam |
upariṣṭācchivaṃ tatva mūrdhvaśaktipratiṣṭhitam || 24 ||

vyāpinī tu parā tasya tatvamekādaśātmakam |
ṣoḍaśāvayavātmānaṃ yaduktaṃ hi mayā purā || 25 ||

kakhākhyā ṣoḍaśā devi jñātavyā deśikena tu |

p. 994)

tṛṭirlavaṃ nimeṣastu kalākāṣṭhā muhūrtatā || 26 ||

rātryahaḥ pakṣamāsartu ayanaṃ vatsaraṃ tathā |
yugaṃ manvantaraṃ kalpaṃ mahākalpastathaiva ca || 27 ||

ṣoḍaśāvayavaṃ hyetat kālassaptadaśa smṛtaḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśātmeti yatproktaṃ śṛṇu tasya vadāmyaham || 28 ||

pṛthivīmāditaḥ kṛtvā pradhānāntaṃ tu suvrate |
caturviṃśati tatvaṃ hi puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśakam || 29 ||

ṣaḍviṃśakastu niyatiḥ kālaḥ saptādhiko bhavet |
aṣṭāviṃśa smṛtaṃ rāgaṃ vidyā jñeyā tathā parā || 30 ||

ūnattriṃśastu vijñeyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā |

p. 995)

triṃśatastu kalātatvaṃ kathitaṃ hi śivena tu || 31 ||

ekatriṃśadvarārohe māyātatvaṃ prakīrtitam |
dvātriṃśakasmṛtā vidyā trayastriṃśādhikeśvaram || 32 ||

sadāśivaṃ catustriṃśat pañcatriṃśacchivāsmṛtā |
śivaṣṣaṭtriṃśamākhyātā śivena paramātmanā || 33 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśātmā samākhyāto yathāvattava śobhane |
kalā caturthī sā jñeyā deśikena mahātmanā || 34 ||

prathamaṃ pañcabrahmāṇamaṣṭatriṃśatkramāt smṛtam |
sadyā kalābhiraṣṭābhiḥ vāmadevastrayodaśam || 35 ||

aghoramabhicāṣṭābhiḥ caturbhirvadanaṃ smṛtam |

p. 996)

īśānaṃ pañcadhā devi tasya dehādvinissṛtāḥ || 36 ||

brahmādīn pañcadhā **? pañcabrahmā prakīrtitāḥ |
bindudeve ca catvāraḥ kalā devi vadāmyaham || 37 ||

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyāśāntistathaiva ca |
kalāhyetāstu catvāraḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ || 38 ||

nādasya yāḥ kalā proktā śaktyāyāśca maheśvari |
sūkṣmāsūkṣmatarairbhāvaiḥ sakalaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 39 ||

kalātatvaṃ sacaiveha kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
mātṛkātmeti yatproktaṃ mayā te surasundari || 40 ||

akārādikṣakārāntā mātṛkā parikīrtitāḥ |

p. 997)

sa kāmasya tu deveśi deho jñeyāssadā budhaiḥ || 41 ||

praṇave saṃsthitā tasmin jñātavyā tu varānane |
ekāśīti padātme tu yaduktaṃ parameśvari || 42 ||

navabheda vibhāgena kathitaṃ viditaṃ tvayā |
mantrātmā tu kriyā proktaḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ || 43 ||

kalā ṣoḍaśamityuktaṃ sarvatantreṣu gīyate |
tābhyo bindudvayamante tābhyāṃ te alikadvayam || 44 ||

bindudvayasya madhye tu ekaṃ madhye niyojayet |
dvitīyā bindvadhastāttu mantrātmā sa tu kīrtitaḥ || 45 ||

sa jīvaḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |

p. 998)

tena sarvamidaṃ devi trailokyaṃ sa carācaram || 46 ||

vyāpitaṃ parameśena nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
tiryaṅmṛganarākhyānāṃ sa jīvaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 47 ||

tena sarvamidaṃ vyāptaṃ payasīva yathā ghṛtam |
sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 48 ||

etat jñānaparaṃ devi avijñātaṃ surairapi |
saṃjñeyassarvamantreṣu sarvatantreṣu śobhane || 49 ||

ucchavaseddeva deveśe haṃsaḥ paramakāraṇam |
eṣa te jñānasadbhāvaṃ mantrasadbhāva eva ca || 50 ||

mantrasadbhāvamityuktaṃ jīvasadbhāvamuttamam |

p. 999)

gopitavyaṃ prayatnena jñānametatsudurlabham || 51 ||

aviditvā idaṃ devi jīvasadbhāvamuttamam |
naiva mantrāḥ prasidhyanti japtāḥ koṭiśatairapi || 52 ||

gīyate sarvalokeṣu jīvaṃ paramadurlabham |
subhāṣitamidaṃ vākyaṃ kalānārhanti ṣoḍaśī || 53 ||

yasmātparataraṃ devi jñānajñeyaṃ varānane |
sarvasvaṃ gopitaṃ jñātaṃ na khyātaṃ yasya kasyacit || 54 ||

deyaṃ dāntāya śāntāya gurubhaktaratāya ca |
sarvatantravidhijñāya sādhakāya ca dhīmate || 55 ||

abhiṣiktāya dātavyamācāryasya mahātmanaḥ |

p. 1000)

anyāyai gṛhyate ye tu nābhinandanti ye gurum || 56 ||

jñenaṃ jñeyaṃ viditvā tu prāyaścittīyate naraḥ |
tasmāt jñānaṃ guruñcaiva a * * * * *? vṛte || 57 ||

jñānavit tatvaviccaiva śivaścaiva varānane |
vibhaktiñcaiva kurvīta parameśena bhāṣitam || 58 ||

eṣa te kathitaṃ devi mantrātmā parameśvaraḥ |
viditvā sarvakarmāṇi kuru devi yathecchayā || 59 ||

sarvatantreṣu sāmānyaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
nātaḥ parataraṃ jñānaṃ satyaṃ devi vadāmyaham || 60 ||

bhuvanādhvā sa evoktaḥ sakalaḥ parameśvaraḥ |

p. 1001)

tatpravakṣyāmyahaṃ devi saṃkṣepānna tu vistarāt || 61 ||

pañcadhā pañcadevatvaṃ sakalaṃ paryavasthitam |
te mayā pūrvamākhyātaṃ yathā tu viditaṃ tvayā || 62 ||

anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā pradhānānte ca śobhane |
brahmā tatrādhipatyena saṃsthitaṃ parameśvari || 63 ||

pauruṣamāditaḥ kṛtvā kalātatvamapaścimam |
viṣṇustatvādhipatyena saṃsthitaṃ parameśvari || 64 ||

granthyā tu āditaḥ kṛtvā kalātatvamapaścimam |
rudrastatvādhipatyena saṃsthitaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 65 ||

vidyā tu āditaḥ kṛtvā yāvattatvaṃ sadāśivam |

p. 1002)

īśvara ādhipatyena saṃsthito nātra saṃśayaḥ || 66 ||

upariṣṭācchivaṃ tatvaṃ tatra devaṃ sadāśivam |
vyāpinaṃ pañcadhā devi bhuvanādhvamaśeṣataḥ || 67 ||

pañcaviṃśati tatproktaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ varānane |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tadekāgramanā śṛṇu || 68 ||

parisaṃkhyānupūrveṇa saṃkṣepātkathayāmyaham |
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca || 69 ||

karmendriyastathā pañcapañca buddhīndriyāṇi ca |
tanmātrāṇi tathā pañca katitaṃ tava śobhane || 70 ||

buddhirmanasvahaṅkāraṃ pradhānaṃ sahasaṃyutam |

p. 1003)

caturviṃśat smṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ kṣetrajñaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ || 71 ||

sa eṣa paramo devi kṣetrabhuk kāraṇaṃ smṛtam |
vyaktāvyaktavijñānāt jñātvā kucyati bandhanāt || 72 ||

vyaktā buddhissamākhyātā avyaktā prakṛtiḥ smṛtā |
jñasvabhāvo bhavedātmā puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśakam || 73 ||

turyarūpasvabhāvasthaṃ sa eṣa paramaṃ padam |
niṣkriyaṃ tatsvabhāvena akartā karmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 74 ||

kartā pradhānamityāhuḥ kṣayayuktaṃ guṇānvitam |
evaṃ vadanti deveśi ṣaṣṭitantrādhvavādinaḥ || 75 ||

mahadādikrameṇaiva bhūtādividhivācakaiḥ |

p. 1004)

viṣayendriyatanmātraiḥ guṇādividhivācakaiḥ || 76 ||

prakṛtissā tu vijñeyā bhedairnāmāṅgalakṣaṇaiḥ |
bhoktā tu puruṣaḥ proktaḥ cinmātro guṇavarjitaḥ || 77 ||

pañcaviṃśakamityuktaṃ sāṃkhyajñānārthavādibhiḥ |
niṣkalatvena manyante sarvajñaṃ sarvatomukham || 78 ||

nirguṇaṃ guṇasaṃyuktaṃ cintyate sāṃkhyavādibhiḥ |
ityetatpauruṣaṃ tatvaṃ sāṃkhyānāṃ paramaṃ padam || 79 ||

asya doṣaṃ śṛṇu devi svasiddhānte yathā bhavet | 
yairbaddhaḥ paśutānaiti ajñatvañca prajāyate || 80 ||

bhoktṛtvā nityatā caiva akartā niṣkrayastathā |

p. 1005)

arthitvaṃ pāratantrañca dharmāṣṣaṭ pauruṣāstvime || 81 ||

bhoktā yassa atṛptastu martyadharmā anityatā |
niṣkrayatvādudāsīno akartā ajñatā punaḥ || 82 ||

madhyasthatā parāśīti pāratantryadaridratā |
dharmāhyete smṛtāstasya mātṛkaiḥ pratipāditāḥ || 83 ||

sarvajñatā na ca tasya nirguṇatvaṃ na caiva hi |
niṣkalatvaṃ na tasyaiva śaktyāgrakalanā śrutaḥ || 84 ||

guṇaissupratibaddhastu muktistasya na śāśvati |
kiñcitkālaṃ bhavenmuktiḥ pradhānaguṇabandhanāt || 85 ||

svākayaṃ bhāvaṃ kalā tasya caitanyena samanvitā |

p. 1006)

rogamānandabhāvena vidyāhyarthavivekinī || 86 ||

trividhaṃ bandhanākhyātaṃ puruṣasya mahātmane |
māyātmakamidaṃ devi adhobhāgapravartakam || 87 ||

eṣa te devi ākhyātaḥ sāṃkhyajñānasvanirṇayam |
sakalaṃ vyāpayitvā tu saṃsthitaṃ tantu suvrate || 88 ||

ṣaḍviṃśeti yathā proktaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ hi suvṛte |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tadekāgramanāḥ śṛṇu || 89 ||

kalādhastātkarmakalā puruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
narāṇāṃ nayane sūkṣme yogī nārāyaṇeśvaraḥ || 90 ||

sā niṣṭhā vedavādīnāṃ yogināñca nigadyate |

p. 1007)

sa caiva paṭhyate taistu ṣaḍūrmirahitā tanuḥ || 91 ||

vadante naiva jānante anyārthena vadanti te |
asminneva śṛṇu devi ṣaḍūrmirahitaṃ yathā || 92 ||

brahmāviṣṇustathā rudro īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
śaktinā rahitaṃ devi ṣaḍūrmirahitaṃ śivam || 93 ||

mūlatatvaṃ samākhyātaṃ vijñānānandakārakam |
neti neti ca haṃsākhyaṃ paramaṃ brahmavācakam || 94 ||

idaṃ tattparamaṃ tatvamidaṃ tatparamaṃ padam |
idaṃ sā paramāmuktiḥ yā sā vedavido dhruvam || 95 ||

triprakāreṇa bhedena yogibhiḥ samudāhṛtam |

p. 1008)

ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānameva ca || 96 ||

evaṃ tridhā vadantyeva haṃkāraṃ tathāpare |
buddhimanye tathā devi pradhānaguṇagocaram || 97 ||

pañcaviṃśañca ṣaḍviṃśaṃ vedamanye tathā pare |
baddhā vadanti bahavaḥ ṛṣayassaṃśitavratāḥ || 98 ||

ekaṃ tatsakalaṃ devamupāsyaṃ vidivaukasaḥ |
ṛṣayo yakṣarakṣāṃsi piśācoragakinnarāḥ || 99 ||

gandharvāsuradaityāśca tathā vidyādharādayaḥ |
siddhāścaiva tathā devi ye cānye bhuvi mānavāḥ || 100 ||

hrasvaṃ dīrghaṃ kṛśaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmātsūkṣmataraṃ tathā |

p. 1009)

sthūlātsthūlataraṃ devaṃ kalpayanti manīṣiṇaḥ || 101 ||

arūpaṃ rūpavān devaṃ kalpayanti anekadhā |
haritaṃ kapilañcaiva mañjiṣṭhaḥ sagareva ca || 102 ||

babhruḥ piṅgaḥ kapotaśca dhūmraśśabalakaṃ tathā |
śyāmaṃ tadulamāntrantu āpabaddhe ca puṣkare || 103 ||

khadyotābhanibhañcaiva tārāmātraṃ tathaiva ca |
candrabiṃbanibhaṃ kecit ādityodayasannibham || 104 ||

indranīlanibhañcaiva padmarāganibhaṃ tathā |
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ deva sakalaṃ parameśvari || 105 ||

sakalaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ ādhāraṃ parikīrtitam |

p. 1010)

asmātprabhūyate sarvamasminneva layaṃ punaḥ || 106 ||

asmātsiddhiśca muktiśca saṃsthitā nātra saṃśayaḥ |
uttiṣṭhamāditaḥ kṛtvā vidveṣākarṣaṇaṃ tathā || 107 ||

karoti sādhakendrastu yastatvaṃ japate sadā |
kiṃ tasya bahubhirmantraiḥ jñātaistu varavarṇinī || 108 ||

cintāmaṇiriva paraṃ tatvaṃ sakalaṃ sarvatomukham |
yāge yāge jape home yathā sidhyanti yoginaḥ || 109 ||

yathā ca yajanaṃ kuryāt śṛṇu devi samāsataḥ |
yāgasthaṃ sakalaṃ devaṃ śiraḥ pāṇyādilakṣaṇam || 110 ||

tryakṣaṃ daśabhujaṃ devaṃ sarvāyudhadharaṃ tathā |

p. 1011)

evaṃ japaṃ tathā dhyāyan pūjayetparameśvaram || 111 ||

na rūpeṇa vinā siddhiḥ tasmādrūpaṃ vicintayet |
cintyamānasya deveśe sarvavighnakṣayo bhavet || 112 ||

nirvighnassidhyate mantrī kathitaṃ sarvato mayā |
yogakāle tathā devi dhyātavyaṃ sakalaṃ priye || 113 ||

pūrvamuccārayettatvaṃ sakalaṃ yatprakīrtitam |
bindusthaṃ śabdalīnantu jyotīrūpaṃ śikhānvitam || 114 ||

dhyānakāle tu yogībhiḥ dhyātavyaṃ sakalaṃ sadā |
japakāle japaṃ kuryāt evaṃ sakalaniṣkalam || 115 ||

namaskārasya ante tu jñeyaṃ bindudvayaṃ budhaḥ |

p. 1012)

bindudvayātparo devi sa vai niṣkalamucyate || 116 ||

homakāle yathā homaḥ kartavyaḥ sādhakena tu |
svāhākāra samāyuktaṃ homakāle tu homayet || 117 ||

pratiṣṭhā nāma sā jñeyā bindudevakalā priye |
tayā homavikalpastu kartavyantu sadā budhaiḥ || 118 ||

*? ne bindusamāyuktaṃ mantraṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
śāntyarthe vauṣaḍityevaṃ puṣṭyarthe namasuvauṣaṭ || 119 ||

amṛtīkaraṇe caiva vaṣaṭkāreṇa homayet |
māraṇe tāḍane caiva icchā devi vicakṣaṇam || 120 ||

phaṭ phaṭkāra samāyuktaṃ homaṃ kuryājjapaṃ tathā |

p. 1013)

evaṃ vijñāya kartavyaṃ japayogaṃ samādhinā || 121 ||

anyathā naiva sidhyeta kalpakoṭiśatairapi |
sakalaṃ japamānastu niṣkalaṃ japitaṃ bhavet || 122 ||

niṣkalaṃ japate yastu sakalasyopari sthitam |
acirāddevi siddhyeta parameśena bhāṣitam || 123 ||

naiva puṇyaṃ bhavettasya naiva pāpaṃ kathañcana |
kathitaṃ sa rahasyantu na mayā gopitaṃ tava || 124 ||

tvayāpi devadeveśi gopanīyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
anyāye pṛcchate yastu guruvutkrāntigocaram || 125 ||

tasya devi na dātavyā ājñaiṣā parameśvari |

p. 1014)

dharmaśīlavinītasya gurubhaktasya dhīmate || 126 ||

jñānametatpradātavyaṃ mamājñāparipālake |
tasmācchreyārthibhirnityaṃ pūjanīyo gurussadā || 127 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare sakalavicāro nāma trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
īśvaraḥ - 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi adhivāsanamuttamam |
tithinakṣatrayogāśca itarānukūlameva ca || 1 ||

tārā candrakalenaiva kuryādbhūmiparigraham |

p. 1015)

pūrveṇa brahmaṇe kuryāt kṣatriyasyottareṇa tu || 2 ||

vaiśyasya vasudhā pītā kṛṣṇā śūdrasya kīrtitā |
brāhmaṇe madhurāsvādā kaṣāyā kṣatriyasya tu || 3 ||

vaiśyasya ṣaḍḍūsā jñeyā śūdrasya kaṭukā smṛtā |
ājyagandhādvije bhūmīraktagandhā tu kṣatriye || 4 ||

dhānyagandhāviśe bhūmī śūdrasya kṣāragandhinī |
parīkṣya nipuṇo bhūmiṃ vārayogavidhānavit || 5 ||

pūjayitvā vidhānajño riktāṃ rāśigaṇaissaha |
horāddrekkāṇi aṃśāśca tārā caiva balābalam || 6 ||

p. 1016)

laukikaṃ dhārmikāścaiva kalajñānavicakṣaṇaḥ |
yadi syāllaukiko devi kālajñānaṃ na jānati || 7 ||

tadātvādhyātmikaṃ kālaṃ parīkṣeta vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tatassaṃpūjya deveśaṃ lakṣayecca kramādbudhaḥ || 8 ||

divyāntarikṣabhaumāni prañcāntarinitarāṇi ca |
lakṣayitvā vidhānena ācāryassusamāhitaḥ || 9 ||

śaṅghadundubhinirghoṣaiḥ vedaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ |
gītavādyaravodghoṣaṃ jayaśabdasamanvitam || 10 ||

siṃhanādaravañcaiva kumārījanakrīḍanam |
bhaumānyetāni śabdāni antarikṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 11 ||

p. 1017)

meghastanitanirghoṣaṃ meghasañjanamaṃbaram |
snigdhavidyullatāramyaṃ sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ śubham || 12 ||

praśāntāni tu gṛhṇīyāt apraśastāni varjayet |
kākavṛddhacalolūkaśvānamārjārakūjitam || 13 ||

uṣṭragardabhaśabdāni vānaraṃ ṛkṣasūkaram |
eteṣāñcaiva yaśśabda praśastantu śobhane || 14 ||

pūjati grahacakre tu saṅkramādgṛhya yatnataḥ |
khātayejjānumātrantu pāṃsumuddhṛtya pūrayet || 15 ||

pāṃsurabhyadhiko yatra sā bhūmiḥ sarvakāmadā |
jñātvā bhūmirvidhānajño vāstuyāgaṃ yajedbudhaḥ || 16 ||

p. 1018)

vidyāṅgāvaraṇaṃ yaṣṭvā hunedaṣṭaśataṃ kramāt |
vāstuyāga smṛto hyeṣaḥ tato bhūmyāṃ viśodhanam || 17 ||

śodhayitvā tato bhūmiṃ kīṭakeśāsthivarjitām |
secayetpañcagavyena tāṃ bhūmiṃ suparīkṣitām || 18 ||

praśastataramudbhūtaiḥ palāśāśvatthamudgaraiḥ |
koṣṭayet sudṛḍhāṃ bhūmiṃ nimnonnatavivarjitām || 19 ||

parvottarapluvā kāryā yāgabhūmissuśobhanā |
samaṃ sarvatra kartavyaṃ bhūmibhāgamidaṃ śubham || 20 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena paritaḥ parivārayet |
puruṣāyā na kartavyamiṣṭakaṃ mṛṇmayañca vā || 21 ||

p. 1019)

dvāramekantu kartavyaṃ sakavāṭārgalānvitam |
evaṃ kṛtvā tato devi viṣṇubhūmiparigraham || 22 ||

gaṇayāgaṃ yajedādau tato bhūmiparigraham |
pañcāṅgañca śivaṃ yaṣṭvā juhuyādaṣṭaśataṃ kramāt || 22 ||

gaṇayāge trikoṇantu maṇḍalaṃ padmalāñchitam |
pīṭhoṅkārasamāyuktaṃ pūjayeta gaṇeśvaram || 24 ||

vāstuyāge catuṣkoṇaṃ madhye paṅkajabhūṣitam |
bhūmyāṃ parigrahohyeṣa kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 25 ||

adhivāsanaṃ tataḥ kuryāt śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
saṃbhāraiḥ saṃbhṛto mantrī adhivāsanamārabhet || 26 ||

p. 1020)

aṣṭau tu kalaśān kuryāddiśāsu vidiśāsu ca |
evaṃ vinyasya yatnena śivākhyaṃ kalaśaṃ nyaset || 27 ||

hemajaṃ tāmrajaṃ vāpi ṛgmajaṃ vāpi kārayet |
athavā mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi kārayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ || 28 ||

akālamūlakalaśāścandanenopalepitam |
su sugandhaistathā gandhaiḥ kaṇṭhasūtravibhūṣitam || 29 ||

akṣataiḥ pañcagavyena dhūpairgandhaistathaiva ca |
āsanaṃ kalpayettatra īśānyādiśamāśritam || 30 ||

kalaśaṃ sa hiraṇyantu mūrtinā sahasaṃyutam |
pañcabrahmā nyasetpūrvaṃ aṣṭattriṃśatkalāṃ tataḥ || 31 ||

p. 1021)

pṛthivīmāditaḥ kṛtvā tatvāni parikalpayet |
evaṃ vinyasya tatvāni tritatvāṃstu tridhā nyaset || 32 ||

vidyāṅgāni tato dadyānnetrādi kṣurikā nyaset |
evaṃ vinyasya yatnena śivasyāvāhanaṃ kuru || 33 ||

sadyenāvāhayeddevaṃ vāmadevena sthāpayet |
aghoreṇa tu sānnidhyamarghyaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu || 34 ||

īśānena tu puṣpāṇi śivasya parikalpayet |
hṛdaye hṛdayaṃ dadyāt śiraḥ śirasi vinyaset || 35 ||

śikhāyāntu śikhā dadyāt kavacaṃ sarvagātrikam |
astraṃ hastapradeśe tu vinyasya parameśvari || 36 ||

p. 1022)

evaṃ vinyasya deveśi cintayecchivamavyayam |
mantrātītaṃ paraṃ devaṃ tatvātītaṃ paraṃ padam || 37 ||

anena vidhinā pūjya sakalaṃ parameśvari |
astraṃ japtvā tato devi vikirān sarvato diśi || 38 ||

vikirenmantraviccaiva pañcagavyaṃ tataḥ punaḥ |
gandhodakena saṃśodhya kalaśaṃ bhrāmayettataḥ || 39 ||

vardhanyāmastrajaptāyāṃ japannastrantu bhrāmayet |
acchinnodakadhārāntu dadyāt bāhye tu mantravit || 40 ||

caturasraṃ tasya madhye tu darpaṇañcaiva bāhyataḥ |
vardhanīmagrato gacchet kalaśaṃ tasya pṛṣṭataḥ || 41 ||

p. 1023)

śivantu japataścaiva ācāryeṇa samanvitaḥ |
bhrāmayitvā tato devi punastatraiva sthāpayet || 42 ||

sthāpayitvā tato mantrī arghyaṃ dadyāttataḥ punaḥ |
sthaṇḍilantu tato gacchet vidhinā pūjayettataḥ || 43 ||

śivaṃ pūjya tato devi gacchenmantryanalaṃ punaḥ |
āgneyyāṃ diśi āśritya agnikuṇḍantu kārayet || 44 ||

hastamātrapramāṇantu kuṇḍaṃ kuryāttrimekhalam |
caturaṅgulamekaikā hrasvā hrasvatarā smṛtāḥ || 45 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena prokṣyā streṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ullekhanantu tenaiva uddhṛtyābhyukṣayet punaḥ || 46 ||

p. 1024)

avakuṇḍantu astreṇa gāyatryāvopalepayet |
upalepite tu paścāt kuryādrekhopakalpanam || 47 ||

rekhatrayantu kartavyaṃ pūrvapaścimataśśubham |
ekā uttarataḥ kāryā dakṣiṇāyatamuttarā || 48 ||

vajra eṣa samākhyāto draḍhīkaraṇamuttamam |
viṣṭaraṃ kuṇḍamadhye tu vāgīśvaryāḥ prakalpayet || 49 ||

nīlotpaladalaśyāmāṃ dhyātvā rūpavatīṃ śubhām |
tasmin tato mantrī ṛtumatīṃ vicintayet || 50 ||

tāmrapātre śarāve vā ānayejjātavedasam |
abhyarcya pañcabhirmantraiḥ vidyāṅgaiśca yathākramam || 51 ||

p. 1025)

avakuṇṭhanaṃ tu kartavyaṃ kavacena vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tridhā kuṇḍantu taṃ bhrāmya hṛdayena vidhānavit || 52 ||

prakṣipedyonimārgeṇa hetibhūtaṃ vicintayet |
garbhādhānaṃ pratimantrī sadyena tu tridhā hutiḥ || 53 ||

puṃ praśānte trayaṃ dadyāt āhutīnāntu mantravit |
arghyaṃ pādyaṃ tato dadyāt garbhādhānaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet || 54 ||

vāme puṃsuvanaṃ kāryaṃ dadyānmantrī trayāhutiḥ |
rudrāṇyā tu trayaṃ dadyāt kṛtaṃ puṃsuvanaṃ bhavet || 55 ||

aghoreṇa trayaṃ dadyāt sīmantonnayanaṃ budhaḥ |
trayo brahmaśireṇaiva arghyaṃ pādyaṃ tataḥ punaḥ || 56 ||

p. 1026)

vaktrapañcakasaṃyuktaṃ vaktṛniṣkṛtimeva ca |
vaktrodghāṭantu kuryādānupūrveṇa mantravit || 57 ||

sadyādyā īśaparyantaṃ dadyādāhutayastrayaḥ |
ekenaiva tu mantreṇa juhuyānnatra saṃśayaḥ || 58 ||

astreṇa niṣkṛtiṃ kuryāt vaktrāṇāntu varānane |
āhutīnāṃ trayaṃ dadyāt vaktre vaktre na saṃśayaḥ || 59 ||

udghāṭantu tatvena dadyād vai āhutitrayam |
udghāṭite tato vaktre jātakarmantu homayet || 60 ||

āhutīnāṃ trayaṃ mantrī dadyāt tatpuruṣeṇa tu |
astreṇaiva trayaṃ dadyāt āhutīnāṃ varānane || 61 ||

p. 1027)

evaṃ hute tato mantrī jātakarma kṛtaṃ bhavet |
jātakarma kṛte paścāt rakṣāṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 62 ||

pūrveṇa brahmā tatraiva vinyaset * * * * * ? |
dakṣiṇe rudradevatyaṃ kalpayedviṣṭaropari || 63 ||

paścime viṣṇudevatyaṃ kalpayet sa viṣṭaram |
uttareṇa śivasyaiva kalpayeta yathākramam || 64 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā tato mantrī nāmakarmantu kārayet |
īśānena tu hotavyaṃ nāmakarma vicakṣaṇaḥ || 65 ||

kavacena tathaiveha juhuyādāhutitrayam |
hutvā hyevaṃ tato devī śivāgnirjanito bhavet || 66 ||

p. 1028)

saṃskārasiddhihetvarthaṃ pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet |
saṃskāreṇa yadā śuddhaṃ agnimāhutya suvṛte || 67 ||

caruṃ śrupeta mantrajñā vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
sāṅgena devadevena pañcabrahmayutena ca || 68 ||

sakalīkṛtya tāṃ sthālīṃ śivaṃ tatraiva vinyaset |
tritatvaṃ vinyaset pūrvaṃ śivaṃ tattvāttu vinyaset || 69 ||

ghṛtadigdhāntu tāṃ kṛtvā kṣīrapūrṇāñca kārayet |
yavagodhūmanīvāraiśśāliṣaṣṭikataṇḍalaiḥ || 70 ||

kaṅgunāvātha kurvīta carukaṃ su samāhitaḥ |
madhyamaṃ pākasaṃyuktaṃ pācayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ || 71 ||

p. 1029)

yadā svinnaṃ vijānīyāt tadā tamavatārayet |
cālanaṃ ghaṭṭanālepaṃ kuryādekādaśena tu || 72 ||

taptābhidhāraṇañcaiva tathā bhūtābhidhāraṇam |
ekādaśātmadevena juhuyādāhutitrayam || 73 ||

saṃpātābhirhutañcaiva kṛtvā mantrī vicakṣaṇaḥ |
vibhāgabhājitaṃ kṛtvā carukaṃ mantrasaṃskṛtam || 74 ||

bhāgaṃ devāya kalpeta dvitīyaṃ homakarmaṇi |
tṛtīyaṃ sādhakebhyaśca pañcagavyapurassaram || 75 ||

vidhinā kalpitaṃ devi dadyādvaktrābhimantritam |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhisarpiḥ kuśodakam || 76 ||

p. 1030)

abhimantrya sarvamantraistu amṛtatvaṃ śivena tu |
pañcagavyantu kartavyaṃ vidhinā'nena suvṛte || 77 ||

āśu śuddhimato vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kathayāmi te |
ājyādhiśrayaṇaṃ kuryādudvāsyoplavanaṃ tataḥ || 78 ||

saṃplāvanaṃ tataḥ paścāt kuryāccāpyavadyotanam |
nīrāyaṇaṃ tataḥ kuryāt ājyaśuddhiṃ vidhānavit || 79 ||

gāyatryā kārayetsarvamājyasaṃskārakaṃ śobhane |
saṃskāraiśśodhite ājye ājyabhāgantu homayet || 80 ||

dakṣiṇenottareṇaiva madhyadeśe tathaiva ca |

oṃ agnaye namaḥ | oṃ somāya namaḥ | omagnīṣomābhyāṃ namaḥ |

p. 1031)

omagnaye sviṣṭakṛte namaḥ | 
ājyabhāgavibhāgantu kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 81 ||

śuddhe ājye tato mantraṃ binduṃ binduntu prakṣipet |
ājyaśuddhividhirhyeṣaḥ kathitantu yathākramam || 82 ||

ājye śuddhe tato devi kuryādvaktrābhidhāraṇam |
sadyādi īśaparyantamekaikāhutayo hunet || 83 ||

vaktrābhidhāraṇaṃ hyeṣa kathitaṃ vai śivena tu |
anena sarvakarmāṇi kartavyāni tu deśikaiḥ || 84 ||

ājyādhiśrayaṇe brahmā pūrvaṃ tatraiva cintayet |
udvāsane tu gāyatrī utplavane viṣṇudaivatam || 85 ||

p. 1032)

saṃplavecaivamityuktaṃ rudraṃ tatraiva cintayet |
avadyotasvaro jñeyo deśikena mahātmanā || 86 ||

nirātmane tathā devi svayaṃ devassadāśivaḥ |
abhimantrya samantraistu śivaṃ tatraiva cintayet || 87 ||

sāvitryā bindubinduntu sarvamājyeṣu prakṣipet |
karmamantraprayogena adhidevaiśca saṃyutam || 88 ||

sarvakarmaprasidhyeta sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ |
āsanaṃ kalpayettatra vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 89 ||

hṛnmadhye kalpayeddevi āsanaṃ tava saṃjñitam |
anantaṃ vinyasetpūrvaṃ dharmajñānaṃ tathaiva ca || 90 ||

p. 1033)

navaśaktiṃ tathā devi karṇikāpadmasandhiṣu |
akāraṃ vinyaseddevi karṇikāyāṃ varānane || 91 ||

ādityamaṇḍalaṃ tena cintayet susamāhitaḥ |
hrasvoṅkārasamāyuktaṃ somamaṇḍalakalpitam || 92 ||

ukāro hrasvaṃ deveśi agnimaṇḍalamuttamam |
ākāraṃ brahmadevatyamādityasyādhidaivatam || 93 ||

ikāraṃ viṣṇudevatyaṃ somamaṇḍaladaivatam |
ukāraṃ rudradaivatyamagnimaṇḍaladaivatam || 94 ||

evaṃ vinyasya yatnena mūrtiṃ tatraiva vinyaset |
īśvareṇa samāyuktaṃ vinyaseta varānane || 95 ||

p. 1034)

bindustu īśvaro jñeya ātmā haṃsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
nyasyā tu devadeveśi pañcabrahmāṇi vinyaset || 96 ||

ādityaṃ somamagniñca nyastamātmani suvṛte |
ātmatatve tu vaktavyamātmā tu parameśvari || 97 ||

īśānaḥ puruṣeṇaiva aghoreṇa tu suvṛte |
vāmadevena sadyena lakṣamātraṃ prakīrtitam || 98 ||

aṣṭatriṃśat kalābhistu śirovaktrantu kalpayet |
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃyuktaṃ kartavyaṃ susamāhitaiḥ || 99 ||

sadyojātakalābhistu sthiratvaṃ parikalpayet |
pañcamūrdhānamīśena pūrvādārabhya mantrataḥ || 100 ||

p. 1035)

krameṇa kalpayeddevi īśānasya kalā budhaiḥ |
vaktre tatpuruṣasyaiva kalāni parikalpayet || 101 ||

pūrvamādikrameṇaiva sakalaṃ mūrdhni vinyaset |
aghorasya kalāṃ devi hṛdayādivinirdiśet || 102 ||

hṛdi grīvā ca aṃsau ca nābhau ca jaṭhare tathā |
pṛṣṭhehyurasi mantrajña aghorasya kalā nyaset || 103 ||

vāmasya guhyadeśe tu vinyaseta yathākramam |
gude caiva tu liṅge ca ūrubhyāṃ jānunī tathā || 104 ||

jaṅghe sphijau ca kaṭyāñca pādapārśvau ca tāvubhau |
guhyāṅgakramayogena vāmadevakalā nyaset || 105 ||

p. 1036)

pādau hastau ca nāsāyāṃ śiraścaiva bhujāvubhau |
sadyojātakalā devi sadyaḥ sarvatra mūrtiṣu || 106 ||

evantu sakalīkṛtya tatvāni parikalpayet |
tatvānyaṣṭādaśaṃ devi navatatvānyathāpi vā || 107 ||

pañcatatvāni deveśe tathā ṣaṭtṛṃśameva ca |
pañcatatvāni vinyasya tritatvamupari nyaset || 108 ||

vidyāṅgāni tato dadyāt śivasyāvāhanaṃ kuru |
āvāhite tato devi aṅgāni parikalpayet || 109 ||

hṛdaye hṛdayaṃ dadyāt śire caiva śiro nyaset |
śikhāyāntu śikhāṃ dadyāt kavacaṃ sarvagātrakam || 110 ||

p. 1037)

asraṃ hastapradeśe tu vinyaseta śubhānane |
āvāhayeta sadyena vāmadevena sthāpayet || 111 ||

aghoreṇa tu sānnidhyamarghyaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu |
īśānena tu puṣpāṇi makuṭaṃ kuṇḍalāni ca || 112 ||

īśānaṃ madhyato dadyāt pūrve tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset |
aghoraṃ dakṣiṇenaiva vāmadevaṃ tathottare || 113 ||

sadyojātantu deveśe paścimena tu vinyaset |
āgneyyāṃ hṛdayañcaiva īśānyāntu śiro nyaset || 114 ||

naiṛtyāntu śikhāṃ dadyāt vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ tathā |
astraṃ dvārapradeśe tu vinyaseta yathā budhaḥ || 115 ||

p. 1038)

garbhāvaraṇametattu dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ śṛṇu |
anantaṃ pūrvato dadyāt sūkṣmaṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset || 116 ||

śivottamaṃ tathā devaṃ paścimena tu vinyaset |
ekanetrottareṇaiva vinyaseta sadā budhaḥ || 117 ||

īśānyāmekarudrastu trimūrtiṃ hyagnigocare |
śrīkaṇṭhaṃ naiṛtenaiva śikhaṇḍiṃ vāyugocare || 118 ||

dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ hyevaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ devi śṛṇuṣva kathayāmi te || 119 ||

aindrīntu diśamāśritya indrādyāstu yathākramam |
evaṃ vinyasya yatnena caturthe'strāṇi vinyaset || 120 ||

p. 1039)

yaṣṭvā evaṃ vidhaṃ devi dīkṣāṃ kurvīta mantravit |
pūrvoktena vidhānena kurvīta vidhivistaram || 121 ||

apare'hani deveśe yajecchaṇḍeśvaraṃ guruḥ |
pañcāṅgena samāyuktamardhacandrapurottare || 122 ||

tribhāgahomaṃ kṛtvā vai vijñāpyaivaṃ maheśvaram |
samayāṣṭakadoṣasya kṛtaṃ homaṃ mayā tava || 123 ||

evaṃ vijñāpayeddevi deśikastu mahātmanaḥ |
samayāṣṭakadoṣeṇa sādhakenaiva lipyate || 124 ||

hute caṇḍeśvare paścāt gurupūjāntu kārayet |
pūjayettadguruṃ prājño yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ || 125 ||

p. 1040)

grāmairhiraṇyagośvādyaiḥ deśadānaistathaiva ca |
karṇikākaṭakeyūraiḥ pūjayeta sadā gurum || 126 ||

yena yena ca santuṣṭaḥ tattaddadyāttu deśikaḥ |
nivedayettadātmānaṃ tadā tu ninṛṇo bhavet || 127 ||

sarvasaṃhitasāmānyaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
ṣaḍvidhaṃ yajate yastu ācāryaḥ susamāhitaḥ || 128 ||

tena tuṣṭena tuṣṭāstu aśeṣabhuvanādhipāḥ |
śivavatpūjayeddevi ācāryaṃ tatvapāragam || 129 ||

yathāhaṃ tādṛśassohi gurustatvaviśāradaḥ |

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare adhivāsanaḥ paṭalaḥ caturdaśaḥ |
p. 1041)

īśvaraḥ - 

śarīraṃ sarvajantūnāṃ sarvadevamayaṃ smṛtam |
veditavyaṃ prayatnena sādhakaistatvacintakaiḥ || 1 ||

tatvāni coditānyādau mayā tu tava śobhane |
śarīre tāni sarvāṇi jñātavyāni vipaścitaiḥ || 2 ||

iḍā caiva suṣumnā ca piṅgalā ca tathaiva hi |
nāsāṃ sañcārayogaṃ vai jñātavyaṃ tatvavedibhiḥ || 3 ||

kalānāñcaiva sarvāsāṃ nāḍīnāṃ vāyubhissaha |
sañcāraṃ hyeṣa tatvānāṃ bhoktā bhojyaṃ tathaiva ca || 4 ||

p. 1042)

kālavelavibhāgāni saṅkramāni tathaiva hi |
jñātvā sarvamaśeṣeṇa sādhakassavidhīyate || 5 ||

dakṣiṇena suṣumnā tu yadā vahati suvṛte |
vijñeyassa sadā tajjño uttarāyaṇamuttamam || 6 ||

iḍāyāntu yadā devi tiṣṭhate parameśvaraḥ |
dakṣiṇāyanamityeva kathitaṃ tava suvṛte || 7 ||

madhyasthantu yadā devi piṅgalāyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ |
viṣuvaṃ sa tu vijñeyaṃ yogakālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 8 ||

suṣumnāyāṃ sthito brahmā akārākṣarasaṃyutaḥ |
ādityaḥ sa tu vijñeyo jyeṣṭhā śaktiḥ prakīrtitā || 9 ||

p. 1043)

jñānaśaktiriti jñeyā satvasthaṃ taṃ vinirdiśet |
sāṃgratsthānaṃ sa vijñeyaṃ dakṣiṇena vyavasthitaḥ || 10 ||

iḍāyāṃ saṃsthito viṣṇurīkārākṣarasaṃyutaḥ |
somasya tu sa tu jñeyo vāmā śaktiḥ prakīrtitā || 11 ||

kriyāśaktiḥ sa vijñeyo rājasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam |
svapnasthānaṃ sa vijñeyaṃ vāmatastu vyavasthitam || 12 ||

madhyamaṃ rudramityāhuḥ ukāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
raudrī tantu vijānīyādicchāśaktiḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 13 ||

vahnisthānaṃ hi yatproktaṃ suṣuptaṃ parikīrtitam |
tāmasantu vijānīyāt madhyame tu vyavasthitam || 14 ||

p. 1044)

trikālaviṣayaṃ jñānamebhyo devi prakīrtyate |
bhūtaṃ brahma vijānīyāt bhaviṣyaṃ vaiṣṇave pade || 15 ||

vartamānaṃ hi rudratve sarvaśāstre pratiṣṭhite |
trikālamekato devi bindudevaṃ vyavasthitam || 16 ||

sārvakālaṃ sa*? voktaṃ jñānakālasvata smṛtaḥ |
tasyātītaṃ bhavonnādaṃ devadevaṃ sadāśivam || 17 ||

trikālajñānasaṃyuktaṃ jñātvā bhavati sādhakaḥ |
pañcadhā pañcadevatyaṃ sakalaṃ vyāpya saṃsthitam || 18 ||

anantamāditaḥ kṛtvā pradhānānte tu suvṛte |
brahmāṇamādhipatyena veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 19 ||

p. 1045)

pauruṣamāditaḥ kṛtvā kālatatvamapaścimam |
jñātavyaṃ hi tatvajñaiḥ viṣṇustatrādhidaivatam || 20 ||

granthintu āditaḥ kṛtvā vidyātatvamapaścimam |
vijñeyaṃ tatsadā tajjñaiḥ rudrastatrādhidaivatam || 21 ||

īśvaramāditaḥ kṛtvā yāvattatvaṃ sadāśivam |
jñātavyantu varārohe īśastatrādhidaivatam || 22 ||

upariṣṭācchivaṃ tatvaṃ tatra devaṃ sadāśivam |
etatsarvamaśeṣeṇa jñātavyaṃ deśikena tu || 23 ||

sarveṣāmeva tatvānāṃ śivo vyāpya vyavasthitaḥ |
jñātvā sarvamaśeṣeṇa ācāryassa vidhīyate || 24 ||

p. 1046)

nivṛttiḥ kathito brahmā pratiṣṭhā viṣṇurucyate |
vidyā rudrassamākhyātaḥ śāntirīśvara ucyate || 25 ||

śāntyatītantu deveśi devadevaṃ sadāśivam |
nādasyaiva krameṇaiva devatā kathitā mayā || 26 ||

iṇḍikā dīpikā caiva recikā mocikā tathā |
*? nāstu pañcamo jñeyo niṣkalastu sadāśivaḥ || 27 ||

brahmāṇamāditaḥ kṛtvā jñātavyāni varānane |
sūkṣmānyāyena mayā proktā devatā te prakīrtitāḥ || 28 ||

etāḥ kalāḥ samākhyātāḥ devatā nāḍayastathā |
jñātavyaṃ tatvato devi sādhakena mahātmanā || 29 ||

p. 1047)

kalānāṃ devatānāñca vyāpayitvā vyavasthitaḥ |
sañcāraṃ kurute devi sa tu ātmā vijānataḥ || 30 ||

aṣṭatatvā smṛtā devi yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat |
tasmiṃstaistu samāyuktasañcaret parameśvaraḥ || 31 ||

tiryaṅmṛganarākhyeṣu tatvabhāvendriyeṣu ca |
iṣṭasetsarvabhūteṣu tatvairyukto na saṃśayaḥ || 32 ||

gamāgamaṃ tato devi viditvā sādhako bhavet |
aviditvā na sidhyeta na ca yāti paraṃ padam || 33 ||

bhojyaṃ bhoktā ca dātā ca tatvairyuktaḥ karoti yaḥ |

devyuvāca - 

p. 1048)

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi bhoktā bhojyañca śaṅkara || 34 ||

pānaṃ pibati kastatra svādaṃ gṛhṇāti kaḥ punaḥ |
ko vā kṣipati garbhe tu grāsaṃ gṛhṇāti kaḥ prabho || 35 ||

rasānāṃ vividhānāntu vicārayati kaḥ punaḥ |
etatsarvamaśeṣeṇa kathayasva prasādataḥ || 36 ||

īśvaraḥ - 

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi samāsātsādhakasya tu |
yena vijñātamātreṇa annadoṣe na lipyate || 37 ||

dātā bhoktā ca mantā ca sādākhyapadamāpnuyāt |
annaṃ brahmā raso viṣṇurbhoktā devo maheśvaraḥ || 38 ||

p. 1049)

sthitassarvaśarīreṣu bhuṅkte caiva dadāti ca |
yo vijānāti kartāraṃ bhoktā bhojyañca he priye || 39 ||

śvapaceṣvapi bhuñjāno na sa pāpe na lipyate |
tatvāni manasā cintya svaśarīre yathākramam || 40 ||

karṇau ca cakṣuṣī caiva nāsāyāṃ vadane tathā |
dakṣiṇe śravaṇe caiva khamalaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 41 ||

vāme caiva kalāḍhyantu śravaṇe paricintayet |
sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva nāsāyāṃ parikalpayet || 42 ||

kṣapaṇaṃ kṣayamantañca cakṣuṣī tau vicintayet |
śūnyena paśyate cānnaṃ veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 43 ||

p. 1050)

grāsaṃ gṛhṇāti deveśe kaṇṭhoṣṭhaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
brahmā ca viṣṇuśśravaṇe rudramīśvaracakṣuṣī || 44 ||

ghrāṇe vaktre sthito nityamuktiścaiva sadāśivaḥ |
nāsāśaktiṃ vijānīyādvaktre devaṃ sadāśivam || 45 ||

ṣaṇmukha ekakaraṇaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
evaṃ tatvamayaṃ dhyātvā śivaṃ paramakāraṇam || 46 ||

śivo'hamiti sañcintya bhuñjamāno na lipyate |
ṣaṇmukhenaiva yāgena bhuñjate satataṃ prabhuḥ || 47 ||

trividhena prayogena salilaṃ pibate sadā |
khaṇḍakhādyaiśca bhuñjānaḥ kṣapaṇe gṛhṇate sadā || 48 ||

p. 1051)

badhyate sakalenaiva niṣkalena tu svādviti |
ūrdhvamutkṣipate grāsanna vidyātkhamalaṃ kṛtam || 49 ||

antastho mucyate grāhiṃ nātra kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
śūnyenaiva annādya granthete satataṃ punaḥ || 50 ||

bhojanañcaiva annastho annañcaiva vicārati |
lehyaṃ śoṣyañca peyañca annāni vividhāni ca || 51 ||

kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ parameśāno veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
evaṃ tatvamayaṃ divyamācāryacchinnasaṃśayaḥ || 52 ||

sakṛdbhojitamātreṇa koṭirbhavati bhojitaḥ |
atha tatvavidaścaiva evaṃ bhuñjate priye || 53 ||

p. 1052)

parisaṃkhyā na vidyeta ityāha bhagavān śivaḥ |
atatvapratibuddhasya jñānine koṭirucyate || 54 ||

jñānitatvavidaścaiva parisaṃkhyā na vidyate |
dvijānāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ koṭiṃ saṃbhojya yatphalam || 55 ||

jñānine śānticittāya sakṛt bhuṅkte tu tatphalam |
bhojyaṃ māyātmakaṃ sarvaṃ kartā devo maheśvaraḥ || 56 ||

bhoktā caiva śivassākṣāt evameva na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ vai yo vijānāti deśikastatvapāragaḥ || 57 ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā gṛhamāyāntaṃ krīḍantyoṣadhayo gṛhe |
evaṃ bhojyavidhiṃ samyak na tu sarvatra vyāpayet || 58 ||

p. 1053)

putrasyāpi na dātavyaṃ ye narāḥ pāpabuddhayaḥ |
evaṃ vai yo vijānīyādajñānīcānyathā bhavet || 59 ||

ābhuṅkte satataṃ devi annapāne na saṃśayaḥ |
ūrdhvena vahate prāṇa āpānodhaḥ pravartate || 60 ||

evaṃ jñātvā tu bhoktavyaṃ mucyate sarvakilviṣaiḥ |
tena bhuktena deveśe dātaroḍasya kulāni tu || 61 ||

mucyante yātanā kṣipraṃ suciraṃ narakārṇavāt |

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare devatātatvabhojyavidhiḥ pañcadaśaḥ 
paṭalaḥ |
p. 1054)

devyuvāca - 

puryaṣṭakasamāyukta ātmā sarvatra yujyate |
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi bhagavan vaktumarhasi || 1 ||

sukṛte karmabhoktṛtve duṣkṛte ca tathaiva hi |
puryaṣṭake yadāhyātmā vāyubhūto vyavasthitaḥ || 2 ||

vidyamāne'pi taddehe pratyakṣeṇa tu dṛśyate |
śrūyate yātanā tasya narakeṣu anekadhā || 3 ||

svarge ca vividhaṃ bhogamaṇimādyā maheśvara |
tanubhūtaśśarīrātmā bhogān bhuñjati śaṅkara || 4 ||

narake yātanā tasya kathayasva prasādataḥ |

īśvaraḥ - 

p. 1055)

śṛṇu devi idaṃ guhyaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilam || 5 ||

svarge vā narake vā'pi yathā sukṛtaduṣkṛtau |
bhuñjate pūjitaṃ karma kathayāmi maheśvari || 6 ||

tyakte sthūlaśarīre tu laukike bhūtasaṃbhave |
puryaṣṭake yadāhyātmā vāyubhūto vyavasthitaḥ || 7 ||

śabdasparśaṃ tathā rūpaṃ gandhādiviṣayoragaḥ |
tridhāntaḥ karaṇaṃ devi tairna mucyetkṣaṇādapi || 8 ||

sarvathā vartamānasya gacchantamanugacchati |
tiṣṭhantamanutiṣṭheta dhāvantamanudhāvati || 9 ||

na tairvinā kṣaṇamapi ātmā tiṣṭheta suvṛte |

p. 1056)

taistu yuktaṃ punardehaṃ saṃprapadye varānane || 10 ||

pūrvadehe vinirmukte gacchatastasya dehinaḥ |
akasmājjāyate dehaṃ kṣaṇamātreṇa śobhane || 11 ||

pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśasaṃyutaḥ |
bhūtātmakaṃ tadā dehaṃ jāyate paramāṇubhiḥ || 12 ||

tadā bhogātmakaṃ dehaṃ aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam |
tārādyaṃ jñānajaṃ vāpi akṛtādyādidehajam || 13 ||

paiśācādiṣu deheṣu tatveṣu vividheṣu ca |
narake ca varārohe yātanā tu pṛthagvidhā || 14 ||

bhūtebhyo jāyate dehaṃ prāṇidvayaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |

p. 1057)

brahmaviṣṇvātmakā bhūtā rudrātmā īśvarātmakāḥ || 15 ||

sadāśivātmakā devi taireva paramāṇubhiḥ |
viṣayāścaiva teṣveva jñātavyāni varānane || 16 ||

tairyuktantu saṃbadhyeta tairvimuktastu mucyati |
tasmāttu devadeveśe nyaseddevi sadāśive || 17 ||

evaṃ nyaste tadā yogī niścayaṃ śivatāṃ vrajet |
sanyāsa eṣa vikhyātaḥ śivena paramātmanā || 18 ||

nyāsadīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmāṇamāditaḥ kṛtvā devaiśca sahasaṃyutaḥ || 19 ||

puryaṣṭakaṃ nyaseddevi parivāreṇa deśikaḥ |

p. 1058)

pūrvamādikrameṇaiva śabdādīnāṃ yathākramam || 20 ||

teṣāntu tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śatamekaiva homayet |
evaṃ hute śate devi pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet || 21 ||

tataśśabdañca sparśañca śuddhe brahmaṇi vinyaset |
rasaṃ vai vinyasedviṣṇoḥ rūpaṃ gandhastathaiva ca || 22 ||

rudratatve tu kartavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā |
ahaṅkārastathā buddhirīśvare tantu vinyaset || 23 ||

manastu vinyaseddevi tatve caiva sadāśive |
nyaste puryaṣṭake devi pūrṇāhutyā tu pātayet || 24 ||

brahmoṅkāreṇa deveśe brahmaviṣṇū tu homayet |

p. 1059)

pūrṇāhutyā tu deveśi pātayet susamāhitaḥ || 25 ||

rudrañca īśvarañcaiva rudroṅkāreṇa homayet |
sadāśivasya tatvasya nidhanena tu homayet || 26 ||

pūrṇāhutyā tu dātavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā |
sakalādikrameṇaiva grāhakāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 27 ||

iyaṃ sanyāsinī dīkṣā kathitā tava śobhane |
dīkṣā pañcavidhā devi kathitā tu samāsataḥ || 28 ||

anyāyakathitā dīkṣā tasmiṃsthairye niyojayet |
tatra śudhyenna śudhyeta viśeṣaḥ kathito mayā || 29 ||

dīkṣā saṃśuddhadehantu pañcatve samupāgate |

p. 1060)

samayabhede hetvarthe antyeṣṭintu samārabhet || 30 ||

ṣaḍvidhādhvānamārgasya yadraucyeta varānane |
evaṃ saṃhārayogena huneccaiva yathākramam || 31 ||

ekaikāmāhutiṃ juhuyādante pūrṇāhutiṃ dadet |
samayācāradoṣasya pramādātskhalitasya ca || 32 ||

antyeṣṭistu śivenaikā prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
athavā mātṛkādhvānaṃ kṣakārādi samāsataḥ || 33 ||

akārāntaistu deveśi pañcapañcāhutiṃ hunet |
ekaikena tu varṇena homayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ || 34 ||

samastena tu mantreṇa mātṛkeṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ |

p. 1061)

tena pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyādantyeṣṭintu samarpayet || 35 ||

dīkṣitasyeha **? ryā antyeṣṭirdeśikena tu |
antyeṣṭiḥ śudhyate caiva prāyaścittādviśudhyate || 36 ||

śuddhānpūrvamivān pāśān sādhakassusamāhitaḥ |
sarvājñā laṅghane caiva antyeṣṭi homayettataḥ || 37 ||

jñānavijñānasaṃpannaḥ ācāryastatvapāragaḥ |
kālacakravidhānajñaḥ kālajñassa vidhīyate || 38 ||

sa vai mocayatejantu dvārajño nātra saṃśayaḥ |

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ | 
1062)

devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi kālacakramanuttamam |
kasmātpravartate kālaḥ kathaṃ kālo nigadyate || 1 ||

kimarthaṃ vartate kālaḥ kaḥ kālaḥ prerakastu kaḥ |
yena sañcodyamānastu kālaḥ kalayate prabho || 2 ||

kasmin kasmin pradeśe tu carate kāla īśvaraḥ |
laukikādhyātmikañcaiva kathaṃ vijñāyate prabho || 3 ||

gṛhārikṣāstathāṃśāśca horādekkāṇadhastathā |
vināvināvayaścaiva muhūrtānāṃ tathaiva ca || 4 ||

vāyavyo nāḍayaścaiva svarāścaiva kalāstathā |

p. 1063)

ghaṭikānāṃ samākhyā hi tatvānāñca yathākramam || 5 ||

dakṣiṇottarasaṅkrāntyo ayanaṃ viṣuvaṃ tathā |
laukikādhyātmikañcaiva kathayasva prasādataḥ || 6 ||

śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ yattvayā paripṛcchritam |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yatsurairapi durlabham || 7 ||

laukikādhyātmikañcaiva yathā vai kālasañcaraḥ |
yathā vai jñāyate devi laukikaṃ kālamuttamam || 8 ||

ādhyātmikaṃ yathā kālaṃ kathayāmi viśeṣataḥ |
etatkālavidhānajño labhate śāśvataṃ padam || 9 ||

sidhyate nātra sandehaḥ kālajñastu mahāmate |

p. 1064)

laukikādhyātmikaṃ kālaṃ yo vetti nikhilena tu || 10 ||

sa kālajñasmṛto devi laukike khyātiṃ ca gacchati |
śubhāśubhañca deveśe lokasyaiva hitāhitam || 11 ||

kathayetsarvavṛttānta āścarya anekadhā |
kālacakraṃ samākhyātamādityāt saṃpravartate || 12 ||

muhūrtā tithayaścaiva ahorātrāyanāni ca |
ṛturmāsastathā pakṣo gṛhaṛkṣātharāśayaḥ || 13 ||

sarvametanmahādevi ādityāt saṃpravartate |
aṃśāssandhyastathā tārā horāddrekkāṇayastathā || 14 ||

udite bhāskare caiva sarvametatpravartete |

p. 1065)

sa eṣa kathitaḥ kālaḥ laukikaḥ parameśvari || 15 ||

laukikena tu kālena yathā nāma vyavasthitam |
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane || 16 ||

bhāskare udite caiva gṛhacakraṃ pravartate |
bhāskareṇa samāssarve rāśayaśca na saṃśayaḥ || 17 ||

bhāskaraḥ prathamaḥ proktaḥ keturante vyavasthitaḥ |
grahān patreṣu saṃsthāpya pūrvādidalasaṃsthitān || 18 ||

somamādikrameṇaiva keturante varānane |
gṛhacakraṃ smṛto hyeṣa jñātavyaṃ kālavedinā || 19 ||

somastu pūrvadigbhāge bhaumamāgneyadigdale |

p. 1066)

rajaputraṃ nyaseddevi dakṣiṇe susamāhitaḥ || 20 ||

śanaiścarantu naiṛtyāṃ vinyaseta yathākramam |
bṛhaspatiṃ nyasennityaṃ paścime susamāhitaḥ || 21 ||

rāhuṃ vāyavyatasthāpya śukramuttarato nyaset |
ketumīśānasthāpya tato mantrāḥ prakalpayet || 22 ||

brahmasthānantu yatproktaṃ bhāskaraṃ tatra vinyaset |
sakalaṃ niṣkalañcaiva śūnyatatvaṃ tathaiva ca || 23 ||

kalāḍhyaṃ khamalañcaiva kṣapaṇaṃ kṣayamantakam |
kaṇṭhoṣṭhyañcaiva deveśe gṛhāhyete prakīrtitāḥ || 24 ||

sakalañcāditaḥ kṛtvā yo japyāni varānane |

p. 1067)

parākṣaraṃ paraṃ devaṃ brahmasthānena vinyaset || 25 ||

brahmasthāne sthito nityaṃ bhāskaraṃ surasundari |
karṇike devatānyāsaṃ kartavyaṃ kālavedinā || 26 ||

evaṃ gṛhāṇi vinyasya tatra cāraṃ samabhyaset |
sakalādikrameṇaiva kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 27 ||

ayute dve sahasrañca ṣaṭśatāni yathākramam |
ete prāṇā smṛtā devi vibhajeta yathākramam || 28 ||

ekaikasya tu kālajñassahasradvayameva ca |
śatāni saptavijñeyamekaikasya varānane || 29 ||

gṛhacāraṃ samākhyātaṃ saṃkṣepānna tu vistarāt |

p. 1068)

patre patre tu saṃsthāpya gṛhān sarvān varānane || 30 ||

tatkramāni tathā devi kālajñaḥ pravicārayet |
dīptāṅgāriṇi dhūmā ca sthānatrayamudāhṛtam || 31 ||

karṇikāsthasthitassūryaḥ trīṇi patrāṇi lokayet |
ātmanā saha saṃyuktaḥ karṇikāsthaḥ prabhu smṛtaḥ || 32 ||

pūrve homaṃ vijānīyāt saumyaṃ karma samārabhet |
raudramāgneyadigbhāge śāntiṃ vai dakṣiṇena tu || 33 ||

naiṛtyoccāṭanārthāya śrīkāmaḥ paścimena tu |
vāyavyāṃ kṣipranāśāya dhanalābhāya cottare || 34 ||

īśānyāṃ jñānalābhāya kathitaṃ kālavedinā |

p. 1069)

śāntike pauṣṭike caiva evamādikrameṇa tu || 35 ||

ye ca randhroparandhrāśca patrānte ca vyavasthitāḥ |
aśubhamekataḥ proktaṃ dvitīyasmin śubhaṃ vadet || 36 ||

yadvirodhāyate devi sādhakasya gṛhottamaḥ |
tadgṛhaṃ pūjayennityaṃ karṇikāsthaṃ yadā budhaḥ || 37 ||

tadā saunugrahaṃ yāti sa gṛho nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ekaikasya gṛho devi horātrāṇi prakīrtitāḥ || 38 ||

tatrā'pi carate hyātmā kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
ekaikasya tu horāyāṃ śatāni navasaṃkhyayā || 39 ||

prāṇānāṃ devadeveśi jñātavyaṃ kālavedinā |

p. 1070)

etatte cāramākhyātaṃ śivena paramātmanā || 40 ||

etaccāravidhānajñaḥ kālajño nāvasīdati |
gṛhahorabalenaiva vaktavyantu śubhāśubham || 41 ||

pāpe pāpamatiṃ vidyāt śubhena śubhamādiśet |
evaṃ balābalaṃ jñātvā ātmanasya parasya vā || 42 ||

kathanātpuṇyamāpnoti kālajño nātra saṃśayaḥ |
dehasthaṃ kathitaṃ devi horacāramanuttamam || 43 ||

ataḥ parataraṃ vakṣye rāśisañcāramuttamam |
rāśau rāśau yadāhyātmā carate kathayāmyaham || 44 ||

saṃvatsaramayañceti dvādaśāraṃ suśobhane |

p. 1071)

akāraḥ prathamo devi meṣarāśiḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 45 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
tatrasthaścarate hyātmā yathāvattannibodhame || 46 ||

ekaikasya tu deveśi śatānyaṣṭādaśa kramāt |
eṣa cāraḥ samākhyātaḥ rāśau rāśau na saṃśayaḥ || 47 ||

yogamutpadyate tasya kālayogavidasya tu |
yatpatre paśyate doṣaṃ tanmāse doṣamādiśet || 48 ||

śastrāpātasubhikṣañca durbhikṣaṃ narakaṃ tathā |
kṣemamārogyārthalābhaṃ vadate kālayogavit || 49 ||

rāśau rāśau punassarvaṃ drekkāṇantu samāsataḥ |

p. 1072)

tadrekkāṇe nave rāśau cāraṃ teṣāṃ dadāmyaham || 50 ||

ekaikasya tu drekkāṇe śatāni ṣaṭ prakīrtitāḥ |
ekaikodayasthāvajñaḥ sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 51 ||

yogamutpadyate tasya acirādeva yogine |
uttiṣṭhe cādadhāne ca pādalepe'ñjane tathā || 52 ||

sidhyate nātra sandehaḥ kālajñānavidasya tu |
gṛhoparāgavanmāse hṛtpadme tu sa paśyati || 53 ||

candrasūryoparāge ca sarvaṃ paśyati yoginaḥ |
śubhāśubhantu drekkāṇaiḥ kathayeta varānane || 54 ||

aṃśastrayastu pañcaika ekaikasya samāsataḥ |

p. 1073)

aṃśādrekkāṇahoraiśca ekaikasya samāsataḥ || 55 ||

aṃśādrekkāṇahoraiśca graharāśyādayo nava |
tāracandrabalenaiva śubhāśubhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 56 ||

tārācandrabalenaiva śubhāśubhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
vadate yoginaḥ śrīmān lokasyaiva hitāhitam || 57 ||

eteṣu caratehyātmā tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
sahasradvayaṃ vijñeyaṃ śatāni catureva tu || 58 ||

tārāṇāñcāramākhyātamaṃśānāntu yathā śṛṇu |
tārāṇāṃ yāvatī saṃkhyā aṃśānāṃ tāvatī smṛtāḥ || 59 ||

eṣa te cāramākhyātaṃ cakre saṃvatsarātmike |

p. 1074)

jñānena mucyate devi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 60 ||

evaṃ vai yo na jānāti kālajñānītthaṃ bhavet |
ātmā tu kathitaḥ kāla īśvarassa tu kathyate || 61 ||

tena jñātena deveśi kālajño nāvasīdati |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kālacakramanuttamam || 62 ||

nāḍīvāyukalaissārdhaṃ svaraiścarasasaṃyutam |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yāthātathyena me śṛṇu || 63 ||

dvātriṃśamaṃśakāṃ kṛtvā likheccakraṃ samāsataḥ |
kalā tu devadeveśe ṣoḍaśaiva tu vinyaset || 64 ||

pūṣā yaśā ca sumanā prītituṣṭiriti dhṛtiḥ |

p. 1075)

tathā cakradhṛtikarī saumarī cicchubhaṃ tathā || 65 ||

śaśinītvaṅgirā caiva chāyā saṃpūrṇamaṇḍalā |
daśapañca kalā jñeyā amṛtā caiva ṣoḍaśī || 66 ||

kalāhyetāssamākhyātā nāḍyo vai śṛṇu sāṃpṛtam |
jvālinī tu śamā dhūmrā kāmadā vijayā tathā || 67 ||

amṛtā ya hulā pakṣitvacavāhātyalaṃbuṣā |
tālujihvopajihvā ca tathā jihvāvahā ca yā || 68 ||

buddhivāhā prāṇavāhā tathā cāmṛtavāhinī |
ṣoḍaśaitāssamākhyātāḥ marutādiralaṅkṛtā || 69 ||

kṛkaro devadattaśca pauṇḍarīko dhanañjayaḥ |

p. 1076)

vepathussaṃbhramaścaiva vibhramo bhrama eva ca || 70 ||

prāṇopānasamānaśca udāno vyāna eva ca |
ṛṣayo nāgakūrmaśca ṣoḍaśaite mahānilāḥ || 71 ||

akārādyā svarā devi te tvayā viditāḥ purā |
bibhajya kathayetprāṇān kalādīnāṃ yathākramam || 72 ||

sahasraikaṃ samākhyātamekaikasya varānane |
śatatrayañca jñātavyaṃ prāṇāḥ pañcāśadeva tu || 73 ||

kalādhvaraṃ tathā vāyuḥ nāḍayaśca samāsataḥ |
ekodayena jñātavyaṃ deśikena mahātmanā || 74 ||

nāḍīvāyukalaissārdhaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |

p. 1077)

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi ṛkṣeṣu camare yathā || 75 ||

tathā te kathayiṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
śatāni sapta vijñeyāḥ * *? prāṇāstathaiva ca || 76 ||

prāṇamekādaśaścaiva nimeṣaikaṃ tathaiva ca |
lavamekañca jñātavyaṃ kālajñena mahātmanā || 77 ||

tṛṭiśca saptabhāgena nakṣatre kathitaṃ tava |
aṃśādhvā daśamākhyātā ṛkṣāṇāṃ varavarṇinī || 78 ||

śatamekañca jñātavyaṃ kālajñena mahātmanā |
ebhyaścarati ātmā tu kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 79 ||

ṛkṣe ṛkṣe varārohe aṃśāścatvāri kīrtitāḥ |

p. 1078)

yathā carati viśvātmā śṛṇuṣva kathayāmi te || 80 ||

prāṇāśśataṃ samākhyātaṃ prāṇāyatistathaiva ca |
prāṇā dvādaśa vijñeyā nimeṣadvaya saṃyutam || 81 ||

lavadvayaṃ tathaivoktaṃ śivena paramātmanā |
dvā navati śataikantu prāṇasaṃkhyā prakīrtitāḥ || 82 ||

nimeṣatrayañca vikhyātaṃ tribhāgena tṛṭidvayam |
tṛṭe śṛṅgāgramākhyātaṃ jñātavyaṃ susamāhitaiḥ || 83 ||

saptaviṃśati nakṣatrāṃ śāhyaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
aśīti caturaṃśantu sarvatra parikīrtitam || 84 ||

kālaṃ madhyandine devi avīcī parikīrtitāḥ |

p. 1079)

kṛttikādiṣu ratneṣu jñātavyaṃ kālavedinā || 85 ||

sarveṣu siddhidā devī cī **? parikīrtitā |
yasyai tacchubhaṛkṣeṇa vīcāyāṃ vrajate naraḥ || 86 ||

sarvasiddhimavāpnoti dhaniko nātra saṃśayaḥ |
na tasya aśubhaṃ kiñcit vyābhīcyāṃ vrajate tu yaḥ || 87 ||

eṣa te cāramākhyātaṃ kālacakramupāsinām |
ahorātreṇa jñātavyaṃ kālajñena mahātmanā || 88 ||

etaccāravibhāgajño labhate śāśvataṃ padam |
dine dine sthiredyastu idaṃ cakraṃ samādhinā || 89 ||

sa loke pūjyatāṃ yāti paratra ca śivāntikam |

p. 1080)

naiva puṇyaṃ bhavettasya naiva pāpaṃ kathañcana || 90 ||

darśanāttasya kartavyaṃ ye kecitpāpakarmiṇaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi phalasaṃkhyā yathaiva ca || 91 ||

tatparā kathitā ṣaṣṭiṃ viduṣaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
viduṣāṇi tathā ṣaṣṭiṃ bindurekaṃ pracakṣate || 92 ||

ṣaṣṭibinduphalaṃ proktaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane |
phalasya eṣā saṃkhyoktā kālaṃ vai laukikasya tu || 93 ||

punareva pravakṣyāmi adhyātmena varānane |
taruṇasya pradhānasya niruddhaḥ prāṇinaḥ sa tu || 94 ||

ṣaṭ prāṇāstu samākhyātā vinā prītiṃ samādiśet |

p. 1081)

ṣaṣṭimirvī nāḍyekā ghaṭikā kathitā tu sā || 95 ||

ghaṭikāstu tathā ṣaṣṭi ahorātraṃ pracakṣate |
muhūrtā striṃśadākhyātā ahorātravido viduḥ || 96 ||

ahorātreṇa martyānāṃ muhūrtāstriṃśatkīrtitāḥ |
etānyekaiva saṃkhyātaṃ mātasya surasundari || 97 ||

muhūrtā navaśatā jñeyaṃ kālajñena mahātmanā |
dvādaśe gaṇitaṃ māsaṃ saṃvatsaramihocyate || 98 ||

saṃvatsare muhūrtāni yāvanti kathayāmi te |
sahasrāṇi daśaproktā stathāhyaṣṭaśatāni tu || 99 ||

muhūrtāni varārohe mayā te kathitāni tu |

p. 1082)

muhūrte navaye devi tathaiva kathayāmi te || 100 ||

śatāni saptadeveśe viṃśatiśca na saṃśayaḥ |
eṣacāraṃ mayā proktaṃ veditavyaṃ hitaiṣibhiḥ || 101 ||

anena kramayogena japasiddhimavāpnuyāt |
trikālaviṣayaṃ tasya jāyate jāpinasya tu || 102 ||

ghaṭikā pañcadeveśe rāśau rāśau na saṃśayaḥ |
ghaṭikā pañcadeveśe akārasyodayasmṛtaḥ || 103 ||

evamādikrameṇaiva veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
laukikaṃ praharañcaiva ṣaṭbhāgaṃ parikīrtitam || 104 ||

ṣaṇmāsāstu samākhyātā uttarastu pracakṣate |

p. 1083)

dakṣiṇe tāvatī jñeyā kālajñena mahātmanā || 105 ||

yadrāśau vartate kālaḥ tasyaiva kathayāmi te |
udayaṃ prathamo devi rāśibhiḥ kathitastava || 106 ||

yasmin rāśau sthito hyātmā māsamādi karoti saḥ |
makaraḥ kuṃbhamīnañca meṣañca vṛṣabhastathā || 107 ||

mithunaḥ kathito ṣaṣṭho rāśyaḥ ṣaṇmāsakīrtitāḥ |
karkaṭassiṃha kanyaśca tulāvṛścikameva ca || 108 ||

dhanuṣī ca samākhyāto dakṣiṇe ṛtavāstriyaḥ |
māsi māsi samākhyāto yathāvacchṛṇu suvrate || 109 ||

śatāni caiva catvāri prāṇāḥ pañcadaśaiva tu |

p. 1084)

māsi māsi samākhyāto yathāvattava śobhane || 110 ||

uttare vartamānastu yadā dakṣiṇato vrajet |
yāvantaṃ kramate devi madhye tu viṣuvaṃ smṛtam || 111 ||

eṣā saṅkrāntirākhyātā dakṣiṇottaratasthitā |
indriyārthaṃ parityajya vyomāvastho bhavedyadā || 112 ||

viṣuvantaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ veditavyaṃ hitaiṣibhiḥ |
yadā ūrdhvatvamāyāti devadevo jagadguruḥ || 113 ||

aha evaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ sarvatantreṣu bhāṣitam |
adhobhāge smṛtā rātriḥ madhye tu ayanaṃ bhavet || 114 ||

niścalordhvamadhaścaiva madhyasthantu vijṛṃbhibhiḥ |

p. 1085)

hikkāmudāhṛtā cikkā aṣṭadhā tu krameṇa tu || 115 ||

ūnarātraṃ bhaveddhikkā adhimāsyo vijṛṃbhikā |
ṛṇastu bhavate kasso niśvāso dhana ucyate || 116 ||

eṣa cāraḥ samākhyātaḥ kālacakramupāsinām |
evañcāravidhānajño labhate śāśvataṃ padam || 117 ||

preritaḥ śaktinā devi nānā ceṣṭāni kārayet |
cārasaṃyogayogena ātmānamupacaryate || 118 ||

etaccāramiti proktaṃ śivena paramātmanā |
sakālaṃ kalpanāmantu śaktināṃ parameśvari || 119 ||

vikāritvamupāyāti sa cārairupacaryate |

p. 1086)

akālakalamityuktaṃ vādino'pi dvidhoddhṛtam || 120 ||

svayaṃ sākṣāt smṛtaḥ kālo devadevassadāśivaḥ |
nityāvasthamidaṃ kālaṃ brahmādīnāṃ varānane || 121 ||

tadeva cārayogena sañcārairupacaryate |
nityāvastho'nyathā kālaḥ kena tadupacaryate || 122 ||

anena upacāreṇa kālamevamupāsyate |
cāreṇa jñāyate kālaṃ cārāt siddhirna saṃśayaḥ || 123 ||

cāre bhuktiśca muktiśca cārajño nāvasīdati |
tasmāccāramidaṃ devi veditavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 124 ||

sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ kālaṃ yo vijānāti kālavit |

p. 1087)

na kālajñassa vijñeyo bhavennakṣatra sūcakaḥ || 125 ||

jyotirjñānamidaṃ devi mayā khyātaṃ samāsataḥ |
veditavyaṃ prayatnena kālajñaissa tu vedibhiḥ || 126 ||

aharvṛddhikṣayaṃ rātrau rātrau vṛddhikṣaye'hani |
anena upacāreṇa laukikamupacaryate || 127 ||

na rātrirna divā yasya ayanaṃ viṣuvaṃ tathā |
nityaṃ nityasthito devi svayaṃ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ || 128 ||

icchā tasya mahādevi upacārairupacaryate |
evaṃ kālamiti proktaṃ sarvataḥ saṃpravartate || 129 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare kālacakraprakaraṇaṃ nāma saptadaśaḥ 
paṭalaḥ |
p. 1088)

devyuvāca - 

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi prasādātkathayasva me |
tvanmukhāmṛtasārantu pītaṃ jñānāmṛtaṃ mayā || 1 ||

kalākalitasantānaṃ jagajjanmalayodbhavam |
śrutametanmayā sarvaṃ tvatprasādāt sudurlabham || 2 ||

adhunā śrotumicchāmi prasādaṃ kuru śaṅkara |
āyuḥ pramāṇaṃ yadbhogaṃ manujānāṃ maheśvara || 3 ||

vijñānaṃ maraṇaṃ teṣāṃ kathaṃ jñāsyanti mohitāḥ |
utpannasya vināśo'sti kṛtireṣā tu kīrtitāḥ || 4 ||

tadvināśe tu kiṃ rūpaṃ chinnaṃ kālakramaṃ yataḥ |

p. 1089)

atra mṛtyusamīpasthaṃ kathaṃ vijñāyate prabho || 4 ||

etatsarvaṃ samāsena kathayasva prasādataḥ |

īśvaraḥ - 

sādhu sādhu punassādhu praśnametatsudurlabham || 5 ||

pṛṣṭosmitvatprasannārthaṃ śrūyatāntu varānane |
yathā cāsannamṛtyustu jāyate tu parasya tu || 6 ||

divasamudāsidādvantu muhūrtaṃ sadya eva ca |
yena yena ca cihnena lakṣyate tu śṛṇuṣva me || 7 ||

sa ca vai dvividho lakṣyo bahirabhyantare nṛṇām |
ṛkṣācakraṃ gṛhaiścaiva aṅgāriṣṭā viparyayāt || 8 ||

p. 1090)

bahirliṅgantu vijñeyaṃ antarliṅgantu nādajam |
bahirliṅgāni vakṣyāmi samāsāttu varānane || 9 ||

navakoṣṭhaṃ kṛtaṃ cakraṃ kṛtvā tu gaṇayedbudhaḥ |
tatraiva koṣṭhe koṣṭhe tu likhennakṣatramaṇḍalam || 10 ||

kṛttikādi bharaṇyantaṃ lakṣayeta gṛhā hitam |
rikṣātrayasmṛto janma tathā saṃpadviparyayau || 11 ||

kṣemañca pratyarañcaiva sādhanaṃ naidhanaṃ tathā |
maitraṃ paramamaitrañca tārācakraṃ śubhāśubham || 12 ||

taṃ vai krūragrahākrāntaṃ janmādau lakṣayedbudhaḥ |
ādhānaṃ karma janmaistu bhaumādityaśanaiścaraiḥ || 13 ||

p. 1091)

ete krūragrahā devi yasya tiṣṭhanti so hataḥ |
ete grahā viruddhāstu sa naro hi hatoddhṛvam || 14 ||

eko'pi saumyasaṃpṛkto bhavate kṛcchrajīvanaḥ |
saumyadvaye kapālastha saṃpadastu pade pade || 15 ||

saumyatraye'pi sumanā nityatuṣṭo bhavennaraḥ |
yathā janmatraye devi krūrāssaumyaśubhāśubhāḥ || 16 ||

vadantyevaṃ tathānye'pi vadyantāṃ tārasaṅgamam |
pāpaghnāssaṃpadasthāssyupīpatsthā padakārakāḥ || 17 ||

kṣemaghnā kṣemadāścaiva pratyare bahudoṣajāḥ |
vidyāsiddhiñca kāryañca sādhakasya bhavanti te || 18 ||

p. 1092)

dehanāśaṃ prakurvanti nidhanasthāna saṃsthitāḥ |
maitre tu maitratāṃ kuryuḥ yadyasyānyāpi krauryatā || 19 ||

gṛhā hiṃsanti bhāryāñca gṛhāḥ paramamaitragāḥ |
saumyāḥ śubhaṃ prayacchanti ekadvitriguṇodayāḥ || 20 ||

guṇakramācchubhaṃ krūrā prayacchanti gṛhā yadā |
evaṃ gṛhagatiṃ budhvā ātmanaśca parasya vā || 21 ||

prabudhyanti narā devi ātmanaśca śubhāśubham |
aṅgāriṣṭaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi yathā sphuṭam || 22 ||

śuṣkakaṇṭhoṣṭhatāluśca akasmādrudhiracchaviḥ |
skandho'pi vakratāmeti vināśaṃ vatsarāntike || 23 ||

p. 1093)

agnilamaṇḍalaṃ vyomni paśyate ca dine dine |
sitaṃ haritakṛṣṇañca vatsarārdhāyuṣo hi saḥ || 24 ||

yadādityaṃ viraśmiñca candraṃ lāñcchanavarjitam |
suṣumṇāntārakājyoti so'pi ṣāṇmāsikāyuṣaḥ || 25 ||

hiraṇyavarṇaṃ puruṣaṃ svapne vā kṛṣṇapiṅgalam |
paśyate vā śiracchāyāṃ ṛtutrayaṃ sa jīvati || 26 ||

tailapānaṃ tathā'bhyaṅgaraktamālyāṃvarasrajā |
kharoṣṭrayānamāsīnagṛdhrakākaśivādibhiḥ || 27 ||

paśyetpretapiśācāṃśca udvāhañcāṅgabhakṣaṇam |
svapne ca lapsyate caiva so daikaṃ yadi jīvati || 28 ||

p. 1094)

śaṅkhāvartabhuje nṛttajjomamedhā gulphasandhiṣu |
yadā niṣpandatāṃ yāti so'vaśyaṃ vadhamāpnuyāt || 29 ||

arundhatidhṛvañcaiva somārkajyotimaṇḍalam |
na paśya* * *? dhānaṃ so'vaśyaṃ mriyate naraḥ || 30 ||

arundhatī bhavejjihvā na paśyedvarṣāyuṣaḥ |
alpāyurnaiva paśyeta nāsikāgranthi vā smṛtam || 31 ||

du**? pāṇijaiḥ kāntai vakṣyale jyotimaṇḍale |
paśyedarkasomamaṇḍalaṃ caturmāsāyuṣo hi saḥ || 32 ||

tālurandhragataṃ dhūmaṃ tanma **? na mucyate |
na tu paśyati mūḍhātmā ṛtumekaṃ sa jīvati || 33 ||

p. 1095)

akasmājjāyate sthūlo akasmācca kṛśo bhavet |
atibhīto'ti saṃ* * *? rṣamekāyuṣo hi saḥ || 34 ||

lohadaṇḍena pāṇiṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇāṃbaracchadam |
puruṣañcābhimukhaṃ svapne ṛtudhyā * * * *? || 35 ||

*? rya vai snātamātrasya hṛdayañcaiva śuṣyati |
anuṣṇañca bhavedgātraṃ dvaumāsāyurgatāyuṣaḥ || 36 ||

ameghavidyudaṃ paśyeddi * * * *? nurniśi |
dṛṣṭe deśe'pi digdāho māsatrayaparāyuṣaḥ || 37 ||

na śṛṇoti sphuṭaṃ śabdaṃ cakṣuṣī śrūyate sakṛt |
gandhamāghrāti cāṇdhānya * * *? vigatāyuṣaḥ || 38 ||

p. 1096)

paśya jihvā bhavetkṛṣṇā raktapadmotpalaṃ mukham |
varṇañca vividhāṅgeṣu udayaṃ rudate sakṛt || 39 ||

nābhikaṃpo **? śośca so'rdhamāsādvipadyate |
nābhijñāya śrījihvaṃ dīpamāghrāni pāṣiṣā *? || 40 ||

kākaiśca kṛtamāsīna caturmāsāyuṣo naraḥ |
hikkā * * *? te nityaṃ vaktrarandhrānugo marut || 41 ||

laṃbakarṇaiva paśyeta māsārdhe vigatāyuṣaḥ |
evaṃ vidhairbahuliṅgaiḥ boddhavyaṃ mṛtyucoditaiḥ || 42 ||

adhunā'ntargatā devi yogināṃ nādajaṃ śṛṇu |
idaṃ trinetra saṃproktaṃ yogino bhāskarodaye || 43 ||

p. 1097)

tatkāle cātmano mṛtyuṃ jīvitavyaṃ vicārayet |
tato yogī śucirbhūtvā dhīro yogāsane sthitaḥ || 44 ||

budhyamānotmajannādaṃ suṣumnāntargataṃ budhaḥ |
apramattassadā tiṣṭhet yukto nādaikatāṃ gataḥ || 45 ||

ekaikasya tu nāḍyāyāḥ prāṇānnava śataṃ vahet |
navaśaktīti saṅkrānti kāle vai kalayetsadā || 46 ||

nāḍyāvasthaḥ sthitohyātmā prakṛtyā vahatīśvaraḥ |
ye tu saṅkrāntike prāṇā suṣumnā tu sthito vahet || 47 ||

viṣu *? pravahe nātha budhvā kālaṃ samādiśet |
ahorātreṇa cābdaikaṃ yogī jīvitumicchami || 48 ||

p. 1098)

ahorātradvayenaiva jīvetsaṃvatsaradvayam |
**? rabdantu vijñeyañcaturbhiścaturabdikam || 49 ||

bhavet pañcābdikaṃ mṛtyuryasya pañcāhanirgamam |
ṣaṭsaptāṣṭadine vyūḍhe tāvadvarṣāṇi jīvati || 50 ||

ahorātreṇaiva kālaḥ kalayateśvaraḥ * *? |
kadācicchakaleśānaḥ saptapraharanirgamam || 51 ||

ṣaṇmāsāyurbhaveddevi trimāsaṃ ṣaṭprahārikam |
pañcakohyardha * * *? caturbhirmāsajīvinaḥ || 52 ||

ardhamāsāyuṣo mantrī praharatrayanirgamam |
praharadvayaṃ tathā vyūḍhaṃ dinānyaṣṭau sa jīvati || 53 ||

p. 1099)

vyuḍhaṃ praharamekantu jīvet * *? catuṣṭayam |
praharārdhaṃ vahedyasya sa jīvati dinadvayam || 54 ||

sadyo mṛtyumavāpnoti yasya dvitripathaṃ gataḥ |
yaṃ vā kālaṃ samāruhya pravahet * * * * ? yam || 55 ||

māsārdhāhnasamālakṣyettatkāle niścayo bhavet |
uttarāyaṇaje kāle kathitaṃ tava śobhane || 56 ||

athavā'nyo'pi deveśi * * * ? yogibhissadā |
śrotramārgakṛtāṅguṣṭhau ghoṣaṃ na śṛṇute naraḥ || 57 ||

ṣaṇmāsābhyantare devi māraṇaṃ tasya nirdiśet |
ghoramadhye * * * * * * * *? na 
kāravam || 58 ||

p. 1100)

na śṛṇoti sadā tasya prāpyamāsaṃ mriyeta saḥ |
utpādyañca tathākarṇya mṛtyuyogaṃ tathaiva ca || 59 ||

lakṣyetsatatayuktantu yo * * *? sya cintakaḥ |
yogī vaktrāntanivahaṃ kālasaṅkrāntipañcakam || 60 ||

utpādyamānamapara * * * * * ? rannayet |
dravyahānirmanodvegaṃ kuryādrogavivardhanam || 61 ||

gṛhabhedaṃ suhṛnnāśaṃ tejohānirvivardhanam |
nāsikādakṣiṇapuṭe dakṣiṇāyanajīvite || 62 ||

saṅkrāntyaṣṭau yadā yāti karaṇayogodayo hi saḥ |
kānala * * * * * * ? na granthibhaṅgandaram || 
63 ||

p. 1101)

sphoṭikā śūlarogāṃśca uro doṣaḥ prakopanam |
vāmaghrāṇapuṭāntasthaṃ saṅkrāntyāhyaṣṭapañcakam || 64 ||

jvararogaśirortiṃ vā śūlasaṃstaṃbhanoriṣā |
nīroga pramehañca mūtrakṛcchrakāpayet || 65 ||

śreṣmalāvyādhimardhāsaṃstho maheśvaraḥ * * *? |
yatkāle vahate vāyuḥ tatkāle divase pare || 66 ||

vyādhibhiḥ pīḍyate janturvāmayāme ta *? nare |
atordhvasparśabhaṃ lakṣaṃ nāsādhastāttu **? ri || 67 ||

ūrdhvena ūrdhvaṃ viśaśe tarudoṣāḥ pūrvacoditāḥ |
dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇe sparśi vā *? krośaparābhavam || 68 ||

p. 1102)

madhye madhyasphuṭasparśi parābhibhavanaṃ yayuḥ |
sāṅkrāntyeko bahudhā pravahante itastataḥ || 69 ||

tasya lā * * * * * * * * * ? pū 
janitam |
yadā tu mandaṃ cārisyānnibījasthā samāśritaḥ || 70 ||

dharmaiśvaryābhilābhaṃ kuryātpriyasamāgamam |
yadā dvādaśa * * * * * * * * *? 
dvaye || 71 ||

saṃvatsareṇa ma **? prāptavānyoginassadā |
* *? rakavicchedānmāsaikaikantu chanda * * ? || 72 ||

navaśīti tu * * * * * ? prāṇakṣayo bhavet |
divasaikaikavicchedādyāvattriṃśatsamāgatāḥ || 73 ||

p. 1103)

yadā pūrvedyu velāyāṃ mṛtyuḥ pravara * * *? |
* * * * * * * * * ? lamṛtyuṃ 
samādiśet || 74 ||

iḍā suṣumnagetyevaṃ boddhavyaṃ kālasañcaram |
dakṣiṇāyanajaṃ kālaṃ ka* * * * * * * ? || 
75 ||

**? nāṃ yogakāle tu śuddhassaprāṇagaśśivaḥ |
suṣamnā dakṣiṇe prāṇe tadvahe caiva * * *? || 76 ||

* * * * * * * * * * * ? 
mayanasmṛtam |
trirucchvāsava *? tābhyāṃ te caiva viṣuvāyanam || 77 ||

makaraṃ kuṃbhamīnañca meṣañca mithunaṃ tathā |
suṣumnā * * * * * * *? yacottarāyaṇā || 78 
||

p. 1104)

karkasiṃhakanyā ca tulāvṛścirdhanustathā |
iḍayāntarmahādevi rāśayo di * * * * ? || 79 ||

**? nāḍyodaye devi tatkarkaparikīrtitaḥ |
makaramādisaṅkrāntyai kaikeyā budhaḥ || 80 ||

ādityādi bhave * * * * * * ? caturdaśa |
vijñeyaḥ prāṇavikṣepaḥ prabudhyante ca te kramāt || 81 ||

divācāro bhavedyasya vettā tasya dvayaṃ bhavet |
pra* * * ? matikālaṃ samīparārdhakalpitam || 82 ||

pūrvodayātsamārabhya prāṇasaptadaśa kramāt |
prāṇāṣṣaṣṭissamākhyātā prāṇāścaikādaśaṃ punaḥ || 83 ||

p. 1105)

nimeṣaikaṃ lavaṃ hyekaṃ tṛṭimekādaśa nyūnakam |
kṛttikādyā bhaveccāraṃ bharaṇyāntāni moditāḥ || 84 ||

evaṃ śarīrajañcārarikṣeṣu kathitaṃ tava |
jñātvā yogī japenmṛtyuṃ dhyātvā de* *? lakṣaram || 85 ||

sa eva bhagavān kāla ajayaḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalaḥ |
nāḍikāgrahasaṃstho hi kālaḥ kalayate prabhuḥ || 86 ||

tatra cittaṃ samādhāya kālabījaṃ vicintayet |
śaṃbhumambujapādañca bījaṃ kāleśvaraṃ sthitam || 87 ||

nāḍikāgrahasaṃsthāne vṛdhyā tantu japetkalam |
tadā pravartate tasya trikālajñānamuttamam || 88 ||

p. 1106)

bhūtaṃ kathayate yogī bhaviṣyadvartamānakam |
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare yuktaḥ kālajñānaṃ pravartate || 89 ||

trikālajñānasaṃsiddhiḥ jāyate yoginasya tu |
kālātmā japato devi yato vā vā maheśvari || 90 ||

na tasya kalayate kālaḥ kāle svacchāṃ vṛjet *? | 
hatamṛtyujarārohi sarvarogavivarjitaḥ || 91 ||

dūrācchravaṇavijñānaṃ mananañcāvalokanam |
jāyate nātra sandeho yogī kālajayeṣiṇaḥ || 92 ||

ekacittassamāpanno nāsikārandhramāśritaḥ |
jāyate yoginassarvaṃ kālamṛtyurmahadbhayam || 93 ||

p. 1107)

japate dhyāyate yogī nātra kuryādvicāraṇām |
mantraṃ vā japate yastu pañcākṣaraṃ mahāmatiḥ || 94 ||

śatañca viṃśatiṃ vā'pi pañcāṣṭakaśatāni ca |
japato mantrarājānaḥ oṃ jṛṃsaśśivāya namaḥ || 95 ||

etanmṛtyujayaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvaduḥkhāpahaṃ param |
nānena sadṛśo mantraḥ triṣu lokeṣu vidyate || 96 ||

idaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ mantraṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam |
pūjitaṃ tridaśaihyetaṃ mantrarājaṃ sudurlabham || 97 ||

tasyādau tu trayo devāścaturthamīśvaraṃ param |
pañcamaṃ devadeveśaṃ sadāśivaṃ paraṃ padam || 98 ||

p. 1108)

etatpañcātmakaṃ devaṃ japitaṃ sarvadevataiḥ |
hutañca satataṃ taistu mṛtyujit sa tu te surāḥ || 99 ||

asya mantraprasādena devairmṛtyurjitaḥ purā |
jarārogavinirmuktāḥ paramaiśvaryasamanvitāḥ || 100 ||

sarvakāmasamṛddhāstu pāśānugrahakārakāḥ |
nirdvandvā nirahaṃkārāḥ pralaye bhinnadehajāḥ || 101 ||

na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ gatāstu paramāṃ gatim |
etanmantravaraṃ prāpyātmānaṃ paṭhate sadā || 102 ||

tridhā caiva bahudhā caiva paśujñānābhimāninaḥ |
ātmānaṃ prākṛtaṃ tatvaṃ antarātmā tu pauruṣam || 103 ||

p. 1109)

paramātmā bhavedviṣṇuḥ yogī nārāyaṇeśvaraḥ |
ātmā tu karmaṇaḥ kartā'ntarātmā tu kārakaḥ || 104 ||

paramātmetvudāsīnaṃ dhṛvaṃ te guṇayoginaḥ |
aṇimādiguṇairyuktaḥ paramātmā maheśvaraḥ || 105 ||

dhyāyanti nityayuktā vai sanakādyāstu yoginaḥ |
mokṣaṃ jānanti yogīnāṃ vyākhyātaṃ tava śobhane || 106 ||

vedavādinamapyevaṃ sakalasyamupāsinaḥ |
oṅkāravākyarūpeṇa brahmavidyāvadanti hi || 107 ||

brahmaviṣṇustathā rudraḥ trayo devāssamekataḥ |
satvaṃ rajastamaścaiva guṇatrayamudāhṛtam || 108 ||

p. 1110)

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptañca padatrayamudāhṛtam |
ṛgyajussāmavedāśca trayo vedāssa eva tu || 109 ||

eṣa vai paramoṅkārassarvadevamayaṃ viduḥ |
ānandaṃ paramaṃ brahma sāmyāvasthaṃ tamomayam || 110 ||

agniragnau yadā kṣiptaṃ toye toyamiva sthitam |
mṛtpiṇḍadaṇḍapāṣāṇannissukhaṃ niṣpracetanam || 111 ||

nirvāṇaṃ paramānandamevaṃ vedavido dhṛvam |
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi sādaraṃ śṛṇu suvṛte || 112 ||

upāsyate yathā devaṃ sakalaṃ sarvato mukham |
pradyumnamaniruddhañca tathā saṅkarṣaṇo mahān || 113 ||

p. 1111)

vāsudeveti bhagavān sarvāvāso naro hariḥ |
pradyumnakārako nāma kāmo vai kāmarūpiṇaḥ || 114 ||

aniruddho niruddhā ahaṅkāraḥ prapaṭhyate |
saṅkarṣaṇo bhavedbuddhiḥ prāṇāpānamayī purī || 115 ||

vāsudeveti prakṛtiḥ guṇasāmye tamomayī |
guṇasāmye sthitā yasmāt nirvikalpe nirāmayaḥ || 116 ||

* * * *? kṣetrajñānāṃ tatparaṃ vāsa ucyate |
pradhānakimahorātramuṣitvā prāṇinastathā || 117 ||

bhūya eva vrajantyayaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhāracoditāḥ |
vasante sarvabhūteṣu svabhāvaguṇavāsanā || 118 ||

p. 1112)

vāsanādvāsanā caiva vāsudeveti gīyate |
nāsau devadeveti paṭhyate pāñcarātrake || 119 ||

akārokārabhedena makāraṃ mārarūpiṇam |
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptākhyaiḥ satvarājasatāmasaiḥ || 120 ||

rūpairetaissamākhyātā vedayogamanīṣiṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ yasturyanāmānaṃ tasyādhaḥ prakṛtisthitā || 121 ||

sāṃkhyānāṃ puruṣasyaiva prāgevoktaṃ mayānaghe |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi || 122 ||

vaimalākārakāścaiva tathā pāśupatā ca ye |
