vaimalānāṃ dhruvaṃ devaṃ kārakāṇāṃ tatopari || 123 ||

p. 1113)

granthidhruvaṃ māyīnāṃ śivasṛṣṭeravasthitā |
teṣāṃ tatparamaṃ sthānaṃ dīkṣādhvānaviśodhitam || 124 ||

īśvaraṃ pāśupatyānāṃ sthānañcaivamudāhṛtam |
caryādhvānaviśuddhātmā gacchate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 125 ||

asti nāsti tathāstīti arhantā pudgaleti ca |
jīvākhye niyataṃ devamupāsyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 126 ||

eṣa devi samākhyātaṃ sākyāccārahataissaha |
nyāyavaiśeṣikāścaiva naiśreyapadavādinaḥ || 127 ||

purāṇapuruṣetyevamitihāseṣu gīyate |
purāṇe kavirityevaṃ puruṣaṃ parameśvaram || 128 ||

p. 1114)

devaṃ viśvapatiṃ śuddhamīśvaraṃ viśvatomukham |
ekadhā bahudhā caiva tathā śatasahasradhā || 129 ||

gīyate ye'titatvajñā devadevaṃ maheśvaram |
bahubhirnāmabhedaistu ṛṣibhiḥ parigīyate || 130 ||

jīvaṃ kecidvadantyevaṃ puruṣeti tathā pare |
prāṇinaḥ kecidityevaṃ pudgalaṃ paśureva hi || 131 ||

sūkṣmaṃ sarvagatetyevamajamanye manīṣiṇaḥ |
ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmā tathā pare || 132 ||

* * * * * * * ? namananye dhṛtikīrtitāḥ |
ataḥ paratareṇaiva pātālāni yathākramam || 133 ||

p. 1115)

kathayāmi samāsena tannibodha varānane |
ābhāsatālasutālañca śrītālañcagabhastikam || 134 ||

śiloccayaṃ samākhyātaṃ pātālaṃ hemasaṃjñakam |
eteṣāmupariṣṭāttu bhuvanāssapta kīrtitāḥ || 135 ||

bhūlokaḥ prathamaḥ proktaḥ yasmin saṃskāramiṣyate |
caturdaśavidhaścaiva yasmin saṃsāramaṇḍalam || 136 ||

uddhareta varārohe tathaiva kathayāmi te |
brahmāṇamāditaḥ kṛtvā paiśācāntantu śobhane || 137 ||

paśustu prathamaḥ prokto mṛgaśca tadanantaram |
pakṣiṇaśca tathā proktā tathā caiva sarīsṛpaḥ || 138 ||

p. 1116)

sthāvaraṃ pañcamaṃ proktaṃ mānuṣyañca tathā punaḥ |
caturdaśavidhaṃ devi jñeyaṃ saṃsāramaṇḍalam || 139 ||

ebhyassaṃsāratohyātmā saṃsārī tena sa smṛtaḥ |
mānuṣyeṣu punaḥ paścāt jātiruddharaṇaṃ smṛtaḥ || 140 ||

antyajaḥ prathamaḥ proktaḥ śūdratvaṃ tadanantaram |
vaiśyakṣatraṃ tathā brahmajātiruddharaṇaṃ kuru || 141 ||

brahmatve tu tathā yojya kuryāt saṃskārakarmaṇi |
garbhādhānāditaḥ kṛtvā antyeṣṭintu paścimam || 142 ||

pūrvameva mayā proktaṃ bhūloke caiva kārayet |
kratubhiśśuddhadehe tu bhūloke tu niyojayet || 143 ||

p. 1117)

bhūloko'tha bhuvarloko svarloko'tha mahattathā |
janastapastathā satyaṃ krameṇa punaruddharet || 144 ||

lokaiśśuddhaistathā devi brahmaviṣṇvāsane tathā |
rudrāsanaṃ tathā proktaṃ brahmāṇḍābhyantare tathā || 145 ||

nāgāśca garuḍāścaiva surāḥ kiṃ puruṣāstathā |
agnikumārakāścaiva mayaścaiva balistathā || 146 ||

saptaite kathitā devi pātāleṣu vyavasthitāḥ |
balohyatibalaścaiva balavān balavikramaḥ || 147 ||

balaśca balanantaśca balograścaiva kīrtitāḥ |
etaissapta samākhyātāḥ bhuvanīśā mahābalāḥ || 148 ||

p. 1118)

etaiśśuddhairime śuddhāḥ pātālāssapta eva tu |
śarvo rudrastathā bhīmo bhava ugrastathaiva ca || 149 ||

mahādevastathaiśāno rudralokādhipāstvime |
kāpālīśopyajo buddho vajradehapramardanaḥ || 150 ||

vibhūtikhyayaḥ śāstā pinākī tridaśādhipaḥ |
daśarudrāssamākhyātā pūrvāyāḥ diśi saṃsthitāḥ || 151 ||

agnirudro hutāśī ca piṅgalo khādako haraḥ |
jvalano dahano babhruḥ bhasmāntakaḥ kṣayāntakaḥ || 152 ||

āgneyyāṃ diśi rudrāstu kathitāstava śobhane |
yo dhūmraharo dhātā vidhātā karta eva ca || 153 ||

p. 1119)

kālarudraśca vikhyāto dharmo dharmapatistathā |
saṃyoktā ca viyoktā ca ityete kathitā mayā || 154 ||

yāmyāyāṃ daśarudrāstu kathitāhyanupūrvaśaḥ |
naiṛto māraṇo hantā krūradṛṣṭirbhayānakaḥ || 155 ||

ūrdhvakeśo virūpākṣo dhūmralohitadṛṣṭavān |
naiṛte daśarudrāstu kathitāhyanupūrvaśaḥ || 156 ||

balohyati balaścaiva pāśahasto mahābalaḥ |
śveto'tha jayabhadraśca dīrghabāhurjalāntakaḥ || 157 ||

baḍavāmukho bhīṣaṇeśo varuṇarājñā supūjitāḥ |
vāruṇyāṃ daśarudrāstu kathitā hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 158 ||

p. 1120)

śīghro laghurvāyuvegaḥ sūkṣmastīkṣṇaḥ kṣayāntakaḥ |
pañcāntakaḥ pañcaśikhaḥ kapardī meghavāhanaḥ || 159 ||

vāyavyāṃ daśarudrāstu kathitāhyanupūrvaśaḥ |
nidhīśo rūpavāndhanyaḥ saumyadeho jaṭādharaḥ || 160 ||

lakṣmīdhṛk ratnadhṛk śrīdhṛk prasādaśca prakāśanaḥ |
uttarāṃ diśamāśritya daśarudrā mahābalāḥ || 161 ||

vidyādhipo'tha sarvajño jñānabhuk vedapāragaḥ |
sureśvaraśca sarveśo bhūtapālo balipriyaḥ || 162 ||

sarvavidyā vidhātā ca sukhaduḥkhakarastathā |
īśānyāṃ daśarudrāstu kathitā hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 163 ||

p. 1121)

vṛṣo vṛṣadharo'nanto krodhano marutāśanaḥ |
ugraseno'dhivīraśca phaṇīndro vajradaṃṣṭravān || 164 ||

adhastāddaśarudrāstu kathitāhyanupūrvaśaḥ |
śaṃbhurvibhurguṇādhyakṣaḥ stryakṣastridaśavanditaḥ || 165 ||

saṃvāhaśca vivāhaśca nabholipsurvicakṣaṇaḥ |
ūrdhvāṃ diśaṃ samāśritya daśarudrā mahābalāḥ || 166 ||

bhūrloke tu yadā prāpya kuryāt saṃskārakarmaṇi |
sthāvaraṃ prathamaṃ śodhyaṃ tataśśedhyaṃ sarīsṛpam || 167 ||

pakṣiṇaśca mṛgāścaiva paśavaścetyanukramāt |
devayonirpunaśśodhya evameva vipaścitaiḥ || 168 ||

p. 1122)

saṃsāroddharaṇaṃ kṛtvā jātiruddharaṇaṃ kuru |
antyajaḥ prathamaṃ proktaśśūdraśca tadanantaram || 169 ||

vaiśyakṣatrakrameṇaiva brahmāntamuddharet kramāt |
sadyādivaktraparyantaiśśodhayeta yathākramam || 170 ||

antyajasthamīśamantreṇa gāyatryā devayonayaḥ |
dhyeyañca vividhaṃ yattadvidyāṅgaiśśodhayet kramāt || 171 ||

brahmatve yojayitvā tu kuryāt saṃskārakarmaṇi |
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantaṃ jātakarmaṇi || 172 ||

nāmaniṣkramaṇañcaivānnaprāśanameva ca |
cūḍopanayanañcaiva vṛtabandhaṃ tathaiva ca || 173 ||

p. 1123)

mekhalādantakāṣṭhañca ajinañca triyāyuṣam |
**? magnirupāsañca vratāni punareva tu || 174 ||

bhūteśaṃ pāśupatyañca gaṇañcaiva tṛtīyakam |
gaṇeśvaraṃ caturthantu uttamañcādhisākakam || 175 ||

saptaṃ * * * * * *? apūrveṇa prakīrtitāḥ |
aiṣṭikaṃ dārthakañcaiva bhautikaṃ saumikaṃ tathā || 176 ||

devavratāssamākhyātāścatvārohyanupūrvaśaḥ |
godānantu tataḥ kṛtvā patnīsaṃyojanaṃ tathā || 177 ||

aṣṭakāparvaṇi śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇyāśrayaṇī tathā |
caitrī cāśvayujī caiva pākayajñāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 178 ||

p. 1124)

ādheyañcāgnihotrañca darśañcaiva tataḥ punaḥ |
paurṇamāsīti vijñeyā sautrāmaṇī tataḥ param || 179 ||

haviryajñā smṛtā hyete jñātavyāstu yathākramam |
agniṣṭomotyagniṣṭomamuthyayajñaktṛtīyakaḥ || 180 ||

caturthī ṣoḍaśī jñeyā vājapeyo'tha pañcamaḥ |
trirātraṃ ṣaṣṭamityuktaṃ aptoryāmañca saptamaḥ || 181 ||

somasaṃsthāssamākhyātāḥ saptacaiva krameṇa tu |
hiraṇyapādaḥ prathamo hiraṇyaguhyamataḥ param || 182 ||

hiraṇyameḍhraṃ tṛtīyantu hiraṇyapāṇiścaturthakam |
hiraṇyagarbhastathā proktā pañcamaḥ parameśvari || 183 ||

p. 1125)

hiraṇyaśrotraṃ tathā devi ṣaṣṭhametat prakīrtitam |
hiraṇyatvak samākhyātaṃ hiraṇyākṣaṃ tathā'ṣṭamam || 184 ||

hiraṇyajihvo navamo daśamo'tha hiraṇyadhṛk |
āsahasrasya yajñasya daśayajñāḥ paraḥ smṛtāḥ || 185 ||

ekaistu parivārāstu śatena parivāritāḥ |
daśayajñopariṣṭāttu aśvamedhantu homayet || 186 ||

evaṃ gṛhastho bhavati vānaprasthaṃ tathā punaḥ |
pārivrājyaṃ tato yojya antyeṣṭintu tato hunet || 187 ||

antyeṣṭintu tato hutvā ūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā tu yojayet |
puryaṣṭakasamāyuktaṃ paścāttatveṣu yojayet || 188 ||

p. 1126)

jalānalānilavyoma ahaṅkāraṃ tathaiva ca |
buddhirguṇastathā'vyaktaṃ kramaśaḥ kathayāmite || 189 ||

amareśaṃ prabhāsañca naimiṣaṃ puṣkalaṃ tathā |
aṣaḍiñcaiva daṇḍiñca bhārabhūtiñca lākulam || 190 ||

guhyāṣṭakamidaṃ proktaṃ śivena paramātmanā |
hariścandraśśrīśailaṃ jalpeśvaramataḥ param || 191 ||

āmrātikeśaṃ mahākālaṃ tathā madhyamakeśvaram |
kedāraṃ bhairavañca atiguhyāṣṭakaṃ smṛtam || 192 ||

gayāñcaiva kurukṣetraṃ khalaṃ nākhalaṃ tathā |
vimalañcāṭṭahāsañca mahendraṃ bhīmamaṣṭamam || 193 ||

p. 1127)

guhyādguhyataraṃ hyetat kathitantu mayā tava |
vastrāpadaṃ rudrakoṭimavimuktaṃ mahālayam || 194 ||

gokarṇaṃ bhadrakarṇañca svarṇākṣaṃ sthāṇumaṣṭakam |
pavitrāṣṭakametaddhi kathitaṃ hyanupūrvaśaḥ || 195 ||

phaṭalañca dviraṇḍañca mākoṭaṃ maṇḍaleśvaram |
kālañjaravanañcaiva śaṅkukarṇaṃ sthaleśvaram || 196 ||

sthūleśvaraṃ tathā proktaṃ sarveṣāmupari sthitam |
sthānāṣṭakamidaṃ jñeyaṃ ahaṅkāre vyavasthitam || 197 ||

paiśācaṃ rākṣasaṃ yākṣaṃ gāndharvañcaindrameva ca |
saumyantvatha prajeśañca brāhmañcaivāṣṭamaṃ smṛtam || 198 ||

p. 1128)

devayonyaṣṭakaṃ hyetat budhyāvaraṇamāśritam |
saṃvarta ekavīraśca kṛtānto jananāśanaḥ || 199 ||

mṛtyuhantā ca raktākṣo mahākrodhaśca durjayaḥ |
ete krodheśvarāḥ proktāḥ anupūrvaṃ yathākramam || 200 ||

tejoṣṭakaṃ punarvakṣye kathyamānaṃ mayā śṛṇu |
lokādhyakṣagaṇādhyakṣaṃ tridaśe tripurāntakam || 201 ||

sarvarūpaśca śāntaśca nimeṣo meṣaṇastathā |
tejoṣṭakaṃ samākhyātaṃ anupūrveṇa te mayā || 202 ||

tejoṣṭakopariṣṭāttu sthitaṃ yogāṣṭakaṃ punaḥ |
akṛtañca kṛtañcaiva bhairavaṃ brāhmameva ca || 203 ||

p. 1129)

vaiṣṇavantvatha kaumārabhaumaṃ śraikaṇṭyameva ca |
yoginaḥ kathitā hyete yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 204 ||

p. 1132)

dvitīyā paṅktirākhyātā anupūrvaṃ yathākramam |

p. 1133)

karmendriyaṃ tataḥ pāyu budhya jihvendriya smṛtaḥ |

tejovāraṇametattu śodhanīyaṃ yathākramam |

p. 1134)

ākāśe śodhanīyāstu anupūrveṇa mantravit |

pṛthivyādiṣu tatveṣu ekaikeṣu yathākramam |

evaṃ hutvā yathānyāyaṃ hunedyogāṣṭakaṃ punaḥ |

p. 1135)

aṃbhā ca salilā caiva tuṣāvṛṣṭistathaiva ca |

sumārī uttamāṃbhā catuṣṭayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |

tārā caiva sutārā ca tarayantī tathaiva ca |
pramodā pramuditā caiva modamānā tathaiva ca || 236 ||

ramyakā ca samākhyātā sadā pramuditā ca yā |

prākāmyamīśitatvañca vaśitvañca tathaiva hi || 238 ||

p. 1136)

sarvakāmāvasāyitvamaiśvaryaṃ kathitaṃ mayā |

anugrahārthaṃ lokānāṃ saṃsthitaṃ sarvadehiṣu |

p. 1137)

p. 1139)

vāmā jyeṣṭhā ca raudrī ca adhikārātmikā trayaḥ |
gārhapatyo dakṣiṇāgnirāhavanīyaṃ tṛtīyakam || 254 ||

japatassiddhimāyāti kālamṛtyujayo bhavet |

p. 1140)

etaddevi samākhyātaṃ kālamṛtyujayeṣiṇaḥ |
kathitaṃ sarahasyantu kālacakraṃ samāsataḥ || 259 ||

satataṃ japamānastu na mṛtyuvaśago bhavet |

acireṇaiva kālena si mṛtyuṃ vijayiṣyati |

p. 1141)

anantaṃ kālarudrañca dik śrotraṃ bhuvanaṃ tathā |

p. 1142)

gulake saṃsthitā hyete veditavyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 47 ||

tṛtīyantu punarvakṣye nirañjanamataḥ param |
icchāśakti tatordhve tu ūrdhvaṃ prītyādayasmṛtāḥ || 48 ||

prīti pramuditāścaiva praṇītassukhaduḥkhinaḥ |
kathitaṃ te samāsena aṣṭau te bhuvaneśvarāḥ || 49 ||

tatastu samayaḥ kṣudraḥ prabhavādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
parato'nyaghano nāma rudroṅkāramataḥ param || 50 ||

* ? vasthitaḥ |
parivāryo'ṣṭabhedena madhye nādastu saṃsthitaḥ || 51 ||

p. 1151)

karṇikāsthasthitastatra catasro yogabhaktayaḥ |
nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyāśāntistathaiva ca || 52 ||

upariṣṭātsthitaṃ nādaṃ nirālaṃbo tatopari |
kasmin * * *? ddha jñeyaṃ sarvaṃ vyāpya vyavasthitam || 63 ||

ataḥ suṣumnā vijñeyā sarvādhvānasamāptaye |
tato brahmabile līnā padmasūtranibhā sthitā || 54 ||

dhyātavyo yogibhirnityaṃ tuṣārakaṇa dhūsaraḥ |
ato * * * * * * ? nirālaṃbo tatopari || 55 ||

tasminyuktassamuccena na punaḥ paśutāṃ vrajet |
tasmin yukto yadāhyātmā tattulyasamatāṃ vrajet || 56 ||

p. 1152)

prakriyāsūtrametattu kathitantu yathākramam || 56 1/2 ||

iti niśvāsakārikāyāṃ dīkṣottare prakaraṇe ekonaviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ |

|| samāpto'yaṃ saṃhitā iti ||

idaṃ pañcasūtrojvalaṃ samudāyena dvādaśasāhasrikaṃ samāptaṃ 
niśvāsakārikākhyaṃ tantram ||

oṃ namo namaḥ | oṃ huṃ namaḥ | oṃ haṃ sa | oṃ paramaśivena likhitam 
niśvāsakārikākhyaṃ tantram | oṃ hariḥ oṃ | oṃ śiva śiva śiva |
